# Mushoku Tensei: Jobless Reincarnation Lorebook

A comprehensive lorebook compiled from Mushoku Tensei light novels (Volumes 1–26), canon side stories (Old Dragon's Tale, Subjugation of the King Dragon King, Redundancy, Jobless Oblige), and author Q&As. Featuring exhaustive character profiles with synchronized age milestones, in-world K-calendar chronologies, anatomical evolutions, magic systems, world geography, organizations, artifacts, and historical events.

---

# Characters

---

# Rudeus Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Rudeus Greyrat, Rudeus, Quagmire, Dead End, Dragon God's Right Hand, Magic King, Magician King, K407, K481 -->

SHORT_DESC: {Reincarnated Japanese NEET [Birth: K407 - Death: K481] possessing Laplace Factor, god-class mana, and voiceless casting; famed as 'Quagmire', 'Dragon God's Right Hand', and #7 of Seven Great Powers (K430); master of Saint-to-Emperor elemental magic, wielder of Demon Eyes of Foresight and Clairvoyance, and builder of Magic Armor series | [Personality & Quirks] Plagued by chronic imposter syndrome, lecherous impulses, and immense trauma from his past life as an overweight shut-in; deeply devoted, protective, and humble patriarch who worships Roxy's panty as a divine holy relic; fiercely loyal to his three wives (Sylphiette, Roxy, Eris) and his six children; harbors a paralyzing phobia of failing or losing his family; speaks in polite, formal keigo even to enemies; enjoys crafting intricate miniature magic figurines.}

HISTORY: {[Buena Village Childhood: Age 0-7 / K407-K414] Magic prodigy under Roxy, befriended Sylphiette; [Roa Tutoring: Age 7-10 / K414-K417] Tutored Eris Boreas Greyrat; [Demon Continent Odyssey: Age 10-13 / K417-K420] Teleported in Metastasis, traversed wilderness in Dead End with Ruijerd and Eris; [Northern Lands Adventurer: Age 13-15 / K420-K422] Solo adventurer 'Quagmire'; [Ranoa Magic Academy: Age 15-17 / K422-K424] Reunited with and married Sylphiette, enrolled special student; [Begaritt Labyrinth Campaign: Age 17 / K424] Rescued Zenith, lost Paul, married Roxy; [Asuran Royal Succession: Age 17-18 / K424-K425] Crowned Queen Ariel, married Eris; [Biheiril Kingdom Campaign: Age 19-20 / K426-K427] Crushed Hitogami's forces alongside Orsted; [Peaceful Patriarch & Passing: Age 21-74 / K428-K481] Expanded Orsted Corporation, passed away surrounded by family.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K407-K414] Plump toddler growing into active boy, 145cm small-framed; [Adolescent Tutor: Age 7-10 / K414-K417] Agile child frame from Paul's morning sword drills; # Sylphiette Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Sylphiette Greyrat, Sylphiette, Sylphie, Sylphy, Fitz, Silent Fitz -->

SHORT_DESC: {Quarter-elf silent magician [Birth: K407], first wife of Rudeus Greyrat, and former bodyguard 'Silent Fitz' to Princess Ariel; gentle, deeply devoted, and exceptionally perceptive; master of voiceless wind, water, and healing magic; suffered trauma from mana exhaustion following the Metastasis Event, turning her hair from emerald green to pure snow white; disguised as a male royal guardian under the alias Fitz before reuniting with and curing Rudeus in Ranoa; serves as the warm, supportive matriarch of the Greyrat household, welcoming Roxy and Eris as sister-wives with open arms | [Personality & Quirks] Gentle, sweet, patient, and self-effacing, yet harboring immense inner strength and quiet household authority; fiercely devoted to Rudeus, willing to accept anything for his happiness; acts as the peaceful mediator between volatile Eris and reserved Roxy; enjoys cooking, baking sweet honey tarts, and managing household finances; blushes furiously when teased about her Fitz days; secretly possesses a passionate, possessive romantic drive behind closed doors.}

HISTORY: {[Buena Village Childhood: Age 4-10 / K411-K417] Rescued by Rudeus from village bullies, taught voiceless magic; [Metastasis & Asuran Palace: Age 10-15 / K417-K422] Teleported into Asura royal palace, rescued Princess Ariel, became 'Silent Fitz'; [Ranoa Exile & Reunion: Age 15-16 / K422-K423] Accompanied Ariel to Ranoa, reunited with Rudeus, cured his condition and married him; [Asuran Royal Succession: Age 18 / K425] Accompanied Ariel and Rudeus to Asura, secured Ariel's throne; [Household Matriarch: Age 19+ / K426+] Raised Lucy, Sieghart, and managed the Greyrat estate; [Oldeus Alternate Fate: Age 16-17 / Alternate K423-K424] Abandoned broken Rudeus after Roxy's death, executed in failed Asuran coup.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 4-10 / K411-K417] Petite, delicate quarter-elf child frame; [Silent Fitz Era: Age 10-15 / K417-K422] Adolescent growth, slender and androgynous, easily disguised as an elven boy; [Adult Matriarch: Age 16+ / K423+] 162cm / 5'4", 48kg, slender, graceful elven silhouette that ages at one-third human rate; [Late Timeline: Age 70+ / K477+] Retains radiant appearance of a woman in her early 30s | Height & Weight: [Childhood: Age 7 / K414] 115cm, 20kg | [Silent Fitz: Age 12 / K419] 142cm, 34kg | [Adult: Age 16+ / K423+] 162cm / 5'4", 48kg | Breasts: [Childhood: Age 4-10 / K411-K417] Prepubescent child, flat | [Silent Fitz: Age 10-15 / K417-K422] Flat, androgynous chest wrapped in tight binders | [Adult Wife: Age 16-20 / K423-K427] Petite, elegant, firm A-cup | [Motherhood: Age 21+ / K428+] Modest, soft B-cup during nursing periods | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood / Silent Fitz: Age 4-15 / K411-K422] 4.2", Tightness: Pristine virgin | [Adult Consummated: Age 16+ / K423+] 4.8", Tightness: Snug, deeply loving, highly responsive and wet | Skin: [Childhood & Fitz Era: Age 4-15 / K411-K422] Pale, fair porcelain complexion; [Adult Matriarch: Age 16+ / K423+] Luminous, smooth, slow-aging elven skin | Face: [Childhood: Age 4-10 / K411-K417] Cute, androgynous, delicate round cheeks; [Silent Fitz: Age 10-15 / K417-K422] Slender, handsome elven youth face behind sunglasses; [Adult Wife & Mother: Age 16+ / K423+] Breathtaking elven beauty with soft chin, delicate cheekbones, and gentle maternal smile | Hair: [Childhood: Age 4-10 / K411-K417] Short unruly emerald-green hair; [Post-Metastasis onward: Age 10+ / K417+] Pure snow-white hair from mana shock, growing into silky chin-length bob with green highlights in late life | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 4+ / K411+] Moist, expressive ruby-red eyes inherited from Elinalise, softening into affectionate maternal warmth | Ears: [Childhood to Adult: Age 4+ / K411+] Long, slender pointed elven ears | Distinguishing Traits: Pointed elven ears; snow-white hair; voiceless wind/water magic; sunglasses from Fitz era | Outfits by Timeline: [Buena Childhood: Age 4-10 / K411-K417] Boyish cotton tunic and shorts, leather moccasins; [Silent Fitz: Age 10-15 / K417-K422] White-and-gold Asuran royal guardian uniform, hooded cloak, tinted sunglasses, white gloves, short wand; [Ranoa Matriarch: Age 16+ / K423+] Soft pastel linen blouses, floral aprons, elegant green-and-white wool skirts, silver wedding ring}

# Roxy Migurdia
<!-- Keys: Roxy Migurdia, Roxy, Shirokaze Roxy -->

SHORT_DESC: {Migurd demon Water King magician [Birth: K373], second wife of Rudeus Greyrat, and professor at Ranoa Magic Academy; revered by Rudeus as his supreme goddess and divine teacher; possesses a petite, youthful Migurd body that barely ages over two centuries; master of Water King-class spells and silent incantations; taught young Rudeus magic in Buena before traveling the world; trapped in the Begaritt Teleport Labyrinth for a month before being heroically rescued by Rudeus; married Rudeus in Sharia, giving birth to Lara and Lily; provides wise academic counsel, tactical magical support, and steady guidance to the family | [Personality & Quirks] Intellectual, earnest, modest, and dignified, with an adorable, slightly clumsy streak in daily household chores; self-conscious about her petite, childlike stature and small bust; deeply loves and respects Rudeus, finding immense comfort in his devotion; gets flustered and pouty when treated like a child; loves reading historical grimoires, drinking sweet fruit wines, and tutoring students; takes great pride in her academic research at Ranoa Magic Academy.}

HISTORY: {[Migurd Village Departure: Age 14-30 / K387-K403] Left the Demon Continent due to telepathic muteness; learned Water magic; [Buena Tutoring: Age 37-41 / K410-K414] Tutored young Rudeus in Buena Village, awarding him Water Saint rank; [Shirone & Teleport Search: Age 44-50 / K417-K423] Searched for Fittoa refugees, served as Shirone court tutor; [Begaritt Labyrinth Crisis: Age 51 / K424] Trapped in the Teleport Labyrinth, rescued by Rudeus, married him in Sharia; [Biheiril War & Academy Dean: Age 54-150+ / K427-K523+] Mobilized magic artillery in Biheiril, served as Dean of Ranoa Magic Academy; [Oldeus Timeline Divergence: Age 50 / Alternate K423] Tragically died from Magic Stone Disease while pregnant after Hitogami's rat trap.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Lifelong Migurd Constant: Age 14-150+ / K387-K523+] 145cm / 4'9", 38kg; childlike, petite frame that remains virtually unchanging from adolescence through two centuries of lifespan; slender limbs, light steps, remarkably youthful appearance throughout the entire story | Height & Weight: [Adolescent to Centenarian: Age 14-150+] 145cm / 4'9", 38kg | Breasts: [Lifelong Migurd Constant: Age 14-150+] Petite, firm, soft AA-cup (slight swelling to modest A-cup during pregnancy and nursing) | Vaginal Depth: [Lifelong Migurd Constant: Age 14-150+] 4.4", Tightness: Exceptionally tight, snug, gentle, deeply sensitive and responsive | Skin: [Adolescent to Centenarian: Age 14-150+ / K387-K523+] Smooth, porcelain pale-blue-tinted demon skin, perpetually soft | Face: [Adolescent to Centenarian: Age 14-150+ / K387-K523+] Endearing, mature resting affect belying her petite childlike frame; doll-like features with subtle pout | Hair: [Tutor & Adventurer: Age 37-50 / K410-K423] Long water-blue hair in twin low braids; [Motherhood & Academy Dean: Age 51+ / K424+] Sleek water-blue hair occasionally pinned up or worn loose under academic caps | Eyes: [Adolescent to Centenarian: Age 14-150+] Large, luminous aquamarine-blue eyes filled with intellectual curiosity and gentle warmth | Distinguishing Traits: Petite Migurd stature; water-blue twin braids; wide-brimmed witch hat; divine panties revered as holy relic | Outfits by Timeline: [Buena Tutoring: Age 37-41 / K410-K414] White-and-gray shirt, black skirt, wide-brimmed indigo witch hat, midnight-blue robe, Water King wand; [Labyrinth Adventuring: Age 44-51 / K417-K424] Traveling cloak, reinforced leather boots, mystic robe, water staff; [Ranoa Professor & Matron: Age 51-70 / K424-K443] Ranoa faculty robe, dark pleated skirt, tailored blouses, silver wedding band; [Late Timeline Headmistress: Age 70-150+ / K443-K523+] Elaborate Dean's ceremonial robes of deep sapphire and gold}

# Eris Boreas Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Eris Boreas Greyrat, Eris, Mad Dog, Mad Sword King -->

SHORT_DESC: {Sword King of the Sword God Style [Birth: K405 - Death: K477], third wife and chief combat vanguard of Rudeus Greyrat; a ferocious, passionate noblewoman possessing unrivaled martial ferocity; reared in Roa as the violent heiress of House Boreas before being civilized by young Rudeus; survived the brutal Demon Continent crossing with Dead End; after feeling inadequate following their defeat by Orsted, left Rudeus to train at the Holy Land of Swords, attaining Sword King status under Gal Farion; returned to save Rudeus from Orsted in Sharia and married him; serves as the indomitable physical shield and vanguard mother of the Greyrat clan | [Personality & Quirks] Wild, fiery, aggressive, and fiercely protective of Rudeus; exhibits canine-like body language (pouncing, biting Rudeus's neck, baring fangs in excitement); acts as the dominant physical force in the bedroom, frequently demanding passionate marital intimacy; possesses absolute confidence in combat, cutting through enemies with blistering speed; loves eating massive cuts of roasted meat, outdoor hunting, and sparring with Ghislaine and Ruijerd; deeply affectionate mother to Ars and Christina.}

HISTORY: {[Roa Childhood: Age 7-10 / K412-K415] Violent noble prodigy tamed by tutor Rudeus; [Dead End Odyssey: Age 10-13 / K415-K418] Traversed Demon Continent; survived Orsted encounter; consummated first love with Rudeus on her 15th birthday before departing for training; [Holy Land of Swords: Age 13-20 / K418-K425] Trained fanatically under Gal Farion; defeated Nina Falion; attained Sword King rank; [Rescue & Marriage in Sharia: Age 20 / K425] Intercepted Orsted to save Rudeus; married Rudeus as his third wife; [Biheiril War Vanguard: Age 22 / K427] Slew Gal Farion and North God Kalman III's forces; [Vanguard Matron & Passing: Age 23-72 / K428-K477] Trained children in swordsmanship; died peacefully at age 72; [Oldeus Timeline Divergence: Age 18-35 / Alternate K423-K440] Sacrificed her life shielding broken Rudeus from Demon Lord Atoferatofe.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 7-10 / K412-K415] Small, fiery athletic noble girl; [Dead End Vanguard: Age 10-13 / K415-K418] Leaner traveler frame, rapidly developing vanguard muscles; [Holy Land Training: Age 13-20 / K418-K425] Sculpted powerful amazon physique, rock-hard abs, explosive sinew; [Peak Adult Vanguard: Age 20-40 / K425-K445] 170cm / 5'7", 63kg, athletic, voluptuous, rock-hard core, powerful thighs, the most buxom of Rudeus's wives; [Elderly Warrior Queen: Age 60-72 / K465-K477] Weathered, formidable warrior build until passing at 72 | Height & Weight: [Childhood: Age 9 / K414] 132cm, 28kg | [Dead End: Age 12 / K417] 150cm, 42kg | [Holy Land: Age 15 / K420] 163cm, 54kg | [Peak Adult: Age 20+ / K425+] 170cm / 5'7", 63kg | Breasts: [Childhood: Age 7-10 / K412-K415] Flat, developing | [Dead End: Age 11-13 / K416-K418] Developing B-cup to C-cup | [Holy Land & Adult Wife: Age 14+ / K419+] Voluptuous, heavy, firm E-cup to F-cup with sculpted pectoral muscle support | Vaginal Depth: [Youth Maiden: Age 10-14 / K415-K419] 4.6", Tightness: Extremely tight | [Adult Wife & Mother: Age 15+ / K420+] 5.4", Tightness: Snug, intensely muscular pelvic floor, highly voracious and passionate | Skin: [Childhood: Age 7-10 / K412-K415] Fair noble skin; # Paul Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Paul Greyrat, Paul, Paul Notos Greyrat -->

SHORT_DESC: {Father of Rudeus, Norn, and Aisha, husband of Zenith and Lillia, and former garrison knight of Buena Village [Birth: K388 - Death: K424]; born Paul Notos Greyrat, high-born scion of House Notos Greyrat in Asura; fled home at twelve to escape noble corruption and an abusive father; an exceptionally gifted S-Rank swordsman proficient in dual-wielding across Sword God (lunges), Water God (parries), and North God (dirty tricks, feints, kicking dirt); charismatic, impulsive, and hedonistic founder of 'Fangs of the Black Wolf'; retired to Buena Village upon conceiving Rudeus with noble healer Zenith; a deeply loving yet profoundly flawed father | [Personality & Quirks] Possesses an unruly, hot-blooded temperament with a tendency to strike first in panic and suffer crushing guilt afterward, buying lavish reconciliation gifts; a notorious emotional lightweight with alcohol, breaking down into drunken sobs, hugging companions, and venting his deep insecurities about parenting; exchanged lecherous winks and bawdy jokes with young Rudeus behind Zenith's back, treating his son as an equal peer; harbored an inferiority complex toward toddler Rudeus's unnatural genius; woke Rudeus at dawn for rigorous sparring and jogs; deeply devoted to Zenith despite his Notos libido and his past affair with Lillia; in Milishion, fell into alcoholic despair and self-neglect while running the refugee search group; gave his life in the Teleport Labyrinth, pushing Rudeus clear of the Hydra's lethal strike.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-12: Notos Noble Rebel / K381-K393] Reared in the aristocratic excess of Asura's House Notos; despised court politics and academic studies; fled at age twelve after striking his tutor; [Age 12-22: Fangs of the Black Wolf / K393-K403] Rose to S-Rank fame as a dual-wielding swordsman; conquered brutal dungeons across the Central Continent; had stormy romantic relationships with party members Elinalise and Ghislaine; reformed his reckless bachelor ways upon falling deeply in love with Zenith Latreia; disbanded the party when Zenith became pregnant with Rudeus, settling down in Buena Village; [Age 22-34: Buena Garrison Knight / K403-K415] Served as village knight; intimidated yet fiercely proud of toddler Rudeus's magical genius; had an affair with maid Lillia, which toddler Rudeus cleverly diffused, reconciling the family and integrating newborn Aisha; arranged Rudeus's tutoring post in Roa; [Age 34-39: Fittoa Search Group & Millis Reunion / K415-K420] Separated from his family during the Mana Catastrophe, landing in Millis with toddler Norn; founded the Fittoa Search Group, witnessing horrific refugee tragedies (slavery, massacres, death in volcanic zones) that drove him into severe depression and alcoholism; violently fought Rudeus in a Milis tavern when Rudeus arrived dressed well with Dead End, mistakenly believing Rudeus was living carefree while his family suffered; intervened by Geese Nukadia, leading to a tearful, deeply remorseful father-son reconciliation; [Age 39: Begaritt Labyrinth & The Sacrificial Fall / K420] Reunited with Rudeus, Elinalise, and Talhand in Rapan; led the raid into the Teleport Labyrinth to rescue Zenith; slashed through labyrinth guardians to the lowest chamber; during the climactic duel against the titanic Manatite Hydra, saw a lethal neck strike aimed at Rudeus's blind spot; kicked Rudeus out of the blast radius and took the Hydra's crushing maw directly, being bisected at the waist and killed instantly to ensure his son lived.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adult Veteran: Age 16-41 / K397-K422] above average, intact but bloodshot and drawn | [Adult Veteran: Age 16-41 / K397-K422] Bloodshot, drawn, grown-out beard | Hair: [Adult Veteran: Age 16-41 / K397-K422] Unkempt; [Adult Veteran: Age 16-41 / K397-K422] Graying at temples | Eyes: [Adult Veteran: Age 16-41 / K397-K422] Bloodshot, shadowed with dark bags during the search corps years | Distinguishing Traits: Calloused dual-wielding swordsman hands; scars across forearms; heavy scent of alcohol and pipe tobacco during Northern and Begaritt travels | Outfits by Timeline: [Adult Veteran: Age 16-41 / K397-K422] Double-edged Beloved Sword with triangular crossguard paired with a curved black short sword dual-wielded in the offhand, money and adventurer's card kept in the sword sheath, adventurer's tunic and boots; [Buena Village Era: Age 25-34] Polished steel breastplate over green village knight tunic, casual wear with the sword still slung at the hip, leather riding pants, tall boots; [Metastasis and Millishion Search: Age 34-36 / K415-K417] Weather-stained brown traveling cloak, dented iron pauldrons, frayed leather armor; [Teleport Labyrinth Expedition: Age 37 / K418] Enchanted steel cuirass, dual scabbards for magic rapier and short broadsword, reinforced steel greaves}

# Zenith Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Zenith Greyrat, Zenith -->

SHORT_DESC: {Mother of Rudeus and Norn, stepmother of Aisha, and devoted wife of Paul Greyrat [Birth: K390]; born Zenith Latreia, second daughter of Earl Latreia of the Holy Country of Milis; an Advanced-class Divine and Water magician who fled her aristocratic family's rigid religious expectations to seek freedom as an adventurer; party healer of 'Fangs of the Black Wolf'; exceptionally sweet, cheerful, and fiercely nurturing; retired in Buena Village upon conceiving Rudeus; accepted Lillia and newborn Aisha into the family with extraordinary magnanimity; rescued from the Teleport Labyrinth core into an apparent mute, vegetative state that masked profound Miko telepathic perception; lives peacefully in Sharia surrounded by loving family | [Personality & Quirks] Rebellious tomboy adventurer past, running away with a knapsack and cursing freely before settling down; playfully twisted Paul's ear whenever he ogled women or made lewd remarks; deeply devoted to the Millis faith while ignoring its ascetic hypocrisies, praying before meals with a small holy pendant; post-Begaritt, perceives her waking life as a continuous 'pleasant dream', enjoying the warm sun in the garden with Lillia, cultivating flowers (especially daffodils), and eating apples; receives a tender kiss on the cheek from Norn every morning before school; communicates wordlessly through telepathy with granddaughter Lara; her calm maternal gaze melts Rudeus's anxieties and childhood traumas.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-15: Millis High Nobility / K383-K398] Reared in the devout, patriarchal House Latreia in Millis; rebelled against arranged aristocratic marriages and fled to the Central Continent; [Age 15-21: Fangs of the Black Wolf / K398-K404] Served as chief healer for Paul's S-rank adventurer party, mastering Advanced Healing and Detoxification; reformed Paul's philandering habits with her pure-hearted devotion; married Paul under the Millis Church's strict monogamy vow and retired upon becoming pregnant with Rudeus; [Age 21-33: Buena Village Motherhood / K404-K416] Reared Rudeus and Norn; warmly embraced Lillia and baby Aisha after toddler Rudeus mediated Paul's indiscretion; [Age 33-38: Teleport Labyrinth Crystal / K416-K421] Swept away by the Mana Catastrophe; teleported into the deepest sanctum of the Teleport Labyrinth in Begaritt; encased in a massive magic crystal beneath the lair of the Manatite Hydra in suspended animation for five years; [Age 38+: Rescue, Miko Telepathy & Peaceful Matriarch / K421+] Freed by Rudeus and Paul; emerged unable to chant or speak, leading observers to believe she suffered brain damage; later revealed by Dragon God Orsted and the Millis Telepathic Miko to possess powerful latent Miko telepathy, clearly perceiving her family's innermost thoughts and love; lived a joyful, tranquil life in the Sharia estate, tending the gardens and doting silently on her grandchildren Lara, Lucie, and Ars.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adult Matron: Age 16-34 / K399-K417] Placid, near-expressionless, serene and dreamy | Hair: [Adult Matron: Age 16-34 / K399-K417] Serene, dreamy, unfocused gaze that lights up when communing telepathically with Lara or Leo | Distinguishing Traits: Inward telepathic resonance; completely relaxed physical state requiring gentle assistance for daily routines; peaceful aura | Outfits by Timeline: [Adult Matron: Age 16-34 / K399-K417] White Millis healer robes with green travel mantle, light brown blouse, black ribbon, khaki belted skirt, white halterneck corset exposing the bust, black thigh-high boots, leather wand holster; [Adult Matron: Age 16-34 / K399-K417] Cozy pastel linen house dresses, woolen aprons, blue and white cleric's outfit with boob window and back cutout, soft leather house slippers; [Teleport Labyrinth Crystal Core: Age 34-39 / K417-K422] Translucent crystalline veil encased in mana amber; [Post-Rescue Sharia Residence: Age 40+ / K423+] Comfortable cream and pale blue silk dresses, white nightgown flaunting ample cleavage, knitted woolen shawls, delicate hair ribbons}

# Lillia Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Lillia Greyrat, Lillia, Lilia -->

SHORT_DESC: {Head maid of the Greyrat household, second wife/concubine of Paul, and mother of Aisha Greyrat [Birth: K385]; former elite palace swordswoman of the Asuran Royal Guard and junior disciple of Water God Style alongside Paul; suffered a permanent tendon injury saving Princess Ariel from an assassin, entering Paul and Zenith's service in Buena Village; seduced Paul in emotional vulnerability, conceiving Aisha; saved from exile by 3-year-old Rudeus's impassioned defense, pledging lifelong maternal loyalty and service to him; managed the expanded Sharia estate with supreme administrative mastery | [Personality & Quirks] Maintained an early chilling dread toward infant Rudeus, observing him with cold suspicion when he stared at women's breasts, suspecting a demonic possession until his impassioned defense of her pregnancy won her eternal devotion; stands ramrod straight with hands folded over her apron, maintaining absolute maid decorum even in crisis; retains deadly Water God reflexes, capable of neutralizing intruders with kitchen knives or fireplace pokers despite her leg limp; trained Aisha relentlessly in elite maidcraft and bookkeeping, strictly scolding her whenever Aisha acted too casual; tenderly bathes, combs, and dresses Zenith every single morning, dedicated to preserving Zenith's dignity; secretly keeps a small wooden box of Paul's personal mementos, grieving in silent privacy; master brewer of Asuran aristocratic herbal teas, tonics, and medicinal infusions.}

HISTORY: {[Asuran Royal Guard: Age 18-23 / K402-K407] Served as an elite palace bodyguard in Ars; injured saving Princess Ariel; relocated to Buena Village as Paul and Zenith's maid; [Buena Village & Rudeus's Defense: K407-K414: Age 23-30] Became pregnant with Aisha; defended by toddler Rudeus when Zenith discovered the infidelity; became a beloved second mother to Rudeus, Norn, and Aisha; [Shirone Captivity & Rescue: K417-K419: Age 33-35] Teleported to Shirone during the Catastrophe; held hostage by Seventh Prince Pax; liberated when Rudeus and Zanoba intervened; [Sharia Estate Matriarch: Age 39+ / K423+] Reunited in Sharia; managed household logistics, servant training, and the care of telepathic Zenith until her peaceful old age.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adult Household Maid: Age 18+ / K402+] Permanent slight limp in the right leg from the poisoned dagger, ending her full-strength running and stomping; [Adult Household Maid: Age 18+ / K402+] 5.2", Tightness: Snug | [Adult Household Maid: Age 18+ / K402+] Fitted Asuran royal guardsmaid uniform with chainmail lining and shortsword holster; [Adult Household Maid: Age 18+ / K402+] Black dress with gigot sleeves, white apron, white waistband emphasizing the bust, white stockings, black strapped shoes, black-and-white hairband, high-collared blouse, spectacles; [Adult Household Maid: Age 18+ / K402+] Worn maid dress, locked chamber attire; [Adult Household Maid: Age 18+ / K402+] High-class black wool maid gown with silver trim, master housekeeper keys at belt, spotless apron}

# Norn Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Norn Greyrat, Norn -->

SHORT_DESC: {Second child of Paul and Zenith, younger sister of Rudeus, elder twin of Aisha, and wife of Ruijerd Superdia [Birth: K414]; mother of Luicelia Superdia and renowned canonical author of the monumental biography 'The Great Magician Rudeus'; struggled through childhood with inferiority living in the shadow of prodigy siblings Rudeus and Aisha; raised by Paul in the Fittoa Search Group, forming an intense bond with her father; overcame crippling social anxiety and a shut-in episode at Ranoa Academy to become the beloved Student Council President; married Ruijerd Superdia in Biheiril | [Personality & Quirks] Habitually puffs out her cheeks and pouts whenever teased by Rudeus or Aisha; profoundly hardworking, compensating for her lack of genius talent by studying late into the night, rewriting lecture notes dozens of times; kisses Zenith on the cheek every morning before leaving for school; initially terrified and resentful of Rudeus in Milishion after he fought Paul, screaming and throwing stones at him, but later recognized his tender care; suffered a severe shut-in breakdown in Volume 10 at Ranoa Academy, barricading herself in her dorm until Rudeus cried outside her door sharing his past-life pain; immense popularity as Student Council President, inspiring a devoted campus fan club ('Norn's Knight Guards'); fell deeply in love with Ruijerd, nursing him through the Superd plague in Biheiril with stubborn, unyielding devotion and proposing marriage regardless of race or age; an insightful diarist and chronicler.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-7: Buena Village & Fittoa Catastrophe / K414-K421] Born in Buena Village alongside twin sister Aisha; separated from Zenith and Rudeus during the Mana Catastrophe; teleported to the Holy Country of Milis with Paul, accompanying her father through years of refugee searching and emotional hardship; [Age 7: Millis Reunion Trauma / K421] Witnessed the violent tavern brawl between Paul and Rudeus, developing a lasting fear and resentment of Rudeus as an arrogant bully; [Age 7-10: Journey with Ruijerd / K421-K424] Escorted to Sharia by Ruijerd Superdia; formed an unbreakable bond of trust and deep romantic affection for the noble Superd warrior; [Age 10-15: Ranoa Academy & Shut-In Breakthrough / K424-K429] Enrolled at Ranoa Magic Academy; overwhelmed by public expectations comparing her to Rudeus and Aisha, she locked herself in her dormitory as a clinical shut-in; Rudeus broke through her door and embraced her in tears, sharing his past-life pain and restoring her spirit; rose to become Student Council President, reforming academy discipline and mastering Water God swordsmanship; [Age 15+: Marriage to Ruijerd & Legacy / K429+] Reunited with Ruijerd during the Biheiril Kingdom campaign; passionately proposed and married him despite their multi-century age gap; gave birth to their half-Superd daughter Luicelia; authored the authoritative biographical history of Rudeus Greyrat and the Orsted Corporation.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth to Adult: Age 8-30+ / K422-K444+] Full D-cup, softened from nursing Luicelia Superdia | [Late Timeline: Age 60+ / K474+] Mature D-cup, dignified maternal figure | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K414-K424] Undeveloped child anatomy | [Adolescent Student: Age 11-18 / K425-K432] 4.9", Tightness: Tight | [Adult Marriage to Ruijerd: Age 23+ / K437+] 5.3", Tightness: Snug | [Motherhood & Late Timeline: Age 30+ / K444+] 5.5", Tightness: Accommodating | Skin: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K414-K421] Soft, fair toddler skin; [Academy & Adult: Age 8+ / K422+] Pristine, fair Milis-Greyrat noble complexion Fair | Face: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K414-K421] Cute, round-cheeked, pouty toddler; [Academy Student: Age 10-16 / K424-K430] Serious, determined, pretty face resembling Zenith; [Adult Wife: Age 17+ / K431+] Graceful, mature beauty with warm maternal dignity Beautiful, resembling Zenith; has an ahoge like her mother and brother; soft fair complexion, round gentle cheeks that turn rosy when flustered, remarkably resembles a young Zenith Greyrat | Hair: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K414-K421] Golden-blonde hair in bouncy twintails; [Academy: Age 8-16 / K422-K430] Long blonde hair parted neatly; [Adult / Married to Ruijerd: Age 17+ / K431+] Elegant golden-blonde hair tied with a decorative ribbon Soft, wavy honey-blonde hair inherited from Zenith; [Adolescent: Age 11-16 / K425-K430] Tied in a high, bouncy ponytail; [Adult: Age 17+ / K431+] Styled in an elegant half-up twist or loose waves | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K414+] Large, earnest hazel-brown eyes full of determination and fierce loyalty Green, expressive and sensitive, inherited from Paul; earnest and determined | Distinguishing Traits: Strong resemblance to Zenith; dignified posture; ink calluses on right fingers from years of writing Ruijerd's biography | Outfits by Timeline: [Millis Travel Era: Age 7-10] Child's traveling clothes, warm traveling coat, woolen scarf, leather boots holding Paul's hand; [Ranoa Student and President Era: Age 12-18] Pristine Ranoa Magic Academy uniform (Magic University uniform), navy pleated skirt, red tie, embroidered Student Council President armband; [Adult and Wife Era: Age 20+] Refined scholar's coat over long pleated skirts and casual dresses at the Sharia home, carrying heavy leather-bound manuscripts and fountain pens}

# Aisha Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Aisha Greyrat, Aisha -->

SHORT_DESC: {Daughter of Paul and Lillia Greyrat, younger half-sister of Rudeus, and twin sister of Norn [Birth: K414]; multi-disciplinary genius in administration, strategy, logistics, household management, and social psychology; reared by Lillia to serve Rudeus as his personal maid and primary administrator; in divergent timelines without Rudeus, was destined to become the greatest magical prodigy of her century, inventing human lifespan-extension magic; in canon, directed estate stewardship and communications for Orsted Corporation; entered a controversial romantic relationship with nephew Ars Greyrat, eventually reconciling with family and bearing their son Leroy | [Personality & Quirks] Boundlessly energetic, cunning, and cheerful, masking complex emotional vulnerabilities regarding her identity and purpose; completely comfortable naked in front of Rudeus, casually walking into the bath unclad without hesitation, wriggling between his legs and playfully demanding he wash her back and rinse her hair, totally unbothered by nudity around him; discovered Rudeus's secret downstairs panty altar and playfully used that knowledge to blackmail him into letting her adopt and cultivate a dangerous Treant in the yard; effortless prodigy who masters cooking, sewing, arithmetic, gardening, magic, and stealth on the first attempt, leading to chronic boredom with ordinary tasks; initially patronizing toward Norn's slower pace, but grew fiercely protective of her sister; deeply loves cultivating flowers and tending gardens with Zenith, sharing apples and daffodils; secretly harbored fears of being viewed merely as a servant rather than a true family member until Rudeus affirmed her indispensable place in the home.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-7: Buena & Shirone Captivity / K414-K421] Born to Lillia; trained from toddlerhood in maidcraft, bookkeeping, and stealth; teleported to Shirone with Lillia; deduced Rudeus's identity from secret letters and assisted in their liberation from Prince Pax; [Age 7-15: Sharia Household Steward / K421-K429] Joined the Greyrat residence in Sharia, declining formal academy enrollment to single-handedly direct estate management, servant training, financial ledgers, and agricultural logistics; [Age 15-20+: Orsted Corporation Manager / K429-K434] Administered regional communications, mercenary contracts, and teleportation network relays for Rudeus's global operations; [Oldeus Timeline Devotion: Age 9-30 / Alternate K423-K444] In the dark future of the Diary, when Sylphiette died, Rudeus collapsed into alcoholic ruin, and the entire family moved out, Aisha was the sole family member who returned to his side, nursing him, managing the house, and keeping him alive for years at his lowest point, before being murdered alongside Julie, Ginger, and Zanoba by Milis Temple Knights; [Late Timeline: Elopement & Reconciliation: Age 20-25 / K434-K439] Fled with nephew Ars Greyrat after their forbidden relationship was discovered; reconciled after a tumultuous family crisis, returning to live as a respected matriarch and mother to Leroy Greyrat.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth to Adult: Age 8-30+ / K422-K444+] Full D-cup, softened from nursing | [Epilogue: Late Timeline: Age 30+ / K444+] Full D-cup, mature maternal figure | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K414-K424] Undeveloped child anatomy | [Adolescent Maid: Age 11-15 / K425-K429] 4.8", Tightness: Tight | [Young Adult Steward: Age 16+ / K430+] 5.2", Tightness: Snug | [Motherhood & Epilogue: Age 30+ / K444+] 5.5", Tightness: Accommodating | Skin: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K414-K421] Fair, soft, energetic child skin; [Youth to Adult: Age 8+ / K422+] Flawless, fair, glowing complexion Fair | Face: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K414-K421] Bubbly, angelic, mischievous child face; [Teen Maid: Age 10-16 / K424-K430] Heart-meltingly cute, expressive features with sparkling smile; [Adult Manager: Age 17+ / K431+] Stunning, clever beauty radiating supreme competence Pretty, drawn with little fangs that make her look more adorable, canine tooth visible when smiling; fair youthful complexion, bright expressive smile, delicate features combining Paul's charm and Lillia's sharpness | Hair: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K414-K421] Bouncy auburn hair tied in a playful ponytail; [Maid & Adult: Age 8+ / K422+] Silky chestnut-auburn hair tied with a maid ribbon or flowing loose Rich reddish-brown (maroon) hair inherited from Lillia, parted in the middle, framing the face and tied behind the head; [Youth: Age 11-16 / K425-K430] Styled in neat, perky twin side ponytails; [Adult: Age 17+ / K431+] Styled in twin braids or a neat chignon tucked under a lace cap | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K414+] Bright, hyper-intelligent dark amber eyes gleaming with mischief and devotion to Rudeus Green, the same green eyes as her father and his half-siblings, though she looks more like her mother; sparkling, razor-sharp and brimming with mischievous intelligence | Distinguishing Traits: Cat-like nimbleness; unerring eye contact; energetic, silent footwork | Outfits by Timeline: [Childhood and Shirone Kidnapping Era: Age 4-7] Frilly child-sized maid outfit sewn by Lillia with high-quality materials, small servant dress and apron, hiding behind Lillia; [Sharia Household Maid: Age 10-16 / K424-K430] Impeccable traditional maid dress with snowy white apron, leather utility belt with keys, ledgers, and measuring tape; [Adult Steward and Orsted Corp Manager: Age 17+ / K431+] Refined adult maid uniform echoing Lillia's with hairband and apron retained, high-class executive maid uniform of midnight-blue velvet, silver pocket watch, sleek leather boots; [Epilogue: Post-Marriage to Ars: Age 25+ / K439+] Civilian dress, having hung up the maid uniform for good}

# Lucy Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Lucy Greyrat, Lucy -->

SHORT_DESC: {Eldest child of Rudeus and Sylphiette Greyrat, elder sister of Sieghart, and eldest among all six Greyrat siblings [Birth: K423]; inherits human, elf, and beastman ancestry; gifted with remarkable intellect, self-discipline, and natural wind/healing magic aptitude; reared in Sharia under the loving care of Sylphiette, Roxy, and Eris; graduated from Ranoa Magic Academy at the top of her class; married Clive Grimoire (son of Cliff and Elinalise) and relocated to the Holy Country of Milis, becoming a prominent diplomatic figure | [Personality & Quirks] Earnest, proud, and deeply responsible as the eldest sibling of the Greyrat clan, always striving to set a flawless example for Lara, Ars, Sieg, Lily, and Chris; secretly hungers for Rudeus's approval and headpats, though intimidated by his towering legendary reputation; in toddlerhood, initially cried and hid behind Sylphie when Rudeus returned from Begaritt with a missing arm and gaunt frame, not recognizing him; loved running away naked from the bath while giggling, forcing Rudeus and Eris to chase her down the hall; grabbed Eris's strong hands to climb furniture, playfully pulling Eris's crimson hair while Eris gingerly supported her; developed a fierce childhood rivalry with Clive Grimoire over grades and magic, which blossomed into deep mutual romance; inherited small pointed elf ear tips from Sylphiette that blush when teased.}

HISTORY: {[Infancy & Early Years: Sharia: Age 0-10 / K423-K433] Born in Sharia shortly before the Begaritt expedition; her birth served as Rudeus's primary motivation to survive the labyrinth; reared alongside her siblings in a vibrant multi-wife home; formed an early bond with Clive Grimoire; [Adolescence: Ranoa Academy: Age 10-15 / K433-K438] Enrolled in Ranoa Magic Academy; initially struggled under the immense shadow of Rudeus's fame, striving tirelessly to prove her own worth without relying on parental favoritism; excelled in elemental and healing magic; [Adulthood: Marriage & Milis Settlement: Age 16+ / K439+] Confessed her feelings to childhood companion Clive Grimoire; married Clive and moved to the Holy Land of Milis, acting as a respected bridge between the Greyrat family, the Milis Church, and the Orsted Corporation; [Oldeus Alternate Fate: Age 0-1 / Alternate K423-K424] In the dark future of the Diary, taken as an infant by Sylphiette to Asura; survived the capital massacre and was raised in hiding after Sylphie's public execution.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth to Adult: Age 10-30+ / K433-K453+] University apprentice robes, wooden practice wand; [Late Timeline / Milis Matron: Age 20+ / K443+] High-class Milis scholarly attire, embroidered silver-white robes, modest jewelry, traveling attire for her move to Milis upon marriage to Clive}

# Lara Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Lara Greyrat, Lara -->

SHORT_DESC: {Second child of Rudeus Greyrat and first daughter of Roxy Migurdia [Birth: K427]; the prophesied 'Savior of the World' chosen by Sacred Beast Leo to spearhead the final defeat of the Man-God (Hitogami) in the distant future; born with bright blue hair, Migurd heritage, and innate telepathy, which manifested from birth; notoriously mischievous, deadpan, and lethargic, deliberately concealing her genius beneath an aura of indolence; possesses extraordinary latent divination, summoning, and elemental magic; destined to journey across the world alongside Sacred Beast Leo to unite the heroes who will permanently vanquish Hitogami | [Personality & Quirks] Habitually uses Sacred Beast Leo's massive fluffy back as a living mattress and pillow, sleeping soundly on him for hours; communicates telepathically with Leo to coordinate deadpan household pranks across the estate, such as moving boots, tripping siblings with subtle earth magic, and hiding slippers; sits in the sunny garden with grandmother Zenith for hours, engaging in silent telepathic conversations when nobody else can understand Zenith; shares her snacks and dried meat directly with Leo from her hand; feigns complete disinterest and sleep during classes, yet absorbs high-tier magical theory effortlessly; deeply attached to her family despite her detached, bored expression; destined in her late teens to don Rudeus's robe and staff, riding Leo into the world as the prophesied hero.}

HISTORY: {[Birth & Sacred Beast Selection: Sharia: Age 0-5 / K424-K429] Conceived prior to the Biheiril campaign; heralded before birth by Sacred Beast Leo, who prostrated before pregnant Roxy and designated the unborn child as his master and the destined savior of humanity; born with the Migurd telepathic mana factor, enabling silent dialogue with Leo from infancy; [Childhood: Estate Prankster: Age 6-14 / K430-K438] Grew up in Sharia alongside her siblings; renowned for silent mischief, stealing panties, altering furniture with earth magic, and outsmarting adult family members while maintaining a placid, unreadable expression; [Prophecy & Departure: The Savior's Journey: Age 15+ / K439+] Tutored by Roxy and Rudeus in advanced magic; possessed unique divination abilities that revealed glimpses of future battles; departed Sharia in early adulthood alongside Sacred Beast Leo, taking up the mantle of world savior to prepare the global alliances for the final war against Hitogami.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K424-K434] Petite, quiet half-Migurd child frame, frequently carried by Sacred Beast Leo; [Adolescent to Adult: Age 15+ / K439+] 148cm / 4'10", 40kg, petite, doll-like demon silhouette closely mirroring Roxy, growing into the appearance of a 13-14 year old human girl due to her Migurd heritage (Rudeus confuses her with Roxy from behind); youthful demon longevity; [Late Timeline: Age 50+ / K474+] Virtually unchanged appearance, looks like a teenage demon girl destined to lead the future war against Hitogami | Height & Weight: [Age 10 / K434] 130cm, 27kg; [Adult: Age 20+ / K444+] 148cm, 40kg | Breasts: [Childhood & Lifelong Migurd Stature: Age 0-50+ / K424-K474] Petite, flat-chested to modest A-cup, unchanging demon silhouette | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood: Age 0-14 / K424-K438] Undeveloped demon child anatomy | [Adult Traveler: Age 15+ / K439+] 4.8", Tightness: Snug | Skin: [Childhood to Wandering Savior: Age 0-50+ / K424-K474+] Smooth, sand-pale Migurd demon complexion, perpetually youthful Fair, smooth pale demon complexion | Face: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0-50+ / K424-K474] Deadpan, sleepy, doll-like Migurd face with an adorable mischievous smirk when playing pranks Mouth shaped like Rudeus's, otherwise a carbon copy of her mother and grandmother; sleepy doll-like facial features, rarely changes expression | Hair: [Childhood in Sharia: Age 0-14 / K424-K438] Striking deep water-blue (light blue) Migurd hair covering most of the forehead except a single ahoge, worn in thick, messy twin braids draping down her chest adorned with beads; sometimes misperceived as Superd green; [Adulthood: Age 17+ / K441+] Tied into a single braid to differentiate herself from her two-braided mother Roxy | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K424+] Aquamarine-blue Migurd eyes possessing telepathic foresight and divine animal resonance Large, mysterious deep blue-indigo (light blue) eyes like Roxy's; possesses innate racial Migurd telepathy and prophetic vision; drowsy, half-lidded expression | Distinguishing Traits: Natural telepathic resonance; often carried riding atop Sacred Beast Leo; carries the prophecy of the world savior | Outfits by Timeline: [Childhood in Sharia: Age 0-14 / K424-K438] Soft oversized woolen sweaters, blue ribbons, child's clothing worn in the Greyrat household, sleeping on Leo's white back; [Prophesied Hero Era / Late Timeline: Age 30+ / K454+] Wandering traveler robes, mage's traveling attire with the Aqua Heartia staff, customized wizard hat resembling Roxy's, riding Leo through the wilderness}

# Ars Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Ars Greyrat, Ars -->

SHORT_DESC: {First son of Rudeus Greyrat and firstborn of Eris Boreas Greyrat [Birth: K428]; named 'Arus' (Ars) after the legendary human hero who led the six heroes against Demon King Necross Lacross; inherited Eris's ferocious physical strength, athletic animal instincts, and blazing crimson hair, paired with Paul and Rudeus's underlying lustfulness and penchant for causing trouble; trained from toddlerhood in the Sword God Style under Eris and Ghislaine, achieving Master-tier swordsmanship at an extraordinarily young age; deeply attached to his half-aunt Aisha Greyrat, developing an intense romantic devotion that culminated in their dramatic elopement; later served as a top-tier vanguard operative for the Orsted Corporation, demonstrating fearsome martial prowess and steadfast loyalty to his family | [Personality & Quirks] Inherited the lecherous Greyrat bloodline—as an infant, bawled his eyes out when held by Rudeus because his father's pecs were rock-hard instead of soft like a woman's breast; loved fondling breasts as a toddler, prompting grandmother Zenith to note telepathically that he grabbed her chest with a pleased smirk just like baby Rudy; burst into terrified tears whenever Dragon God Orsted looked his way during Orsted's rare visits; raised with Eris's boisterous, rough parenting, developing a stubborn, fearless demeanor that only yields to Aisha; possesses an explosive temper when anyone insults his loved ones, yet shows deep filial piety to Rudeus; mimics his mother's stance of resting a hand casually on his sword pommel; prone to sneaking sweets from the kitchen with his sisters.}

HISTORY: {[Childhood: Sharia & Sword Training: Age 0-10 / K426-K436] Born in Sharia while Rudeus was on mission in Shirone; welcomed with joy by Eris, Norn, and Aisha; demonstrated overwhelming physical resilience and sword affinity from toddlerhood; sparred relentlessly with Eris and Ghislaine, developing lightning-fast reflexes and aggressive Sword God combinations; clung tenaciously to Aisha, who nurtured and educated him with unmatched devotion; [The Elopement Crisis: Age 10-12 / K436-K438] Confessed his romantic devotion to Aisha around age ten; engaged in a secret romantic relationship that resulted in Aisha's pregnancy; upon discovery by Rudeus, fled Sharia with Aisha in a desperate elopement, evading trackers across Ranoa before being cornered; earnestly defended his love before Rudeus and Eris, proving his maturity and willingness to bear the responsibilities of fatherhood; [Adulthood: Family Father & Orsted Operative: Age 15+ / K441+] Reconciled with the Greyrat family; married Aisha and reared their son Leroy; undertook high-risk mercenary and security assignments across the Central Continent on behalf of the Orsted Corporation; witnessed his granddaughter Feris inherit Eris's spitting image.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 0-7 / K426-K433] Energetic, broad-shouldered growing boy; [Adolescent: Age 10-15 / K436-K441] 168cm, rapid muscular growth inheriting Eris's athletic power and Paul's physique; [Adult: Age 18+ / K444+] 178cm / 5'10", 74kg, heavily muscled, athletic swordsman build, strong and good-looking; wide chest, powerful sword-arms; [Late Timeline: Age 50+ / K476+] Sturdy veteran swordsman build, grizzled jaw | Height & Weight: [Age 10 / K436] 142cm, 36kg; father of Aisha's child Leroy | Skin: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K426-K436] Sun-kissed golden skin; [Swordsman & Knight: Age 11+ / K437+] Rugged, tanned, athletic warrior complexion Fair; sun-tanned from outdoor sword training | Face: [Late Timeline / Adult: Age 17+ / K443+] Handsome, blending Eris's strong features with Rudeus's, closely resembling his grandfather Paul but with fiery hair; intense, bold, and confident | Distinguishing Traits: Exceptional physical strength; natural aptitude for the Sword God style; red-haired reflection of Eris | Outfits by Timeline: [Childhood in Sharia: Age 0-10 / K426-K436] Durable leather play tunics, wooden practice swords, child's clothing worn in the Greyrat household; # Sieghart Saladin Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Sieghart Saladin Greyrat, Sieghart, Sieghardt, Death God -->

SHORT_DESC: {Second son of Rudeus Greyrat and second child of Sylphiette [Birth: K429]; born with bright emerald-green hair bearing the Laplace mana factor, initially terrifying Rudeus before being accepted and loved unconditionally; nicknamed 'Sieg'; named in honor of the invincible hero Siegfried and given the baptismal name 'Saladin' by Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola; possesses titanic physical strength, quiet humility, and an instinctive sense of justice; trained in the North God Style under Kalman II (Sandor von Grandeur), achieving the rank of North Emperor before succeeding Randolph Marianne as the Death God; wields the legendary Dragon-King Sword 'Dragon Slayer' with devastating mastery, becoming one of the most powerful martial figures of the post-Rudeus era | [Personality & Quirks] Born placid and calm, crying normally unlike Lara (who never cried) or Ars (who bawled at his father's hard chest); in childhood, suffered from a quiet inferiority complex, feeling alienated by his green hair and inability to excel at chant magic like his parents; deeply gentle by nature, hating unnecessary violence; inspired by Sandor's tales to become an ally of justice, secretly dressing in a hooded cloak and mask as the hero 'Moonlight'; reflexively bows his head and stammers when praised; extremely fond of sweet pastries baked by Sylphie; holds boundless respect for his father Rudeus and master Sandor, seeking to protect the weak without taking human lives.}

HISTORY: {[Infancy: The Green Hair Crisis: Age 0-2 / K427-K429] Born in Sharia with vivid green hair identical to Sylphiette's childhood color; Rudeus panicked fearing Hitogami's curse or the premature birth of Laplace; Orsted visited in secret at midnight to inspect the baby, confirming Laplace had not yet reincarnated; Lord Perugius summoned the family to Chaos Breaker, baptizing the infant in ancient divination water that glowed yellow (proving human nature) and bestowing the honorable name Saladin; [Youth: The Hero Complex & Sandor's Mentorship: Age 6-14 / K433-K441] Grew up quiet and contemplative, feeling detached from normal children due to his immense physical strength and green hair stigma; mentored by North God Kalman II (Sandor), who instilled in him the philosophy of true heroism and defensive justice; mastered North God swordsmanship, effortlessly deflecting lethal blows and neutralizing armed bandits without killing; [Adulthood: The Death God & Global Champion: Age 15+ / K442+] Traveled to the Shirone Republic and King Dragon Realm; intervened in geopolitical conflicts; inherited the legendary mantle of Death God from Randolph Marianne; stood as a monumental guardian of peace throughout the Six-Faced World.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth to Death God: Age 10-30+ / K437-K457+] Practical traveler's leathers, North God Style swordsgear, practice rapiers; [Death God Era: Age 20+] Black-and-gold Death God duster coat, reinforced steel shoulder guards, dual enchanted blades, Dragon King Sword}

# Lily Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Lily Greyrat, Lily -->

SHORT_DESC: {Second daughter and fifth child of Rudeus Greyrat and Roxy Migurdia Greyrat [Birth: K432]; inherited Roxy's signature aquatic-blue hair, large inquisitive eyes, and petite Migurd stature, combined with Rudeus's intense inventive focus; showed little interest in conventional martial arts, physical combat, or elemental chant casting, instead demonstrating extraordinary genius for magical tool engineering, rune carving, and automaton fabrication; became the prime apprentice and protégée of Prince Zanoba Shirone and Juliette in the Sharia Magical Workshop; collaborated in the mass-manufacturing of advanced prosthetic limbs, household magical appliances, and next-generation Magic Armor components; eventually succeeded Zanoba as Managing Director of the Sharia Magical Tool Corporation, revolutionizing industrial magical manufacturing across the Central Continent and perfecting the General Purpose Magic Armor in Year K483 | [Personality & Quirks] Quiet, introverted, and chronically absent-minded in daily life, but transforms into an intensely focused workaholic the moment she enters a workshop; perpetually has smudges of machine oil, clay dust, or graphite on her nose and fingers; routinely forgets to brush her hair or change out of her stained work smock, causing Roxy and Sylphiette to chase her down with combs; falls asleep slumped directly over her workbenches clutching miniature gears and calipers; loves tinkering with mechanical clocks and music boxes; speaks in a soft, monotone mutter that speeds up rapidly when explaining complex magical circuitry; possesses zero interest in romance or socialite parties, preferring the company of golems, automatons, and Julie's forge.}

HISTORY: {[Youth to Adult Researcher: Age 10-30+ / K438-K458+] Designed mass-market prosthetic arms and automated farming golems; assumed direct leadership of the Sharia Toy and Tool Guilds, ensuring the lasting technological legacy of the Greyrat family; [K483: General Purpose Magic Armor Breakthrough: Age 55] Perfected the mass-producible General Purpose Magic Armor, enabling ordinary warriors and adventurers without monstrous mana pools to pilot powered exoskeletons, drastically reducing mortality rates in high-rank monster subjugations.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0-10 / K428-K438] 147cm / 4'10", 39kg; petite half-Migurd demon frame closely resembling Roxy, growing into a petite Migurdian stature; youthful demon longevity; slender, bookish, slightly clumsy posture, often lost in thought | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 17+ / K445+] 147cm / 4'10", 39kg | Breasts: [Childhood & Lifelong Migurd Stature: Age 0-50+ / K428-K478] Petite, modest A-cup, resembles Roxy closely | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood: Age 0-14 / K428-K442] Undeveloped demon child anatomy | [Adult Scholar: Age 15+ / K443+] 4.8", Tightness: Snug | Skin: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0-40+ / K428-K468+] Pale, delicate Migurd demon skin, perpetually smooth Fair, soft pale demon skin, perpetually smudged with oil and soot | Face: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0-40+ / K428-K468] Cute, bespectacled, studious doll-like face with thoughtful resting expression Resembles Roxy closely; round studious cheeks, distracted expression behind round spectacles | Hair: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K428+] Silky water-blue hair tied back in a neat workshop ponytail with dark ribbon Brilliant water-blue hair tied into twin braids draped over her shoulders, often messy with workshop hairpins and clips holding stray strands | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K428+] Curious, analytical aquamarine-blue eyes behind round brass reading glasses Calm, studious aquamarine-blue eyes like Roxy's, peering through round reading spectacles | Distinguishing Traits: Non-telepathic Migurd traits; magic item workshop grease stains on fingertips and apron; uncanny knack for mechanical disassembly | Outfits by Timeline: [Childhood in Sharia: Age 0-9 / K428-K437] Simple cotton play smocks, canvas shoes, child's clothing worn in the Greyrat household; [Adult Researcher / Magician: Age 10+ / K438+] Sturdy leather research apron with deep tool pockets, tool belts loaded with calipers and crystals, comfortable academic robes (mage's casual attire)}

# Christina Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Christina Greyrat, Christina -->

SHORT_DESC: {Second daughter of Rudeus Greyrat and Eris Boreas Greyrat, youngest of the six Greyrat siblings [Birth: K434]; nicknamed 'Chris'; inherited Eris's vibrant crimson hair and fiery athletic vitality, harmoniously balanced by an unexpected talent for noble court etiquette, statecraft, and social poise; mentored and favored from early childhood by Queen Ariel Anemoi Asura; completed primary education at the Magic University of Ranoa before enrolling at the Royal Asuran Academy of Aristocracy in Ars; fell in love with and married First Prince Edward of Asura (son of Queen Ariel and Luke Notos Greyrat), eventually crowned Queen Consort of the Asuran Kingdom; stands as the political crowning achievement of the Greyrat family, cementing a perpetual alliance between House Asura, the Greyrat lineage, and the Orsted Corporation | [Personality & Quirks] Harbored a monumental, unabashed 'father complex' since toddlerhood—idolized Rudeus above all living beings, clinging to his knees, sitting on his lap during family councils, and demanding affectionate headpats; unlike Eris who despised books and studying, Christina possessed sharp intellectual curiosity, eagerly learning court manners, high Asuran dance, and calligraphy from Sylphiette, Roxy, and Lillia; inherited Eris's fierce pride and physical backbone beneath her refined noble veneer—when arrogant high-ranking Asuran noble students attempted to intimidate or ostracize her as an upstart noble at the Academy of Aristocracy, she coldly dismantled their schemes with dignified poise and devastating wit; blushes intensely whenever praised by Rudeus; shares a warm, playful relationship with Prince Edward, playfully scolding him when he slacks on his studies.}

HISTORY: {[Childhood: Sharia Manor: Age 0-9 / K429-K438] Reared as the beloved youngest child in the bustling Sharia Greyrat estate; instructed in basic Sword God Style footwork and sword defense by Eris, while receiving thorough tutoring in diplomacy, etiquette, and literature from Sylphiette and Roxy; [Youth: Ranoa Academy & Ariel's Sponsorship: Age 10-14 / K439-K443] Enrolled at the Magic University of Ranoa; demonstrated sharp political acumen and natural charisma; caught the eye of Queen Ariel during royal visits to Sharia, who sponsored her transfer to the Royal Asuran Academy of Aristocracy in the capital of Ars; excelled in royal rhetoric, administrative law, and international diplomacy, winning over the conservative Asuran high aristocracy; [Adulthood: Marriage to Prince Edward & Queen of Asura: Age 15+ / K444+] Formed a profound romantic bond with childhood friend Prince Edward of Asura; overcame minor court intrigues with the backing of Rudeus, Eris, and Queen Ariel; married Edward in a grand royal ceremony in the Silver Palace of Ars; crowned Queen Consort of Asura upon Edward's ascension to the throne, stewarding the realm through a golden era of educational expansion and global cooperation.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood in Sharia: Age 0-9 / K429-K438] Lively, affectionate noble girl, growing rapidly; [Late Timeline / Adult: Age 17+ / K446+] 168cm / 5'6", 54kg, athletic, voluptuous, graceful figure combining Eris's height and curves with feminine poise, resembling Eris's silhouette | Height & Weight: [Age 10 / K439] 140cm, 32kg; [Adult: Age 17+ / K446+] 168cm, 54kg | Breasts: [Childhood: Age 0-9 / K429-K438] Prepubescent child, flat | [Adolescent: Age 10-15 / K439-K444] Developing athletic curves (B-cup) | [Adult: Age 18+ / K447+] Voluptuous D-cup to E-cup, inheriting Eris's bust | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood & Adolescent: Age 0-15 / K429-K444] Undeveloped noble girl anatomy | [Adult: Age 18+ / K447+] 5.4", Tightness: Snug | Skin: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K429-K439] Fair, soft noble skin; [Adult Imperial Princess: Age 15+ / K444+] Luminous, porcelain noble complexion Fair noble skin; radiant, smooth, and well-cared for | Face: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K429-K439] Adorable, cheerful, affectionate girl; [Adult Princess: Age 15+ / K444+] Stunning, radiant aristocratic beauty with refined features and a captivating smile Resembles Eris closely; gentle facial contours, bright warm smile, sparkling eyes | Hair: [Childhood: Age 0-10 / K429-K439] Flaming crimson-red hair in elegant ringlets; [Adult: Age 15+ / K444+] Luxurious crimson curls cascading over shoulders Fiery crimson red hair like Eris's, styled in elegant aristocratic curls framing her shoulders as an adult, tied with silk ribbons | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K429+] Sparkling ruby-amber eyes radiating noble warmth and intelligence Warm, affectionate amber-red eyes like her mother Eris, shining with intelligence | Distinguishing Traits: Deeply attached to Rudeus; courtly grace; fiery red hair styled in aristocratic ringlets; eventual Princess and Queen of Asura | Outfits by Timeline: [Childhood in Sharia: Age 0-9 / K429-K438] Cozy family dresses, child's clothing worn in the Greyrat household, hugging Rudeus; [Ranoa Student: Age 10-14 / K439-K443] Academy girl uniform with pleated skirt; [Royal Academy of Aristocracy: Age 15-17 / K444-K446] Prestigious crimson-and-white Asuran student uniform with gold embroidery; [Late Timeline: Asura Court Princess & Queen: Age 18+ / K447+] Opulent gold-and-purple Asuran imperial court gowns, diamond tiara, traveling attire befitting a queen of Asura}

# Ruijerd Superdia
<!-- Keys: Ruijerd Superdia, Ruijerd, Dead End, Ruijerd of the Superd -->

SHORT_DESC: {500+ year-old legendary Superd warrior, former elite war chief of the Superd battalion under Demon God Laplace, and husband of Norn Greyrat [Birth: pre-Laplace War era]; renowned and feared across the world as the bogeyman 'Dead End'; possesses pale-emerald hair, chalk-white skin, an athletic, scarred warrior physique, and a ruby-colored jewel on his forehead (the Devil's Eye) granting omnidirectional sensory perception; during the Laplace War, his unit was tricked by Demon God Laplace with cursed spears that turned them into frenzied, indiscriminate killers slaughtering allies and civilians alike, culminating in Ruijerd slaying his own son; his dying son snapped the cursed trident, freeing Ruijerd's mind; spent over four centuries roaming the world to clear the cursed stigma of the Superd race and locate any surviving kin; governed by an absolute code of honor, protecting children with terrifying martial ferocity; served as Rudeus and Eris's guardian across the Demon and Central Continents; later married Norn Greyrat and fathered Luicelia, restoring his race's honored place in history | [Personality & Quirks] Stoic, austere, and profoundly gentle toward children, whom he views as sacred treasures; habitually pats children on the head with his massive, calloused hand, saying 'You fought well' or 'Good child'; on the Demon Continent, adhered to an ancient warrior diet of plain boiled or raw monster meat, but secretly developed an immense fondness for Rudeus's seasoned cooking; routinely shaves his head completely smooth every few days to hide his emerald Superd hair beneath bandages or dirt, maintaining his razor with methodical care; regards his black tail-spear as his literal soul and never lets it leave his reach; becomes an unstoppable demon of wrath against anyone who harms children (nearly executing pet kidnappers and scoundrels on the spot); initially hesitant regarding his courtship with Norn due to their five-century age gap, treating her with tender, protective reverence and letting her rest against his broad chest; breaks into a rare, breathtakingly gentle smile when children show him affection without fear.}

HISTORY: {[Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Commanded the Superd shock-troop vanguard for Demon God Laplace; issued cursed tridents that clouded their minds with bloodlust; slaughtered demon allies and human civilians; mortally wounded his young son, who shattered Ruijerd's trident with his teeth, breaking the curse; swore an eternal vow to hunt Laplace, clear his people's name, and protect children across all lands; [Wandering Centuries: The Dead End Legend: Age 100-500 / Pre-K400 to K400s] Traveled alone for over four hundred years; universally shunned and feared as a child-eating demon; [Dead End Party: K417-K419: Age 557-559] Discovered 10-year-old Rudeus and Eris on the Demon Continent following the Mana Catastrophe; formed the 'Dead End' adventurer party; guarded the children across thousands of leagues through the Great Forest and across the ocean; stood firm against North Saint Gallus Cleaner; fought valiantly to protect Rudeus during their fatal clash with Dragon God Orsted; parted ways with tears in Fittoa after escorting Rudeus and Eris safely home; [Millis to Sharia Escort: Age 560 / K418-K420] Located young Aisha and Norn in Millis; escorted them across continents to Sharia, forming an unbreakable bond of trust and affection with Norn; [Biheiril Campaign & Superd Village Liberation: Age 567 / K427] Reunited with Rudeus in the Biheiril Kingdom; found the hidden Superd refuge afflicted by a terminal plague engineered by Hitogami's apostle Geese; saved through Rudeus and Cliff's cure; fought on the frontlines alongside Kalman II against the Fighting God; [Marriage to Norn & Restored Honor: Age 571+ / K431+] Married Norn Greyrat despite their vast age difference; fathered half-Superd daughter Luicelia, witnessing the complete redemption of the Superd name.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] 190cm / 6'3", 92kg | Penile: [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Alabaster-pale, rock-hard Superd demon skin, smooth and nearly unaging Porcelain white, smooth throughout, weather-beaten alabaster demon skin crisscrossed with scars from five centuries of warfare | Face: [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Long scar trailing from right top of forehead across glabella to left cheek | Hair: [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Emerald green, mid-length; [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Piercing sapphire-blue eyes; ruby-red Third Eye (Devil Eye) on forehead detecting life force Fierce, piercing ruby-red eyes | Forehead: [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Turns blue with ominous markings when possessed by Abyssal King Vita | Distinguishing Traits: Superd demon physiology; forked tail that hardened and detached to become his black Superd trident spear, which he considers his soul; formidable battle aura radiating intimidation | Outfits by Timeline: [Centuries Constant: Age 500-570+ / K400-K430+] Ancient Superd warrior tribal armor, obsidian pauldrons; [Dead End Odyssey: Age 557-560 / K417-K420] Brown travel cloaks, brown tribal vest covering back and sides only (exposing frontal torso and arms, never wears a shirt), leather harness, rugged fur-lined trousers, trail wraps, holding his black Superd tail spear, necklace gifted by Rudeus; [Biheiril and Marriage to Norn: Age 567+ / K427+] Distinguished warrior attire, Superd clan tunic, polished spear, wedding medallion}

# Zanoba Shirone
<!-- Keys: Zanoba Shirone, Zanoba -->

SHORT_DESC: {Former Third Prince of the Shirone Kingdom, Blessed Child (Miko) of monstrous physical might, and master artisan of magical craftsmanship [Birth: K397]; born with superhuman muscle density and absolute invulnerability to mundane physical harm, yet utterly incapable of regulating his output—resulting in the horrific childhood accident where he tore the head off his infant brother, earning the dreaded title 'Head Ripping Prince'; exiled to the Ranoa Magic Academy as a political outcast; possesses an ecstatic, worshipful adoration for carved figures, statues, and automatons, revering Rudeus Greyrat as his supreme idol, mentor, and 'Master' (Shishou); co-founded the Sharia Magical Tool Workshop with Rudeus and Julie; co-engineered the Zariff Prosthetic Arm and the historic Magic Armor series; an art connoisseur whose sincere aesthetic purism earned the genuine respect of Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola; supremely loyal to Rudeus, willingly renouncing royal privilege, family status, and life itself to defend the Greyrat household | [Personality & Quirks] Speaks with booming, flamboyant royal cadence ('Ha-ha-ha!', 'Indeed, Master!', 'A masterpiece beyond compare!'), frequently pushing up his thick round spectacles with a heavy finger; prone to passionate, ecstatic speeches extolling the divine curves, clothing wrinkles, and removable panties of figurines, entering a trance-like state that unnerves onlookers; deeply afflicted with tragic sorrow over his monstrous strength, having broken countless delicate tools, quill pens, and clay sculptures before Julie became his hands; treats Julie not as a slave but as a cherished adoptive daughter, indulging her with dresses and sweets; incapable of using offensive magic due to negligible mana capacity, relying purely on god-given physical might; fiercely loyal to his homeland Shirone despite his exile, rushing to its defense during Pax's coup; drinks tea delicately with two fingers to avoid shattering porcelain cups.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-20: Shirone Royal Court & Exile / K401-K421] Born into House Shirone as a Blessed Child; feared as a monster after accidentally killing his baby brother; discovered Rudeus's sculpted Roxy figurine, elevating it to a holy idol; revolted against Prince Pax's imprisonment of Rudeus, physically freeing him; exiled to Ranoa; [Age 22-26: Ranoa Academy & Doll Workshop / K423-K427] Greeted Rudeus in Sharia as his devoted disciple; purchased dwarf slave Juliette (Julie) and trained her as his sculpting hands; partnered with Cliff Grimoire and Rudeus to develop automated dolls and the Zariff Prosthetic Arm for Rudeus's severed left hand; [Age 26-27: Shirone Defense Campaign / K427-K428] Returned to Shirone to defend his kingdom against a coup orchestrated by Hitogami; renounced his royal rights to help Prince Pax establish a republic; [Age 27+: Orsted Corporation & Magic Armor Innovation / K428+] Engineered the colossal Magic Armor (Versions 1 and 2) alongside Rudeus to duel Dragon God Orsted and the Fighting God Badigadi; founded the Sharia Toy Guild and Ruijerd Figurine Corporation; [Oldeus Alternate Future: Age 22-45 / Alternate K423-K446] In the dark timeline of the Diary, remained by Rudeus's side through his drunken ruin; crafted an automaton in Sylphie's likeness (which Rudeus destroyed); died an immortal hero's death charred outside the Sharia cellar door, holding off Milis Temple Knights to the death to protect Aisha, Julie, and Ginger.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adolescent to Adult Prince: Age 15-30+ / K416-K431+] average, intact, ~5" | [Adolescent to Adult Prince: Age 15-30+ / K416-K431+] average, intact, ~5" | [Adolescent to Adult Prince: Age 15-30+ / K416-K431+] average, intact, ~5", doll-maker's hands | Skin: [Adolescent to Adult Prince: Age 15-30+ / K416-K431+] Heavy scholarly vest, Ranoa Academy uniform with scholar's robe, rolled-up linen sleeves, thick spectacles, sculptor's apron, carrying miniature figurines; [Biheiril Battle: Age 26 / K427] Custom titan-sized steel breastplate, giant war club}

# Juliette (Julie)
<!-- Keys: Juliette, Julie -->

SHORT_DESC: {Coal-mine dwarf girl, adopted apprentice, sculptress, and surrogate daughter to Prince Zanoba Shirone and Rudeus Greyrat [Birth: K414]; purchased from the Sharia slave market at age five by Rudeus and Zanoba to serve as Zanoba's earth-sculpting hands, as Zanoba's Blessed Child strength constantly shattered delicate materials; affectionately named 'Julie'; nurtured with paternal love and taught common human speech, reading, arithmetic, and voiceless earth chant-casting by Rudeus; developed extraordinary artistic precision in sculpting, stone manipulation, and magical doll assembly; gentle, soft-spoken, and deeply grateful, she formed an unbreakable familial bond with Zanoba, caring for his daily needs while assisting his cutting-edge research; in mature life, mastered dwarven magical smithing and succeeded Zanoba in managing the Sharia Automated Doll Workshop | [Personality & Quirks] Extremely shy, polite, and timid in conversation, but fiercely determined and hardworking; terrified of displeasing Zanoba or Rudeus, she practiced earth magic until her fingers bled when first learning; notoriously picky about bitter vegetables as a child, stubbornly staring down carrots on her plate like a punished grade-schooler; panicked and dropped her bacon back onto her plate when Rudeus scolded her for eating with her bare hands; learned provocative finger-eating manners from Elinalise (prompting Rudeus to smack Elinalise); possesses a deep maternal protectiveness over Zanoba, ensuring he eats, washes his clothes, and remembers his glasses; covers her face with her sleeves when embarrassed; smells of baked kiln clay, chisel oil, and molten solder.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-5: Coal Mine Slavery / K414-K419] Born into a destitute coal-mining dwarf clan on the Begaritt Continent; sold into human slavery due to crushing clan debts; starved and traumatized in a Sharia slave pen until discovered by Rudeus and Zanoba; [Age 5-10: Education & Apprenticeship / K419-K424] Welcomed into the Greyrat household and Zanoba's dormitory; nurtured with warm food, clothes, and patience; taught spoken language and earth magic by Rudeus; sculpted intricate clay figurines and automated doll joint components; treated Zanoba as her beloved father; [Age 10-15: Sharia Workshop Co-Founder / K424-K429] Assisted Zanoba in manufacturing the mass-market Ruijerd Superdia redemption figurines; constructed precision mana-conductive gears for the Zariff Prosthetic Arm and Magic Armor; [Age 15+: Master Artisan & Dwarf Matriarch / K429+] Traveled with Zanoba during the Biheiril campaign to provide field repairs for Rudeus's Magic Armor; inherited Zanoba's workshop and mentored Lily Greyrat in runic dollcraft; [Oldeus Alternate Fate: Age 9-32 / Alternate K423-K446] In the dark future of the Diary, remained loyal to Zanoba in the Sharia mansion; butchered by Milis Temple Knights in the cellar alongside Aisha and Ginger after Zanoba was immolated guarding the door.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth to Adult Sculptor: Age 7-30+ / K421-K444+] ~120cm, healthy child frame; [Youth to Adult Sculptor: Age 7-30+ / K421-K444+] ~100cm, 18kg; [Adult: Age 17+ / K431+] 130cm, 42kg | Breasts: [Childhood Slave: Age 4-7 / K418-K421] Prepubescent, flat | [Adult Dwarven Sculptress: Age 18+ / K432+] Compact, modest B-cup dwarven bust | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood: Age 4-15 / K418-K429] Undeveloped dwarven child anatomy | [Adult: Age 18+ / K432+] 4.9", Tightness: Snug | Skin: [Slave Childhood: Age 4-6 / K418-K420] Sallow, bruised; [Apprentice in Sharia: Age 7+ / K421+] Healthy, fair dwarven skin Warm tan dwarven complexion, soft throughout, smudged with clay dust and kiln soot | Face: [Slave: Age 4-6 / K418-K420] Hollow-eyed, gaunt; [Sculptor Apprentice: Age 7+ / K421+] Adorable, chubby-cheeked dwarven child face with bright smile Round, childish, adorable features; big expressive eyes full of loyalty and dedication | Hair: [Childhood to Adult: Age 4+ / K418+] Thick, braided dark brown hair tied with colorful wool ties Sandy-blonde dwarven hair, long and feathery, tied into practical braids or held by leather ribbons | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 4+ / K418+] Large, warm amber eyes gleaming with gratitude and crafting focus Big, expressive dark brown eyes full of loyalty and dedication | Distinguishing Traits: Dwarven physical strength; master earth sculptor hands; perpetual clay dust smudges on cheeks | Outfits by Timeline: [Slave Market: Age 4-6 / K418-K420] Tattered slave rags, iron collar, barefoot; [Sharia Sculptor Apprentice: Age 7+ / K421+] White one-piece sleeved dress with skirt and dark sandals, neat working dress, heavy leather apron, clay-carving chisels, small casting wand}
