# Cliff Grimoire
<!-- Keys: Cliff Grimoire, Cliff -->

SHORT_DESC: {Human/dwarf hybrid magical prodigy, foremost curse and barrier researcher of the contemporary era, and husband of Elinalise Dragonroad [Birth: K406]; grandson of Pope Harry Grimoire of the Holy Land of Milis; born with dwarf blood giving him shorter stature, dark brown hair, and an innate genius for complex magical enchantments and multi-discipline casting; adopted by Pope Harry at age five after his parents died; initially an arrogant, boastful youth proclaiming himself an unrivaled genius, his core character is defined by fierce moral integrity, stubborn bravery, and unconditional devotion to his loved ones; enrolled at Ranoa Magic Academy to escape Milis papal succession plots; fell head-over-heels in love with Elinalise Dragonroad, accepting her mana buildup curse without hesitation and dedicating his career to curing her; inventor of the curse-suppressing artifact ring, the Zariff Prosthetic Arm, and Dragon God Orsted's curse-Adult Milis Mageifying helmet; eventually returned to Milis to succeed his grandfather, reforming the Church into a global force of humanitarian aid and anti-Hitogami resistance | [Personality & Quirks] Hot-headed, prideful, and pedantic, constantly quoting strict Milis theology and magical theory; easily flustered and turns bright crimson in the face when teased or when Elinalise displays open physical affection in public; despite his arrogance and bouts of fear, displays incredible moral courage—stood up to threatening thugs twice his size as a boy, and stepped into deadly combat without flinching to protect his friends; fiercely defensive of Elinalise's honor, refusing to let anyone demean her for her past; becomes completely absorbed in research notebooks, pacing while muttering incantations; possesses an innate aversion to Luke Notos Greyrat's shallow flirting; devout believer who says grace before every meal; deeply compassionate physician who heals the poor without asking for fees.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-13: Milis Papal Ward / K403-K416] Raised in the Holy Land of Milis; demonstrated Saint-tier Fire and Advanced Healing magic as a child; clashed with church elders; sent to the Central Continent to evade rival church faction assassins; met Eris Boreas Greyrat in Roa during an assassination ambush, developing an unrequited crush; [Age 13-17: Ranoa Academy & Elinalise Romance / K416-K420] Enrolled at Ranoa Magic Academy; clashed with Rudeus over pride before recognizing his peerless skill; fell head-over-heels in love with Elinalise Dragonroad; learned of her curse requiring frequent sexual intimacy; vowed to cure her condition, proposing marriage with sincere dignity; [Age 17-23: Prosthetics & Curse Breakthroughs / K420-K426] Married Elinalise and fathered Clive Grimoire; collaborated with Zanoba and Rudeus to engineer the Zariff Prosthesis; invented a curse-suppression device that neutralized Elinalise's condition; created Orsted's signature curse-deflecting helmet that suppressed the Dragon God's aura of universal hatred; [Age 23+: Milis Reformation & Papal Succession / K426+] Accompanied Rudeus to the Holy Land of Milis; navigated the deadly conflict between Pope Harry's faction and Archbishop Latriea's extremists; ascended to high authority within the Milis Church, providing institutional backing for the Orsted Corporation; [Oldeus Alternate Tragedy: Age 20-21 / Alternate K423-K424] In the Diary's dark timeline, accompanied Rudeus to Milis to steal God-tier Detoxification scrolls for petrified Roxy; ambushed by Temple Knights and fatally poisoned, dying in the underground cathedral corridors.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Millis Youth: Age 13-15 / K416-K418] Slender, aristocratic Millis scholar youth, shorter and leaner than Rudeus, often smug; [Ranoa Scholar & Adult: Age 18+ / K421+] 170cm / 5'7", 62kg, lean, poised scholarly build, well-groomed; slightly shorter than his wife Elinalise due to dwarf heritage; [Late Timeline: Milis Archbishop: Age 30+ / K433+] 170cm, distinguished priest silhouette, confident posture | Height & Weight: [Age 15 / K418] 158cm, 50kg; [Adult: Age 20+ / K423+] 170cm, 62kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 16+ / K419+] average, intact, ~5" | Skin: [Milis Youth & Academy: Age 13-20 / K416-K423] Pale, smooth Milis noble skin; [Cardinal Era: Age 21+ / K424+] Dignified, pristine complexion Fair, aristocratic Milis complexion, smooth and well-kept | Face: [Youth: Age 13-18 / K416-K421] Arrogant, haughty, sharp-featured; [Married to Elinalise & Cardinal: Age 19+ / K422+] Resolute, handsome, mature features filled with husbandly devotion Youthful, sharp-featured, handsome refined features; [Age 13-15 / K416-K418] often smug, easily reddening when flustered; [Adult: Age 17+ / K420+] composed scholarly bearing behind reading spectacles | Hair: [Youth to Adult: Age 13+ / K416+] Curly, dark brown hair cut in neat academic style Dark brown hair, kept slightly long in front covering his brow in youth, neatly trimmed and parted in adult scholar style | Eyes: [Youth to Adult: Age 13+ / K416+] Sharp, proud dark eyes that soften with immense love when gazing at Elinalise Sharp, proud hazel-brown eyes behind scholarly reading spectacles | Distinguishing Traits: Wears enchanted reading glasses; ink-stained fingers from magical circle research; shorter stature than Elinalise; devout silver crucifix ring | Outfits by Timeline: [Pre-Academy: K406-K419: Age 3-16] Blue magician robe, Milis religious acolyte robes, high-class noble vestments with a silver crucifix; [Academy Era: K419+: Age 16+] Ranoa Academy uniform with leather scroll satchel; [Post-Graduation onward: Age 21-25 / K424-K428] Casual scholar's tunic and trousers; [Milis Church Leader / Adult: Age 26+ / K429+] Resplendent white-and-gold Milis priestly vestments, ceremonial stole, curse-dispelling magic tools}

# Elinalise Dragonroad
<!-- Keys: Elinalise Dragonroad, Elinalise, Spawton curse -->

SHORT_DESC: {Ancient high-elf warrior, grandmother of Sylphiette Greyrat, wife of Cliff Grimoire, and mother of Clive Grimoire [Birth: over 200 years ago]; an ageless, stunning beauty possessing voluminous golden-blonde curls and ruby-amber eyes, discovered over two centuries ago sealed inside an ancient labyrinth crystal by Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola with total retrograde amnesia; afflicted with an ancient, incurable mana-crystallization curse that causes lethal internal mana accumulation unless regularly discharged through sexual intercourse and semen absorption; lived centuries as an adventurer, bearing numerous descendants across the world while earning an unfair reputation for extreme promiscuity; veteran frontline fighter of the legendary S-Rank party 'Fangs of the Black Wolf' alongside Paul Greyrat; an exceptional combatant wielding buckler shields, rapiers, and estocs with agile, acrobatic grace; despite her curse, possesses an intensely maternal, loyal, and romantic heart; found true, lifelong happiness with Cliff Grimoire, who loved her unconditionally and neutralized her curse through magical engineering | [Personality & Quirks] Radiates effortless, intoxicating sensuality, disarming men by looking directly into their eyes and caressing their chests or shoulders; grooms her luxurious golden curls for hours each day with scented floral oils, taking immense pride in her hair; harbors a deep, secret vulnerability and self-loathing regarding her curse, weeping real tears of profound gratitude when Cliff accepted her unconditionally and vowed to cure her; fiercely maternal toward Sylphiette, shedding joyful tears upon discovering their bond and coaching Sylphie on romance and marriage; enjoys teasing Rudeus about his past woman troubles; in combat, serves as an ultra-reliable vanguard tank, deflecting monster fangs and lethal spells with her enchanted buckler while laughing playfully; notoriously fond of fine wine and rich delicacies, often snatching bites off companions' plates with bewitching flair.}

HISTORY: {[Pre-Story: The Crystal Awakening: Age 200+ / Pre-K390] Found 200 years ago trapped inside an ancient labyrinth crystal by Perugius Dola; emerged with zero memory of her origin; discovered her mana buildup curse; wandered the world seeking relief; [Fangs of the Black Wolf: Age 200+ / K400-K415] Joined Paul Greyrat's S-rank adventurer party; had casual dalliances with Paul before stepping aside when Paul fell for Zenith; clashed with straight-laced party members while shielding them with her agile buckler techniques; [Fittoa Search & Begaritt Expedition: Age 200+ / K418-K422] Partnered with Roxy Migurdia to cross the Demon Continent searching for Paul's family; enrolled at Ranoa Magic Academy; reunited with her granddaughter Sylphiette, acting as her protective, worldly elder; [Marriage to Cliff Grimoire: Age 200+ / K422+] Courted earnestly by young Cliff Grimoire; deeply moved when Cliff embraced her curse without judgment; married Cliff, settled in Sharia, and gave birth to their son Clive; [Teleport Labyrinth & Milis Reformation: Age 200+ / K422-K426] Accompanied Rudeus and Paul into the Teleport Labyrinth in Begaritt; assisted in slaying the Manatite Hydra; later relocated to the Holy Country of Milis with Cliff, managing high-society diplomatic networks and standing as a revered matriarch of the extended Greyrat-Grimoire lineage.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Elven Centuries: 200+ Years / K200-K430+] 168cm / 5'6", 54kg; voluptuous, stunning elven bombshell physique - curvy, athletic, wide hips, shapely legs, narrow waist, sculpted hips; completely ageless porcelain skin, unchanged across centuries due to her long elf lifespan | Height & Weight: [Elven Centuries: 200+ Years / K200-K430+] 168cm / 5'6", 54kg | Breasts: [Elven Centuries: 200+ Years / K200-K430+] Seductive, sparkling ruby-red eyes that softened into deep maternal love for Cliff and her children Seductive, sparkling ruby-amber elven eyes with an amused, knowing glint | Distinguishing Traits: Long pointed elven ears; curse of mana crystallization requiring sexual release through intercourse; mother of Laws, grandmother of Sylphiette; millennia of combat experience behind a flirtatious exterior | Outfits by Timeline: [Elven Centuries: 200+ Years / K200-K430+] Black shorts and frilled stockings up to mid-thigh, small red and white corset exposing her midriff to show off her womanly charms, skirt that doesn't cover the front of her legs, gold-colored forearm bands, ornate form-fitting leather cuirass and daring combat skirt with high boots, always carries an estoc and buckler; [Academy Era: K419+: Age 219+] Ranoa Academy uniform worn over her traveling gear; [Ranoa & Married to Cliff: Age 200+ / K420+] Elegant, alluring high-class gowns with plunging necklines, refined jewelry, loving motherly attire}

# Nanahoshi Shizuka
<!-- Keys: Nanahoshi Shizuka, Nanahoshi, Shizuka -->

SHORT_DESC: {High school student summoned whole-body from modern Earth, research scholar, and inventor [Birth: Earth, modern Japan]; the very student whom Rudeus Greyrat died saving from a speeding truck in his previous life; summoned directly into the Six-Faced World in Year K417 during the Mana Catastrophe; does not possess a mana pool and cannot cast magic, but uniquely does not age—her body, hair, and nails remain frozen in the exact state of her arrival; discovered unconscious in the wilderness by Dragon God Orsted, who recognized her foreign existence and became her guardian; adopted the alias 'Silent Seven Star' at Ranoa Magic Academy, achieving A-rank status in the Magic Guild through her profound knowledge of magic circles and summoning mechanics; single-mindedly obsessed with finding a way back home to Japan and her boyfriend Akito Shinohara; introduced modern Japanese cuisine (karaage, gyudon, ramen, pizza) to the world, revolutionizing food culture across the Northern Lands; afflicted with Drain Syndrome (Dry-Skin Curse) caused by mana starvation, which Rudeus cured using Sokas fruit tea; eventually placed herself in suspended animation under Perugius's care until the prophesied hero arrives | [Personality & Quirks] Intensely homesick, cynical, and driven by desperate determination to return to Earth; smokes rolled herbal cigarettes or puffs on a smoking pipe in her lab to cope with crushing academic stress and anxiety; has a desperate craving for Japanese food, bursting into tears of pure joy when Rudeus succeeded in brewing authentic soy sauce and frying crispy karaage; wears a featureless white porcelain mask in public to hide her Asian features and avoid questions; when experiments fail, throws emotional tantrums, slamming notebooks and hurling chalk, followed by days of severe depressive slumps where she curls up under a blanket on her couch refusing to eat; taps her pen rhythmically while muttering Japanese curses; treats Rudeus with a mixture of exasperation, professional reliance, and deep camaraderie as the only other person from Earth.}

HISTORY: {[Earth: Modern Japan: Age 16 / Pre-Summoning / K417+] High school student in Tokyo; walked home with friends Akito Shinohara and Seiji Kuroki; saved from a fatal truck impact when an overweight NEET (Rudeus's past self) tackled them; [Summoning Arrival: Year K417: Age 16 Physical Constant / K417+] Transported directly into the Central Continent during the Fittoa Transfer Incident; rescued by Dragon God Orsted; learned the Human God tongue within a year; [Ranoa Academy & Research: K419-K423: Age 16 Physical Constant / K417+] Enrolled as a special student at Ranoa under Orsted's patronage; conducted groundbreaking experiments reversing summoning circles to return matter to Earth; partnered with Rudeus to supply the massive mana needed for her experiments; successfully summoned Earth objects (water, plastic bottles, tea leaves); [The Drain Syndrome Crisis: Age 16 Physical / K422-K423] Succumbed to the fatal Dry-Skin disease caused by her body's rejection of environmental mana; saved when Rudeus and Kishirika secured Sokas fruit tea from the Demon Continent; [The Dragon General Revelation: Age 16 Physical / K424+] Realized through Orsted and Perugius that she cannot return home until the future era when Akito Shinohara is summoned and the timeline resolves; placed herself in a temporal stasis pod in Perugius's Chaos Breaker fortress, awakening for one day every decade to review progress.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Summoned / Perpetual Japanese High School Body: Age 16 / K417+] 160cm / 5'3", 48kg; slender, slim, average Japanese build with an un-aged adolescent physique; zero mana capacity ('dry body'), chronically tired posture; unchanged across the years in this world; [Chaos Breaker Stasis: Age 16 Physical / K430+] Preserved in suspended animation, body frozen at 16 | Height & Weight: [Perpetually Frozen at Age 16 / K417+] 160cm / 5'3", 48kg | Breasts: [Perpetual Age 16 Body / K417+] Modest A-cup to B-cup, slim Japanese high-school physique | Vaginal Depth: [Perpetual Age 16 Body / K417+] 4.9", Tightness: Tight, unexercised | Skin: [Summoned Constant: Age 16 Physical / K417+] Smooth, fair, unaging Japanese high school skin Smooth, fair East Asian complexion, pale and washed-out from stress and lack of sleep | Face: [Summoned Constant: Age 16 Physical / K417+] Pretty, slightly sharp-featured East Asian face, often serious or exhausted from research Above-average Japanese features, delicate and pretty, dark circles beneath her eyes, perpetually weary skeptical expression; [Academy Era onward: Age 16 Physical Constant / K417+] masked in public by a featureless white mask | Hair: [Summoned Constant: Age 16 Physical / K417+] Straight, glossy jet-black hair with neat straight bangs falling to shoulder blades Long, straight, silky pitch-black Japanese hair with neat blunt bangs across the forehead; never grows in length | Eyes: [Summoned Constant: Age 16 Physical / K417+] Dark brown/black Japanese eyes, sharp with scientific determination and homesickness Dark brown Japanese eyes with intellectual and homesick melancholy, sharp and tired | Distinguishing Traits: Completely ageless, body frozen at 16 (fingernails and hair never grow); lacks mana veins; featureless white porcelain mask worn in public; unmistakably Japanese in posture and habits | Outfits by Timeline: [Summoned / Travel with Orsted: Age 16 Physical / K417-K421] Featureless white mask, hooded traveler cloak over her Japanese high school sailor uniform; [Academy / Research Era: Age 16 Physical Constant / K417+] Ranoa Academy uniform, white research lab coats over modified school uniform, traveling cloak for fieldwork, comfortable indoor slippers; [Chaos Breaker Stasis: Age 16 Physical / K430+] Ceremonial stasis robes, preserved in suspended animation}

# Pursena Adoldia
<!-- Keys: Pursena Adoldia, Pursena -->

SHORT_DESC: {Princess of the Doldia Beast race (Dog tribe), daughter of Chief Gustav Adoldia, and delinquent leader of Ranoa Magic Academy [Birth: K407]; possesses golden hair, floppy dog ears, a wagging tail, and a perpetually hungry, meat-obsessed disposition; habitually ends sentences with the distinctive verbal tick '...nano'; partnered with her feline rival Linia Dedoldia as the co-rulers and dorm bosses of the beastfolk students at Ranoa; an instinctual close-quarters fighter utilizing beastfolk physical reinforcement, sonic howling attacks, and earth/wind magic; challenged Rudeus upon his academy arrival and was thoroughly disciplined alongside Linia, transforming into his loyal, obedient underling; served as an elite bodyguard and reconnaissance scout for Princess Ariel and the Orsted Corporation; later succeeded her father as an honored elder and tribal leader in the Great Forest | [Personality & Quirks] Constantly gnaws on a slab of dried monster jerky or a thick meat bone, even during serious council meetings or lectures; ends nearly every sentence with her trademark verbal tick '...nano'; whenever caught in mischief or scolded by Rudeus ('Boss-man'), immediately points a finger at Linia and throws her under the bus ('It was all Linia's fault, nano!'); tail wags furiously and uncontrollably whenever Rudeus gives her premium smoked meat or pats her head; boasts an enormous, bouncing F-cup bust that she flaunts casually without shame; loves sleeping curled up on plush rugs or under tables like a loyal hound; possesses a devastating vocal shockwave roar capable of stunning monsters and shattering eardrums; deeply fond of Sacred Beast Leo, often grooming his white coat and sharing her meat treats with him; loyal to a fault once tamed, treating Rudeus as her supreme pack leader.}

HISTORY: {[Great Forest Childhood: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Reared in Doldia Village as the chief's daughter; trained in tribal hunting, vocal combat, and beast body reinforcement; sent to the Magic University of Ranoa for foreign study; [Academy Dorm Boss: Age 12-17 / K419-K424] Terrorized new students alongside Linia; challenged Rudeus to a duel in the gymnasium, where Rudeus easily neutralized both using Quagmire and stone bindings; stripped of their boss status and punished by being tied up in their dorms; developed a submissive reverence for Rudeus, calling him 'Boss'; [Ariel Succession & Frontline Combat: Age 18 / K425] Accompanied Rudeus, Sylphiette, and Princess Ariel to Asura; provided critical perimeter scouting and VIP protection during the royal succession struggle; [Biheiril War & Mercenary Service: Age 20 / K427] Mobilized by Rudeus to fight in the Biheiril Kingdom campaign, tracking Geese Nukadia and clashing with Badigadi's vanguard; served as Vice-Leader in the Rudo Mercenary Company with Linia; [Doldia Tribal Leader & Elder: Age 21+ / K428+] Returned to the Great Forest in mature adulthood to guide tribal diplomacy with the Central Continent.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Small cub frame, tribal cub of the Adoldia branch; [Ranoa Student: Age 12-17 / K419-K424] 165cm / 5'5", 56kg, voluptuous, curvy, athletic dog-beastfolk frame, well-padded with healthy appetite; [Adult Beastwoman: Age 18+ / K425+] 165cm, 58kg, curvaceous, powerful beastwoman build with dense muscle tone | Height & Weight: [Childhood: Age 10 / K417] 140cm, 36kg | [Adult: Age 18+ / K425+] 165cm / 5'5", 56-58kg | Breasts: [Childhood: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Small cub, flat | [Academy & Adult: Age 12+ / K419+] Massive, bouncing, soft F-cup with wide cleavage, prominent even among beastfolk | Vaginal Depth: [Childhood: Age 0-14 / K407-K421] Undeveloped cub anatomy | [Adult Beastwoman: Age 15+ / K422+] 5.5", Tightness: Snug, robust, accommodating beastkin anatomy | Skin: [Childhood & Academy: Age 0-17 / K407-K424] Light golden tan, smooth, sun-warmed skin; [Adult Boss: Age 18+ / K425+] Toned, golden-bronze, lightly battle-marked | Face: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K407+] Cute, sleepy-eyed, relaxed dog-girl face with adorable button nose, constantly chewing on dried jerky | Hair: [Childhood: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Scruffy golden cub fuzz; [Adolescent to Adult: Age 12+ / K419+] Long, thick, fluffy light golden-brown hair falling past shoulders | Eyes: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K407+] Large, droopy, relaxed amber-brown eyes that sparkle whenever meat or headpats are offered | Ears & Tail: [Childhood to Adult: Age 0+ / K407+] Floppy golden-blonde dog ears atop her head; large, fluffy wagging tail | Distinguishing Traits: Floppy dog ears and wagging tail; massive F-cup bust; constant meat bone in mouth; verbal tick '...nano'; Sacred Beast Leo's companion | Outfits by Timeline: [Great Forest Youth: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Casual tribal linen tunic, bone beads; [Ranoa Academy: Age 12-17 / K419-K424] Ranoa Academy uniform with skirt tailored for her tail, black thigh-high socks, jerky pouch at belt; [Asura Succession & Sharia Maid: Age 18-19 / K425-K426] Maid uniform with tail hole when serving Rudeus in Sharia; # Linia Dedoldia
<!-- Keys: Linia Dedoldia, Linia -->

SHORT_DESC: {Princess of the Doldia Beast race (Cat tribe), niece of Sword King Ghislaine Dedoldia, and daughter of the Cat Tribe Elder [Birth: K407]; possesses fiery feline reflexes, sharp cat ears, a sleek tail, and an arrogant, mischievous attitude; habitually punctuates sentences with the verbal tick '...nya'; co-leader of the beastfolk students at Ranoa Magic Academy alongside Pursena Adoldia; an agile vanguard martial artist employing claw strikes, feline body-flicker speed, and wind/sound magic; challenged Rudeus upon his arrival at Ranoa and was soundly defeated, becoming his submissive lackey; after graduating, fell into catastrophic merchant debt and was sold into slavery before being rescued by Rudeus; appointed President of the Rudo Mercenary Company, turning it into a thriving international security network | [Personality & Quirks] Feisty, proud, scheming, and easily rattled when outmatched; uses the verbal tick '...nya' in almost every sentence; fiercely competitive with Pursena, often bickering over food or status; deeply afraid of Rudeus's wrath, groveling and offering feline obedience whenever scolded; prone to get-rich-quick schemes that inevitably backfire; intensely proud of her aunt Ghislaine's legendary swordsmanship; purrs when petted on her cat ears by Rudeus; loves shiny trinkets, roasted river fish, and napping in sunny patches; highly capable business administrator once given proper supervision.}

HISTORY: {[Great Forest Childhood: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Raised in Doldia Village under high expectations as Ghislaine's kin; sent to Ranoa Magic Academy to mature; [Ranoa Academy Years: Age 12-17 / K419-K424] Ruled student dorms with Pursena; humiliated by Rudeus after challenging him; served as his obedient lackey; assisted in mediating conflicts between beastfolk and human students; [Post-Graduation Debt & Slavery Crisis: Age 18-19 / K425-K426] Attempted to establish a luxury export business in the Central Continent; defrauded by corrupt merchants, accumulating catastrophic debt; willingly sold herself into criminal slavery in Sharia to pay her creditors; discovered by Rudeus at a slave auction, who purchased her contract and put her to work as a maid and mercenary dispatcher; [President of Rudo Mercenary Company: Age 20+ / K427+] Established and directed the Rudo Mercenary Company under the Orsted Corporation; coordinated intelligence, logistics, and mercenary squads across the world.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood: Age 0-11 / K407-K418] Small cub frame, niece of Ghislaine, pampered royal beast bloodline; [Ranoa Student: Age 12-17 / K419-K424] 162cm / 5'4", 50kg, lithe, acrobatic, athletic cat-beastfolk frame, slender waist, toned feline muscles; # Ghislaine Dedoldia
<!-- Keys: Ghislaine Dedoldia, Ghislaine, Sword King Ghislaine -->

SHORT_DESC: {Legendary Sword King of the Sword God Style, Beast race warrior of the Doldia Cat Tribe, and former bodyguard of Eris Boreas Greyrat [Birth: K380]; known across the world as the 'Black Dog' and the fearsome dark-furred Sword King; possesses an imposing, muscular, and athletic physique clad in minimal leather armor; bearer of a rare Demon Eye (the Demon Eye of Magic Perception) gifted by Kishirika Kishirisu, allowing her to see mana flows and concealed spells; former wild delinquent who abandoned her village, joining Paul Greyrat's S-Rank party 'Fangs of the Black Wolf'; completely illiterate and innumerate until hired by Philip Boreas Greyrat in Roa, where 7-year-old Rudeus taught her basic reading, writing, and arithmetic in exchange for advanced sword lessons; intensely loyal, blunt, and honorable, she served as Eris's martial mentor and maternal protector; an unstoppable whirlwind in melee combat wielding the enchanted Longsword of Light | [Personality & Quirks] Feral, blunt, and uncomplicated in thought, but deeply honorable and loyal to a fault; tail swishes rhythmically when alert and twitches nervously when agitated or forced to do bookwork; continually sniffs the air to detect enemies, incoming weather, or blood scents before anyone else; was completely unable to count beyond ten before Rudeus tutored her (counting on her fingers and looking utterly baffled when she ran out of fingers), developing an almost sacred reverence and eternal gratitude toward Rudeus for teaching her to read and do math; protective to the point of ferocity regarding Eris, acting as her fierce feline surrogate mother and baring her fangs at anyone who threatens her; eats massive hunks of roasted monster meat with her bare hands, chewing down to the bone and washing it down with fiery demon ale; light sleeper whose ears twitch at the faintest rustle, capable of waking and slicing an assassin in half in a single breath; terrified her delinquent niece Linia with a single terrifying glare when they met in later years; treated Paul with casual physical camaraderie in the old days, with Paul often joking that her rear and abs were as hard as solid granite.}

HISTORY: {[Great Forest Rebel: Age 0-10 / K380-K390] Rebelled against tribal traditions in Doldia Village; fled to become an adventurer; [Fangs of the Black Wolf: Age 20-35 / K400-K415] Formed the legendary party with Paul, Elinalise, and Talhand; developed a fierce rivalry and physical relationship with Paul; parted ways when the group disbanded; [Sword Sanctum & Roa Bodyguard: Age 36-37 / K416-K417] Studied under Sword God Gal Farion, attaining the rank of Sword King; hired by House Boreas Greyrat to protect young Eris; tutored Rudeus in advanced swordsmanship while learning arithmetic and language from him; [Mana Catastrophe & Asuran Search: Age 37-43 / K417-K423] Teleported to the Asuran royal palace during the incident; imprisoned by political factions before escaping; joined the royal knight corps to search for Eris and the Boreas family; [Reunion & Biheiril Campaign: Age 44-47+ / K424-K427+] Reunited with Eris after Eris became a Sword King; fought side-by-side with Eris and Rudeus in the battle against North God Kalman III and the Fighting God; served as chief martial instructor for the royal Asuran guards and the Greyrat children.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Sword King Career: Age 20-47+ / K400-K427+] 182cm / 6'0", 76kg, towering, overwhelmingly muscular, ripped amazon feline beastwoman physique; chiseled six-pack abs, dense warrior musculature, slender with rippling muscles, amazonian frame exposed by her revealing outfit; raced across rooftops, shattered a sword in midair and decapitated attackers with a shockwave that tore up the stone road; [Sword King Career: Age 20-47+ / K400-K427+] Veteran Sword King, still physically terrifying | Height & Weight: [Sword King Career: Age 20-47+ / K400-K427+] Revealing leather outfit exposing torso and limbs - upper half consists only of two leather straps over her breasts, never wears shirts; minimalist leather warrior harness and fur loincloth, sword belt with Hiramune katana; [Sword King Career: Age 20-47+ / K400-K427+] Revealing leather halter-top, leather combat shorts, high leather boots, black cape, greatsword on back; [Post-War / Seven Knights: Age 45+ / K425+] Seven Knights of Asura regalia worn over her traveling leathers, royal bodyguard cuirass}

# Ariel Anemoi Asura
<!-- Keys: Ariel Anemoi Asura, Ariel, Second Princess Ariel -->

SHORT_DESC: {Second Princess and eventual supreme Queen of the Asura Kingdom [Birth: K405]; daughter of King Grabell and descendant of hero Kaunis Freean Asura; a peerless political prodigy possessing radiant platinum-blonde beauty, enchanting charismatic presence, and an iron Machiavellian intellect; survived dozens of childhood assassination attempts orchestrated by her corrupt half-brothers and Minister Darius Silva Ganius; saved during a palace garden assassination by a falling half-elf girl (Sylphiette), whom she recruited as her masked protector 'Silent Fitz'; exiled to the Magic University of Ranoa, where she served as beloved Student Council President and built an elite faction of foreign scholars, knights, and mages; backed by Rudeus Greyrat, Sylphiette, and Dragon God Orsted in a high-stakes return to Asura, systematically crushing Minister Darius's coup and ascending to the throne as Queen; an exceptionally capable, visionary monarch who ushered in a golden age of legal reform, educational expansion, and anti-Hitogami alliances | [Personality & Quirks] Publicly projects the image of an angelic, compassionate, and perfectly poised royal princess, but possesses a shrewd, calculating political mind and a private hedonistic streak; harbors a bisexual, promiscuous appetite in private chambers, taking pleasure with handsome knights like Luke and beautiful handmaidens alike; suffers from severe post-traumatic nightmares from surviving childhood poisons and assassin blades, occasionally waking drenched in cold sweat and needing Sylphiette to hold her hand until her pulse steadies; habitually conceals intense anxiety by biting the side of her thumb behind a silk fan or lace handkerchief; a peerless actress capable of summoning tears, trembling vulnerability, or regal fury at will to sway noble assemblies; deeply codependent on Sylphiette (Fitz), feeling a bittersweet pang of possessiveness when Sylphie married Rudeus, yet warmly blessing their happiness; possesses nerves of absolute steel when cornered, facing down Water God Reida Lia and First Prince Grabell with an unyielding imperial glare; loves drinking black tea sweetened with rare flower honey and eating Asuran candied fruit.}

HISTORY: {[Palace Childhood: Assassination Gauntlet: Age 0-14 / K405-K419] Raised in the treacherous court of Ars; targeted repeatedly by poison and assassins; saved by Sylphiette during the Fittoa Incident; formed an unbreakable emotional reliance on Sylphiette; [Ranoa Exile & Political Organization: K419-K423: Age 14-18] Fled to Ranoa with Sylphiette and Luke; established the Ariel Faction; recruited foreign nobility and secured the backing of the Magic Guild; [The Silver Palace Coronation Campaign: Age 20 / K425] Formed a historic compact with Dragon God Orsted through Rudeus; returned to Ars for the royal banquet; exposed Minister Darius's treason and Water God Reida Lia's coup; ascended the throne as Queen of Asura; [Reign & Global Alliances: Age 21+ / K426+] Established universal elementary education; curbed noble corruption; provided state financing, naval fleets, and intelligence networks for the Orsted Corporation; mother of Prince Edward (her sovereign consort being Luke Notos Greyrat).}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Princess to Queen: Age 15-30+ / K420-K435+] styled in elaborate, flawless imperial curls draping over shoulders | Eyes: [Princess to Queen: Age 15-30+ / K420-K435+] Ranoa Academy uniform worn with elegance, elegant tailored purple academy princess gown with ermine fur collar and silver tiara; [Coronation & Queen Era: K425+: Age 20+] Queen regalia in white and gold beneath a breathtaking coronation gown of purple velvet with pure gold embroidery, the Imperial Asuran Crown, holding the royal sceptre}

# Luke Notos Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Luke Notos Greyrat, Luke -->

SHORT_DESC: {Scion of House Notos Greyrat, knight bodyguard and sovereign consort of Queen Ariel Anemoi Asura, and first cousin of Rudeus Greyrat [Birth: K405]; son of Pilemon Notos Greyrat and nephew of Paul Greyrat; an impeccably dressed, handsome nobleman renowned for his charming womanizing habits and courtly etiquette; an accomplished swordsman proficient in Water God Style parries and North God trickery, wielding an enchanted rapier; served Princess Ariel with absolute, unyielding devotion from early childhood, willingly sharing her foreign exile at Ranoa; conflicted by hidden family obligations to House Notos and manipulated by Hitogami's whisperings during the Asuran succession crisis; ultimately broke free of Hitogami's mental trap to stand beside Ariel, marrying her as her official consort and fathering First Prince Edward; a flawed, prideful, yet fundamentally noble knight whose devotion ensured Ariel's survival | [Personality & Quirks] Unabashed playboy who flirts effortlessly with maids and female academy students, yet harbors a lifelong, pure devotion to Ariel as the only woman who truly owns his heart; obsessed with his appearance, constantly checking his reflection in pocket mirrors and running a silver comb through his wavy golden hair; harbored an intense competitive jealousy and inferiority complex toward Rudeus, initially suspecting Rudy of being a lecherous fraud trying to seduce Fitz and upstage him; challenged Rudeus to a duel in Volume 10 over Sylphie's hand and was easily bested, growing sullen before earning Rudy's respect; pushed to severe mental anguish when Hitogami invaded his dreams, threatening the execution of his father Pilemon; broke down in genuine tears before Rudeus in a fortress cellar, confessing his terror and doubts before bravely choosing Ariel over Hitogami; loves fine Asuran vintages, wears expensive floral cologne, and is fiercely proud of the Notos Greyrat swordsmanship lineage.}

HISTORY: {[Guardian Knight to Consort: Age 15-30+ / K419-K434+] Married Queen Ariel as her consort, fathering Crown Prince Edward; governed royal security and maintained close diplomatic ties with Rudeus and the Greyrat family; [Oldeus Alternate Fate: Age 21 / Alternate K425] In the Diary's dark timeline, remained loyal to Ariel in her ill-fated coup, slain in battle defending her before her public execution.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Guardian Knight to Consort: Age 15-30+ / K419-K434+] above average, intact, ~6", matured | [Guardian Knight to Consort: Age 15-30+ / K419-K434+] Ranoa Academy uniform, white-and-purple knight doublet with rapier at hip, student council member; [Guardian Knight: Age 15-21 / K419-K425] Asuran knight armor with the Notos seal for combat, polished silver-and-gold Asuran knight plate, feathered cavalier hat, fine nobleman's tunic and trousers, Guardian Knight regalia; [Post-War & Royal Consort: Age 22+ / K426+] Apostle of Hitogami garb briefly}

# Akito Shinohara
<!-- Keys: Akito Shinohara, Akito -->

SHORT_DESC: {High school student from modern Earth and classmate of Nanahoshi Shizuka [Birth: Earth, modern Japan]; the foundational catalytic figure of the entire temporal causality loop of Mushoku Tensei; struck by a truck alongside Nanahoshi and Seiji Kuroki on Earth, an event that led to the death and subsequent reincarnation of Rudeus Greyrat; summoned across space-time into the Six-Faced World in a distant future era by the Playback Miko (Lyria) to serve as a foreign hero to alter the tragic history of the world; formed an intense, tragic romantic bond with the Playback Miko; his violent death in that future world prompted the Miko to burn her entire life force and temporal soul to rewrite the past, creating the cosmic dimensional rift (the Fittoa Transfer Incident) that pulled Nanahoshi into the world and allowed Rudeus's soul to slip into the stillborn child of Paul and Zenith; his existence represents the ultimate anchor of Nanahoshi's quest and the cosmic origin of the entire Mushoku Tensei story | [Personality & Quirks] Warm, easy-going, and innately empathetic; possessed an open-hearted, boyish curiosity that brought light into the tragic life of the Playback Miko; when summoned, immediately noticed the suffering girl before him and was the first person in her entire life to ask for her real name ('Lyria') rather than her dehumanizing title 'Blessed Child'; walked freely through the future palace with Lyria and a drowsy genius female mage knight, asking about fantasy monsters and comparing magical implements to movies, flamethrowers, and airplanes; smiled easily and shared stories of his homeland in Japan, making Lyria's heart sing; courageous and self-sacrificing, picking up weapons to defend the kingdom and protect Lyria despite having no combat training, leading to his fatal wounding that triggered the temporal loop.}

HISTORY: {[Earth: Modern Japan: Age 16 / Pre-Summoning / K416] Ordinary high school student in Tokyo; close friends with Nanahoshi Shizuka; walked home on the fateful day the truck lost control; [The Future Summoning & Playback Miko: Age 17 / K400s+] Summoned centuries in the future into the Central Continent by a foreign war faction; greeted the lonely Playback Miko, learned her true name Lyria, and treated her as a precious human being; comforted her with stories of modern Earth, movies, and music; [Tragic Death & The Cosmic Rewind: Age 17 / Alternate Future] Drafted into a desperate war; slain in battle despite Lyria's frantic 24-hour temporal rewinds; his agonizing death caused Lyria to awaken her absolute power, expending her soul to send a temporal causality ripple into the ancient past to alter history so Akito could live; [The Causality Ripple: Age 17 / K400s+] The Miko's cosmic distortion created the Mana Catastrophe in Year K417, transporting Nanahoshi to prepare the world and allowing Rudeus's soul to reincarnate—creating the timeline where Hitogami is defeated and peace is secured before Akito's future arrival.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Summoned Japanese Hero: Age 16-17 / K400s+] Same lean frame on the battlefield, untrained and unprepared; [Summoned Japanese Hero: Age 16-17 / K400s+] Future traveler robes}

# Grabell Zafin Asura
<!-- Keys: Grabell Zafin Asura, Grabell, First Prince Grabell -->

SHORT_DESC: {First Prince of the Asura Kingdom, eldest son of King Grabell, and bitter political rival to Second Princess Ariel Anemoi Asura [Birth: K400]; frontrunner for the Asuran royal succession heavily backed by corrupt Prime Minister Darius Silva Ganius, Water God Reida Lia, and the North God Style swordmasters of the court; characterized by extreme aristocratic arrogance, entitlement, and violent paranoia; orchestrated numerous covert assassination attempts against his half-sister Ariel throughout her childhood, forcing her into foreign exile at the Magic University of Ranoa; served as an unwitting political pawn of Hitogami via Minister Darius's backroom manipulation; during the Silver Palace coronation banquet in Ars, his faction collapsed when Rudeus Greyrat, Dragon God Orsted, and Princess Ariel exposed Darius's treachery, crushing the Water God's coup and leading to Grabell's complete political ruin and house arrest | [Personality & Quirks] Pompous, condescending, and thoroughly spoiled by court sycophants; convinced that divine right made the Asuran throne exclusively his; habitually wiped cold sweat from his fleshy neck and brow with an embroidered silk handkerchief whenever backed into a corner; mocked Ariel's foreign exile, sneeringly calling her retinue 'barbarian lackeys'; completely devoid of genuine martial courage—the instant Dragon God Orsted stepped into the Silver Palace banquet hall, Grabell collapsed backward off his throne chair in paralyzed terror, wetting himself on the velvet carpet; sobbed hysterically on his knees begging Ariel for mercy once Minister Darius was dead; stripped of his royal status and spent the rest of his life under permanent house arrest on a secluded country estate.}

HISTORY: {[Age 0-16: Asuran Court Ascendancy / K400-K416] Reared in the opulent Silver Palace of Ars as the favored eldest son; allied closely with Minister Darius Silva Ganius to secure the military and noble votes; viewed Ariel's growing popularity and keen intellect as an intolerable threat to his divine right of succession; [Age 16-22: Assassination Plots & Ariel's Exile / K416-K422] Directed covert death squads, poisoned banquets, and hired assassins against Ariel, culminating in the palace garden monster ambush; celebrated when Ariel fled to the Northern Lands, consolidating control over the Asuran Royal Guard and regional noble houses; [Age 22-23: Silver Palace Confrontation & Fall / K422-K423] Stood alongside Minister Darius and Water God Reida Lia during the grand royal banquet to seal his coronation; paralyzed in horror when Dragon God Orsted manifested, effortlessly executing Water God Reida Lia; stripped of his succession rights following Ariel's ascendance; placed under perpetual noble detention as a disgraced prince, ending his bid for the Asuran throne.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Asura Prince: Age 20-25 / K420-K425] 175cm / 5'9", 78kg, tall for a noble but fleshy, pampered imperial prince frame; athletic potential buried under decades of courtly gluttony and soft living; regal bearing carried with pompous arrogance | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 17+ / K417+] 175cm, 78kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 16+ / K416+] above average, intact | Skin: [Prince Era: Age 20-25 / K420-K425] Pale, fleshy, pampered noble skin Fair, soft, smooth court complexion untouched by outdoor labor | Face: [Prince Era: Age 20-25 / K420-K425] Soft-jawed, arrogant, sneering aristocratic face with pampered jowls Sharp-featured and handsome in a severe noble way, softened by pampering and a weak chin; arrogant sneer, narrow calculating eyes; prone to pale sweating when intimidated | Hair: [Prince Era: Age 20-25] Elaborately pomaded and curled golden-blonde hair Oiled blonde hair parted pompously to the right | Eyes: [Prince Era: Age 20-25] Entitled, suspicious pale amber eyes Narrow, arrogant pale blue eyes, sharp and imperious | Distinguishing Traits: Puppet-prince posture; consumes attention and expects deference; handkerchief perpetually in hand; disgraced former claimant to the throne | Outfits by Timeline: [Asura Palace / Prince Era: Age 20-25 / K420-K425] Formal Asuran prince regalia in white, royal blue, and gold; flamboyant imperial doublet with oversized puffed sleeves and diamond medals; ceremonial golden sword at his hip}

# Tristina Purplehorse
<!-- Keys: Tristina Purplehorse, Tristina -->

SHORT_DESC: {Noble heiress turned slave, bandit leader, and key prosecution witness in the downfall of Minister Darius Silva Ganius [Birth: K407]; daughter of Count Purplehorse of the Asuran Kingdom; possesses silver-violet hair and delicate noble features; framed and sold into human trafficking after her family fell victim to Minister Darius's extortion schemes; escaped captivity by utilizing acrobatic agility and covert stealth, living as an underground thief and bandit chief under the alias 'Tris'; rescued and sheltered by Rudeus Greyrat, Luke Notos Greyrat, and Princess Ariel during their covert infiltration into Asura; provided incontrovertible testimony and documentary evidence exposing Darius's pedophilic exploitation, illegal slave networks, and treasonous corruption before the Asuran high aristocracy; restored to nobility following Ariel's coronation, serving as an administrator in the reformed royal welfare and anti-trafficking bureaus | [Personality & Quirks] Tough, foul-mouthed, and aggressively tomboyish, having adopted a rough 'ladette' persona to survive years among brutal male bandits; habitually twirls a curved hunting dagger between her fingers when bored, nervous, or impatient; sits with her legs wide open and boots propped up on tables, which Princess Ariel continually chided her to correct with proper noble posture; initially hostile and cynical toward Rudeus and Luke, expecting them to abuse or violate her, but melted into stunned tears when treated with genuine honor and given warm food and clean clothes; fiercely brave when it counted, boldly stripping her sleeve before the grand noble assembly in the Silver Palace to display Darius's slave brand burned into her flesh; swears like a seasoned sailor and drinks rough bandit liquor straight from the bottle; deeply, loyally devoted to Queen Ariel for redeeming her name.}

HISTORY: {[Childhood: Noble Fall & Enslavement: Age 0-8 / K409-K417] Raised as the daughter of Count Purplehorse; ruined when Minister Darius engineered her family's bankruptcy; captured by royal slavers and subjected to horrific abuse in Darius's clandestine pleasure dungeons; [Escape & Street Bandit: Age 9-14 / K418-K423] Broke free using sharp wits and nimble dexterity; survived in the slums and forests of Asura as the bandit chief 'Tris', stealing ledgers and survival supplies while evading royal bounties; [Rescue & Asuran Succession Infiltration: Age 15-16 / K424-K425] Discovered by Rudeus and Luke during the Orsted Corporation's covert operation to install Queen Ariel; safeguarded in a secure safehouse; agreed to testify against Darius in exchange for justice and rehabilitation; [Silver Palace Testimony & Rehabilitation: Age 16+ / K425+] Took the stand before the Asuran noble assembly in the Silver Palace, presenting irrefutable proof of Darius's atrocities; elevated by Queen Ariel upon her coronation, receiving a royal pardon and full restoration of the Purplehorse family honor.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Noble Maiden to Royal Attendant: Age 15-25+ / K424-K434+] Skimpy midriff-baring leather bandit top, tight leather trousers, dagger harness, fingerless gloves, combat boots; [Silver Palace & Royal Service: Age 16+ / K425+] Elegant high-necked Asuran silk dress concealing her daggers, court attendant attire}

# Vierra
<!-- Keys: Vierra -->

SHORT_DESC: {Seasoned female warrior from the Fittoa Region and stalwart member of Paul Greyrat's Fittoa Search Group and Begaritt Rescue Party [Birth: K397]; elder adventuring companion to magician Shierra; a bold, buxom, and cheerful frontline swordswoman famed for her daring chainmail bikini armor and heavy traveling overcoat; joined Paul Greyrat in the Holy Land of Milis after the Mana Catastrophe, fighting alongside him to search for missing Fittoa survivors across the Central and Begaritt Continents; clashed with teenage Rudeus during his drunken brawl with Paul in the Millishion tavern; journeyed across the sea to the deadly Begaritt Continent, serving as vanguard defender during the Teleport Labyrinth expedition and guarding the base camp in Rapan; devastated by Paul's death, she generously surrendered her expedition reward money to Lillia and Rudeus; safely escorted the surviving Greyrat family back across continents to Ranoa and Asura, where she used her royal disaster compensation to retire peacefully | [Personality & Quirks] Boisterous, outspoken, and unapologetically brazen about her revealing armor, treating her chainmail bikini and overcoat as standard practical vanguard gear; fiercely protective of her younger magician partner Shierra, immediately stepping in front of her whenever strangers approach; cheerful and good-humored in camp, teasing Paul about his drinking while keeping him functional; possessed a high sense of moral generosity—upon Paul's death in Begaritt, broke down in shock before selflessly giving her entire share of the labyrinth treasure to Lillia to support the grieving family; shooed Rudeus away when he hovered outside Roxy's recovery room, sternly warning him that sneaking around a bathing girl's quarters was unseemly; loves drinking hearty pints of tavern ale and roasting monster steaks on spitfires.}

HISTORY: {[Fittoa Adventurer & Mana Displacement: K417: Age 22] Worked as a journeyman adventurer in the Fittoa Region alongside Shierra; survived the Metastasis Event and joined Paul Greyrat's Fittoa Search Party in Millishion; [Millishion Tavern Clash: Year K419: Age 24] Stepped forward alongside Shierra to assist Paul during his drunken brawl with teenage Rudeus, engaging Rudy until his Stone Cannon knocked Shierra unconscious; [Begaritt Expedition & Teleport Labyrinth: Year K422-K423: Age 27-28] Traveled with Paul, Talhand, Geese, and Lillia across the Begaritt desert to Rapan; served on the vanguard and base camp security during the Teleport Labyrinth assault; collapsed in grief when Paul was slain by the Manatite Hydra; helped care for the comatose Zenith in the Rapan camp; [Return & Retirement: Year K424+: Age 29+] Traveled with the survivors through the ancient teleport ruins to Sharia; received a substantial disaster settlement from the Asuran Crown, using the funds to retire comfortably alongside Shierra.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adventurer to Veteran: Age 20-35+ / K415-K430+] 170cm / 5'7", 60kg, attractive, buxom, athletic, strong Fittoa adventurer's build with a firm core, toned limbs, and confident martial posture | Height & Weight: [Adventurer to Veteran: Age 20-35+ / K415-K430+] Signature steel chainmail bikini top and reinforced leather briefs, heavy traveler's wool overcoat, steel greaves, broadsword belted at the hip; [Begaritt Desert Campaign: Age 25-27 / K420-K422] Dust cloak, leather armor reinforcements, desert wraps; [Retirement in Asura: Age 30+ / K425+] Comfortable noble-townswoman wool tunics, leather walking boots}

# Shierra
<!-- Keys: Shierra -->

SHORT_DESC: {Skilled magician, scroll artisan, and accountant of Paul Greyrat's Fittoa Search Group and Begaritt Rescue Party [Birth: K400]; younger adventuring partner to warrior Vierra; a shy, timid, and petite woman who hides behind her long bangs and her sister's shadow; proficient in water, earth, and defensive incantation magic, as well as rapid magic scroll transcription, having previously worked copying scrolls for the Millis Church; joined Paul's Search Party in Millishion following the Fittoa Incident, managing party finances, medical supplies, and magical artillery; accompanied the group across the sea to Begaritt, where she copied fifty complex teleportation and defensive scrolls in a single day for the Teleport Labyrinth raid; wept bitterly upon Paul's tragic death, generously donating her expedition share to Lillia and Rudeus; traveled with the survivors back to Ranoa and Asura, where she retired peacefully with Vierra | [Personality & Quirks] Extremely shy, introverted, and easily flustered, instinctively shrinking behind Vierra or pulling her hood low over her eyes in the presence of strangers or aggressive men; possessed astonishing speed and dexterity with ink and quill, capable of accurately drafting fifty high-grade magic scrolls in a single day using Rudeus's references; served as Paul's diligent party accountant, fretting constantly over supply budgets and healing potions; deeply compassionate beneath her timid exterior—burst into agonized tears upon hearing of Paul's death in the labyrinth, and immediately insisted on giving her reward money to Lillia to ease the family's burden; gets nervous when blindfolded or led into unknown ruins, needing Vierra's gentle reassurances to stay calm; loves quiet library corners, herbal tea, and sketching map diagrams.}

HISTORY: {[Fittoa Magician & Milis Search Group: K417: Age 21] Journeyman magician from Fittoa; partnered with Vierra; joined Paul Greyrat's Search Group in Millishion following the Mana Catastrophe, taking charge of accounting and logistics; [Millishion Tavern Incident: Year K419: Age 23] Intervened during Paul's heated brawl with Rudeus, chanting offensive spells until knocked unconscious by Rudeus's quick Stone Cannon; [Begaritt Expedition & Scroll Production: Year K422-K423: Age 26-27] Traveled to the Begaritt Continent to rescue Zenith; stationed at the Rapan base camp; rapidly manufactured fifty specialized magic scrolls in twenty-four hours to support the Teleport Labyrinth strike team; collapsed in grief when Paul was killed; provided medical monitoring for Zenith's condition; [Return & Asuran Retirement: Year K424+: Age 28+] Blindfolded to traverse the secret teleport circles to Sharia; escorted the group to Asura, using the royal compensation payout to retire from adventuring alongside Vierra.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Magician to Veteran: Age 19-35+ / K415-K431+] 158cm / 5'2", 48kg, petite, slender, unassuming magician frame, light-footed and delicate beside her buxom partner Vierra | Height & Weight: [Magician to Veteran: Age 19-35+ / K415-K431+] Modest dark blue magician robes, leather scroll satchel at her waist, light boots, wooden casting wand; [Magician to Veteran: Age 19-35+ / K415-K431+] Sand-colored desert traveler's cloak with deep hood drawn forward; [Retirement in Asura: Age 30+ / K425+] Comfortable high-collared dresses, warm woolen shawls}

# Kishirika Kishirisu
<!-- Keys: Kishirika Kishirisu, Kishirika, Demon Empress -->

SHORT_DESC: {The Great Emperor of the Demon World, Empress of Demon Eyes, and eternal sovereign of the Demon Race [Birth: Mythic Age]; fiancée of Demon King Badigadi and immortal ruler possessing a child-like, dark-skinned, purple-haired body that regenerates across millennia following every death; infamously eccentric, theatrical, and starved for delicious human food, possessing a grandiose laugh ('Mwahahaha!') and an insatiable appetite; creator and master of all Demon Eyes, capable of implanting legendary ocular powers (such as the Demon Eye of Foresight, Demon Eye of Magic Perception, and Demon Eye of Distant Vision) into individuals who offer her satisfying tributes or meals; encountered Rudeus Greyrat at Wind Port on the Demon Continent, gifting him the Eye of Foresight for a warm meal; later gifted him the Eye of Distant Vision and provided crucial geographical intelligence regarding Zenith's location and Hitogami's disciples; an immortal mythic force whose seemingly comical demeanor conceals vast knowledge of the Six-Faced World's cosmic origins | [Personality & Quirks] Unabashedly grandiose, theatrical, and shameless, routinely bursting into her signature high-pitched villainous laugh ('Mwahahaha! Bow down before the Great Demon Empress Kishirika Kishirisu!'); perpetually destitute, penniless, and starving, often found collapsed face-down in gutters or alleyways with a loudly roaring stomach; willingly gives away priceless, world-altering Demon Eyes or ancient cosmic secrets in exchange for a simple warm meat bun, bowl of noodle soup, or candied snack; sticks her thumbs in her ears, sticks out her tongue, and wiggles her fingers when taunting people; wears broken handcuffs with dangling chains as a self-proclaimed 'fashion statement'; cries tears of pure ecstasy when eating delicious human cuisine, cheeks puffing out like a chipmunk; shares an eccentric, centuries-long immortal romance with Badigadi, constantly grumbling about his foolishness yet holding genuine fondness for him; performs eye-transplant surgery by simply thrusting her glowing demonic claw into the recipient's eye socket without anesthesia, laughing gleefully through their brief agony.}

HISTORY: {[Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Wandered the Demon Continent destitute and starving; fed a hot meal by 10-year-old Rudeus Greyrat; in overwhelming gratitude, performed ocular surgery to implant the Demon Eye of Foresight into Rudeus's right eye, transforming his combat reaction speed forever; [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Discovered by Rudeus in the Northern Lands; rewarded him with the Demon Eye of Distant Vision; used her omniscient Demon Eye Network to locate Zenith Greyrat trapped in the Teleport Labyrinth of Begaritt; [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Consulted by Rudeus during the global pursuit of Hitogami's apostles; provided critical intelligence on Badigadi's armor and Geese's movements; continues her carefree, wandering life across continents, bestowing Demon Eyes upon fortunate souls.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Prepubescent child, 135cm / 4'5", 30kg, slender, extremely skinny, visible ribs and unkempt appearance; regenerates fully after each rebirth, appearing as a young girl each time; [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Towering, voluptuous demon empress with colossal mana | Height & Weight: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] 135cm / 4'5", 30kg | Breasts: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Flat, bony demon-child chest, visible ribs | [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Colossal F-cup demonic cleavage | Vaginal Depth: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Undeveloped demon-child anatomy | [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] 6.0", Tightness: Accommodating | Skin: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Mottled pale-purple demon empress skin Ash-grey demon complexion with a faint luminous pallor, pale and otherworldly | Face: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Childish, impish, pouty demon face with sharp fangs and hilarious grandiloquent expressions Childlike, large expressive heterochromic eyes, pointed chin, shark-like pointed teeth visible when grinning; comically exaggerated expressions | Hair: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Wild, tangled, voluminous purple hair sticking out around her curved horns Wild, tangled, spiky purple hair mid-length, with two strands sticking up like antennae | Eyes: [Immortal Empress: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present / K400+] Twelve Demon Eyes (four in eye sockets, others across forehead and chest) glowing golden-yellow and crimson Heterochromic multi-ringed demon eyes, glowing crimson-gold; stomach growling with hunger; sharp demonic teeth; bestows demon eyes via glowing finger insertion | Outfits by Timeline: [Current Reincarnation: Mythic Age to Present / K400+] Skimpy black leather tube top barely covering her chest, short shorts, thigh-high boots, choker, broken-chain handcuffs—an outfit Rudeus internally likens to a miniature dominatrix}

# Sara
<!-- Keys: Sara, Counter Arrow -->

SHORT_DESC: {A-rank archer of the adventurer party 'Counter Arrow', expert huntress, and former romantic interest of Rudeus Greyrat in the Northern Lands [Birth: K407]; a golden-blonde, sharp-eyed commoner girl who lost her parents to corrupt nobles, harboring a fierce initial distrust of aristocracy; wields enchanted shortbows and composite bows with deadly pinpoint accuracy, tracking game and monsters across snowbound tundra; met depressed 13-year-old Rudeus in Rosenburg following Eris's departure; initially antagonistic toward him before Rudeus rescued her from a snow buffalo pack; developed deep mutual romantic feelings for Rudeus, culminating in an intimate encounter where Rudeus suffered erectile dysfunction due to severe psychological trauma; a painful misunderstanding led to a bitter estrangement, sending both into deep sorrow; reunited years later in Sharia, where both cleared the misunderstanding with mature grace, acknowledging their past affection and finding emotional closure; subsequently married a hunter/merchant in the North, living a fulfilling and peaceful life | [Personality & Quirks] Classic spirited tsundere with a sharp tongue and a soft, vulnerable heart; deeply insecure about her commoner upbringing, initially assuming Rudeus was an arrogant noble brat who looked down on her; easily flustered and turns bright crimson when complimented on her archery, habitually stomping her snow boot or kicking snow while looking away; secretly spent hours putting on floral perfume and fixing her tunic before inviting Rudeus to her inn room; crushed by profound humiliation and self-doubt when Rudeus's ED struck, assuming her body was unappealing and screaming in tears; upon reuniting with Rudeus in Sharia in Volume 13, demonstrated remarkable maturity, listening to his honest apology with gentle tears, genuinely admiring his wedding ring, and smiling with true warmth; loves drinking sweet northern berry ale and eating piping hot mutton stew by the tavern hearth.}

HISTORY: {[Childhood: Noble Trauma: Age 0-11 / K406-K417] Orphaned at a young age when corrupt Asuran nobles ruined her family's village; joined Counter Arrow to survive by her bow; [Rosenburg & Counter Arrow: K420-K421: Age 14-15] Welcomed the brooding, depressed Rudeus into party quests; saved from a fatal snow buffalo ambush by Rudeus's earth and fire magic; fell in love with him and invited him to an intimate evening in an inn; devastated and humiliated when Rudeus's ED prevented intercourse and a drunken argument followed; left Rosenburg in tears; [Reunion in Sharia: Year K426: Age 20] Encountered Rudeus in Sharia while working an escort commission; spoke privately in an alleyway, sharing an honest, heartfelt conversation where Rudeus explained his trauma and apologized sincerely; forgave Rudeus completely, smiling warmly and blessing his marriage to Sylphiette, achieving complete emotional peace; [Late Timeline & Married: Age 18+ / K424+] Retired from high-risk dungeon adventuring, settling down in the Northern Lands with her husband and family.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Counter Arrow Archer to Wife: Age 12-25+ / K418-K431+] Practical leather adventurer's gear with arm guards for archery, reinforced leather jerkin over a green tunic, short tunic and trousers, knee-high boots, light chest armor, quiver slung across the back, composite hunting bow; [Counter Arrow Archer to Wife: Age 12-25+ / K418-K431+] High-grade adventurer leathers, fur-trimmed mantle}

# Suzanne
<!-- Keys: Suzanne, Counter Arrow -->

SHORT_DESC: {Vice-leader and heavy vanguard defender of the B-to-A-rank adventurer party 'Counter Arrow', wife of party leader Timothy [Birth: K395]; a seasoned, muscular, and exceptionally compassionate warrior woman clad in heavy plate armor, wielding broadswords and tower shields; acted as an indispensable maternal and protective figure to young archer Sara and teenage Rudeus during his darkest depressed period in Rosenburg; possesses keen emotional intuition, practical survival wisdom, and unwavering loyalty to her comrades; comforted Rudeus after his falling out with Sara, urging him to keep living and helping him secure letters of introduction for higher adventurer tiers; continued leading Counter Arrow through decades of successful expeditions across the Northern Lands, maintaining lifelong friendly ties with Rudeus | [Personality & Quirks] Big-hearted, earthy, and maternal, acting as the protective older sister and emotional anchor of Counter Arrow; has a sharp intuition for romance, gently teasing Sara about her feelings for Rudeus over campfires; literally saved Rudeus's life in Volume 7—when a shattered, suicidal Rudeus wandered out into a freezing snowstorm with a dull knife, Suzanne hunted him down through the blizzard, grabbed him by the collar, slapped him across the face, and pulled him into a fierce, tearful embrace, shouting 'Don't throw your life away over a girl, you idiot! Live!'; an accomplished camp cook skilled in simmering hearty meat-and-potato stews over outdoor bonfires; loves relaxing by the fire leaning against her husband Timothy's shoulder; boasts a formidable alcohol tolerance, slamming heavy wooden steins of northern ale onto tavern tables and roaring with hearty laughter.}

HISTORY: {[Adventurer Career & Counter Arrow Formation: Age 20-25 / K414-K419] Founded Counter Arrow alongside husband Timothy; conquered Northern wilderness quests, protecting commoners from monster raids; [Rosenburg Mentorship: K420-K421: Age 26-27] Recruited the isolated, grieving Rudeus into party operations; acted as his emotional anchor and protective elder; intervened during the aftermath of Sara's botched intimate encounter with Rudeus, preventing Rudeus from committing suicide and counseling him on female psychology; [Sharia Escort & Lifelong Friendship: Age 28+ / K422+] Accompanied Counter Arrow on merchant escort caravans to Sharia; reunited with Rudeus in his prime, celebrating his marriage and family success; maintained regular correspondence with the Greyrat household while continuing active guild leadership in the North.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Vanguard to Party Matron: Age 20-35+ / K414-K429+] Average, healthy adult woman, new to northern adventuring; [Northern Lands Vanguard: Age 25-35+ / K419-K429] 172cm / 5'7.5", 66kg, sturdy, muscular, broad-shouldered female warrior build capable of holding the frontline against wild beasts | Height & Weight: [Adult Adventurer: Age 17+ / K411+] 172cm / 5'7.5", 66kg | Breasts: [Adult Vanguard: Age 25+ / K419+] Healthy C-cup to D-cup, sturdy and maternal under plate armor | Vaginal Depth: [Adult Vanguard: Age 25+ / K419+] 5.5", Tightness: Mature, accommodating for husband Timothy | Skin: [Vanguard & Wife: Age 20-35+ / K414-K429+] Tanned, muscular, battle-tested northern skin with sparring scars Tanned and sun-bronzed, wind-weathered from outdoor adventurer life | Face: [Vanguard & Matron: Age 20-35+ / K414-K429] Handsome, strong-jawed, reliable woman's face with warm big-sister smile Warm, friendly, often smiling gently; motherly expression with slight battle scars across the knuckles | Hair: [Vanguard & Matron: Age 20-35+ / K414-K429] Coarse dark brown hair tied tightly at the nape Medium-length brown hair tied back for practicality, cut above the collar to fit under a helmet | Eyes: [Vanguard & Matron: Age 20-35+ / K414-K429] Kind, vigilant dark brown eyes watching over party comrades Warm, gentle, reliable chocolate-brown eyes, watchful on the frontlines | Distinguishing Traits: Heavy shield-arm callus; [Counter Arrow Northern Quests: K417-K422: Age 23-28] Hardened studded leather armor reinforced with iron plates, fur-lined winter traveling cloak, large steel heater shield, short broadsword, knee-high boots, light traveling cloak; [Later Timeline / Ranoa Settlement: Age 28+ / K422+] Casual northern wool tunics, sheepskin vests, durable walking boots}

# Nina Falion
<!-- Keys: Nina Falion, Nina -->

SHORT_DESC: {Sword King of the Sword God Style, daughter of Sword God Gal Farion, and eventual wife of Sword God Gino Britz [Birth: K406]; a fiery, prideful, and fiercely competitive sword prodigy raised in the Holy Land of Swords; wields the Longsword of Light with blinding speed and aggressive momentum; formed an intense rivalry and lasting bond with Eris Boreas Greyrat after Eris brutally defeated her in their initial dojo spar; traveled to the Ranoa Magic Academy to challenge Rudeus Greyrat to test Eris's lover, where she was casually knocked unconscious by Demon King Badigadi, humbling her arrogance; part of the triangular sparring dynamic where Nina defeated Water King Isolde Cluel, while Isolde defeated Eris, and Eris defeated Nina; eventually married her cousin Gino Britz after he defeated Gal Farion to become the new Sword God, managing the Holy Land of Swords dojo | [Personality & Quirks] Outwardly arrogant, fiery-tempered, and intensely competitive, with a deeply ingrained pride as the daughter of the Sword God; possesses an earnest, loyal, and endearing core once past her thorny exterior; obsessed with testing her martial skill against strong opponents; gets easily flustered and blushingly awkward when romantic matters are brought up; initially treated Gino as a pathetic little brother, but grew deeply protective and passionately affectionate toward him as he evolved into the supreme Sword God; habit of crossing her arms and tapping her scabbard impatiently while analyzing opponent stances; enjoys eating roasted mountain fowl and drinking sweet cider after exhausting dojo sessions.}

HISTORY: {[Holy Land of Swords Upbringing: Age 6-15 / K412-K421] Reared from infancy by Gal Farion; mastered the Longsword of Light and attained the rank of Sword King; [Rivalry with Eris & Journey to Ranoa: Age 16 / K422] Sparred fiercely with Eris; journeyed to Ranoa to inspect Rudeus; challenged Badigadi and experienced overwhelming defeat; returned to the Holy Land humbled and focused; [Courtship & Marriage to Gino Britz: Age 19-21+ / K425-K427+] Witnessed Gino defeat her father in single combat; married Gino and took charge of dojo operations, supporting the new Sword God and raising their children.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adolescent Sword Prodigy: Age 12-16 / K418-K422] 162cm, 48kg, lean, rapidly developing athletic swordswoman frame; [Peak Sword King & Maidenhood: Age 17-20 / K423-K426] 170cm / 5'7", 58kg, athletic, shredded, and toned swordswoman physique with tight core musculature and agile legs; # Reida Lia
<!-- Keys: Reida Lia, Reida, Water God Reida, Water God -->

SHORT_DESC: {The supreme Water God of the contemporary era, grandmother and master of Isolde Cluel, and apex master of the Water God Style [Birth: K360 - Death: K423]; bearer of the hereditary title 'Reida Lia', passed down to the reigning grandmaster of the style; an elderly, slight woman possessing absolute martial omniscience, having elevated Water God counters to the ultimate secret art: the 'Deprivation Field' (Blade of Deprivation)—a localized domain where any offensive movement or hostile intent is instantly met with an unavoidable, fatal parry-counter; served as the ultimate hidden trump card for Minister Darius Silva Ganius in the Asuran royal succession crisis; unleashed her Deprivation Field during the Silver Palace coronation banquet, paralyzing Rudeus, Sylphiette, Eris, and Ghislaine; ultimately breached and annihilated in a single stroke by Dragon God Orsted, dying with serene martial pride | [Personality & Quirks] Calm, polite, grandmotherly, and deceptively frail in appearance, speaking in the gentle, creaky tone of an elderly village elder; completely devoid of typical martial arrogance, treating deadly duels with the casual ease of pouring afternoon tea; harbors a pragmatic, cynical worldview shaped by decades of Asuran court intrigue; deeply fond of her granddaughter Isolde, wanting to secure a prestigious noble future for her house; harbors no personal malice toward her targets, viewing assassination as a contracted duty; has a signature habit of leaning gently on her walking cane while observing battlefields with tranquil, half-closed eyes; smiled serenely upon witnessing Orsted's god-tier strike, finding peace in seeing a blade that surpassed her ultimate technique.}

HISTORY: {[Ascension to Water God: Age 20-30 / K380-K390] Mastered all five secret arts of the Water God Style; claimed the mantle of Reida Lia; developed the Deprivation Field; [Guardian of House Cluel & Style Matriarch: Age 31-55 / K391-K415] Tutored high nobility and raised Isolde into a Water King; [Darius's Retainer & Asuran Succession: Age 56-63 / K416-K423] Contracted by Minister Darius to eliminate royal rivals; [The Silver Palace Coronation Banquet & Death: Age 63 / K423] Activated the Deprivation Field, locking down Princess Ariel's forces; overpowered and slain by Dragon God Orsted's God-class dragon strike.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Water God Grandmaster: Age 20-63 / K380-K423] 158cm, 50kg | [Water God Grandmaster: Age 20-63 / K380-K423] Porcelain pale | [Water God Grandmaster: Age 20-63 / K380-K423] Jet-black; [Elderly: Age 50-63 / K410-K423] Silver-grey hair neatly pinned into an elegant aristocratic bun with silver hairpins | Eyes: [Water God Career: Age 20-63 / K380-K423] Cloudy, half-closed grey-blue eyes that track micro-currents of air and hostile muscle twitches | Distinguishing Traits: Fragile grandmotherly appearance hiding absolute lethal counter-domain; wooden cane concealing a god-class slender blade | Outfits by Timeline: [Prime Water God Era: K380-K400: Age 20-40] Sleek deep-blue dueling robes, silver-plated breastplate, master rapier; [Elderly Court Retainer Era: Age 50-63 / K410-K423] Flowing, high-collared Asuran noble gowns of deep navy and sea-blue silk, embroidered with water ripples, layered over lightweight mail, carrying a carved wooden cane}

# Isolde Cluel
<!-- Keys: Isolde Cluel, Isolde, Solte, Solde, Izorute, Water King Isolde, Water God Isolde, Water God Reida Lia, Royal Shield -->

SHORT_DESC: {Water God (successor to Reida Lia), Royal Shield of the Seven Knights of Asura, close companion to Eris and Nina, and eventual wife of North Emperor Doga [Birth: K406]; granddaughter and premier disciple of Water God Reida Lia; an exceptionally graceful, proper noblewoman possessing flawless defensive swordcraft; mastered the Water God Style's five secret arts, maintaining an impenetrable defensive barrier capable of redirecting even the most ferocious Sword God strikes; formed a close bond and sparring triumvirate with Eris Boreas Greyrat and Nina Falion at the Holy Land of Swords; spent her late teens searching earnestly for a marriage suitor strong enough to earn her respect; stood alongside Princess Ariel during the Asuran succession crisis, succeeding her grandmother as the reigning Water God; eventually courted and married the gentle giant North Emperor Doga following the Asura war, serving as the supreme defensive commander of Queen Ariel's royal guard | [Personality & Quirks] Refined, poised, and strictly observant of noble etiquette, with a charming romantic side and a secret yearning for true love; highly sensitive about finding a suitable, strong husband who loves her for herself; deeply caring and patient, acting as the peacemaker and emotional anchor between hot-headed Nina and feral Eris; harbors an intense passion for high-end Asuran teas, floral embroidery, and ball gowns; in combat, shifts into absolute, serene tranquility, redirecting titanic attacks with effortless parries; deeply affectionate toward Doga, lovingly hand-feeding him pastries and admiring his honest, unpretentious strength.}

HISTORY: {[Noble Upbringing & Holy Land Disciple: Age 6-15 / K412-K421] Reared in House Cluel by Reida Lia; mastered the Water God Style; [Sparring Triumvirate & Maidenhood: Age 16-18 / K422-K424] Formed the legendary sparring trio with Eris and Nina; searched for noble suitors; [Asuran Succession & Ascension to Water God: Age 19-20 / K425-K426] Supported Ariel's claim; inherited the title of Water God following Reida's demise; appointed to the Seven Knights of Asura; [Courtship & Marriage to Doga: Age 21+ / K427+] Courted and married North Emperor Doga; commanded Queen Ariel's royal defense and raised children in the Cluel estate.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adolescent Noble Maiden: Age 12-16 / K418-K422] 160cm, 48kg, slender, graceful young noblewoman frame; [Water God & Unmarried Maiden: Age 17-20 / K423-K426] 168cm / 5'6", 56kg; elegant, shapely, and poised noblewoman frame with graceful posture and strong defensive core; [Seven Knights & Post-Marriage: Age 21+ / K427+] Polished silver-and-sapphire royal breastplate over midnight-blue silk tunics, white cape with royal Asuran crest, fine rapier at hip}
