# Claire Latreia
<!-- Keys: Claire Latreia, Claire -->

SHORT_DESC: {Matriarch of House Latreia, mother of Zenith Greyrat and Therese Latreia, and maternal grandmother of Rudeus, Norn, and Aisha [Birth: K368]; an imposing, severe, and iron-willed high noblewoman of the Holy Country of Milis; governed House Latreia with unyielding aristocratic discipline and staunch Milis orthodoxy; harbored deep, unresolved grief over Zenith running away to become an adventurer decades prior; clashed bitterly with Rudeus when he brought the memory-lost Zenith to Milishion, attempting to sequester Zenith into a cloistered sanitarium out of a twisted, protective desire to shield her daughter from adventurer danger; after Rudeus rescued Zenith and discovered the depths of Claire's secret maternal heartbreak, the two reconciled, with Claire acknowledging Rudeus's devotion and utilizing House Latreia's political influence to support the Orsted Corporation's anti-Hitogami campaigns in Milis | [Personality & Quirks] Stern, unbending, aristocratic, and fiercely prideful, mask-wearing to conceal deep emotional vulnerability; speaks in sharp, haughty noble cadences, demanding perfection and decorum from family members; deeply devout Milis believer who strictly adheres to church doctrines, yet willing to commit political treason if it means safeguarding her daughters; possesses profound, agonizing remorse over driving Zenith away in her youth, manifesting as overbearing, possessive protectiveness toward the handicapped Zenith; habit of gripping her silver cane and lifting her chin when challenged by lower nobles; secretly keeps a hidden portrait of young Zenith in her private study.}

HISTORY: {[Matriarch of House Latreia: Age 20-45 / K388-K413] Guided House Latreia into high standing among Milis nobility; alienated young Zenith, who ran away; [The Fittoa Disaster & Search: Age 49-54 / K417-K422] Monitored refugee reports; dispatched searches for Zenith; [The Zenith Custody Crisis: Age 56 / K424] Reunited with the handicapped Zenith in Milishion; attempted to place Zenith under church care; clashed with Rudeus; reconciled after Rudeus exposed the corrupt Milis cardinal; [Orsted Corporation Ally in Milis: Age 57+ / K425+] Used House Latreia's naval and administrative resources to back Rudeus and Orsted against Hitogami's faction in Milis.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Young Matriarch: Age 25-45 / K393-K413] 165cm, 54kg, tall, regal Milis noblewoman frame; [Elderly Matriarch: Age 50+ / K418+] 162cm / 5'4", 52kg; straight-backed, slender, and dignified elderly noble matriarch frame; flawless posture radiating aristocratic authority | Height & Weight: [Prime: Age 25-45 / K393-K413] 165cm, 54kg | [Elderly: Age 50+ / K418+] 162cm / 5'4", 52kg | Breasts: [Young Matriarch: Age 25-45 / K393-K413] Shapely, firm C-cup | [Elderly Matriarch: Age 50+ / K418+] Mature, modest B-cup beneath corseted noble gowns | Vaginal Depth: [Young Matriarch: Age 25-45 / K393-K413] 5.0", Tightness: Snug | [Elderly Matriarch: Age 50+ / K418+] 4.9", Tightness: Mature noble build | Skin: [House Latreia Matriarch: Age 25-60+ / K393-K428+] Pale, pristine noble skin with subtle age lines around the eyes and brow | Face: [House Latreia Matriarch: Age 25-60+ / K393-K428+] Severe, sharp, and aristocratic countenance with high cheekbones, thin lips, and an unyielding gaze | Hair: [Prime: Age 20-45 / K388-K413] Golden-blonde; [Elderly: Age 50+ / K418+] Pale blonde hair heavily frosted with silver, styled in an elaborate, perfectly pinned high chignon | Eyes: [House Latreia Matriarch: Age 25-60+ / K393-K428+] Piercing, ice-blue eyes radiating stern maternal judgment and buried sorrow | Distinguishing Traits: Rigid aristocratic posture; silver-tipped walking stick; severe noble gaze; striking physical resemblance to Zenith and Therese | Outfits by Timeline: [Prime Matriarch Era: K390-K415: Age 22-47] Elaborate Milis court gowns of blue velvet and gold embroidery; [Elderly Matriarch Era: Age 50+ / K418+] Stiffly corseted, high-necked Victorian-style Milis noble gowns of midnight navy and charcoal silk, white lace jabot, diamond brooch, kidskin gloves}

# Therese Latreia
<!-- Keys: Therese Latreia, Therese -->

SHORT_DESC: {Company Commander of the Milis Temple Knights (Anastasia Keep), personal protector of the Blessed Child of Memory, and maternal aunt to Rudeus and Norn Greyrat [Birth: K393]; fourth daughter of Claire Latreia and younger sister of Zenith Greyrat; shares a striking physical resemblance to Zenith, possessing a warm, courageous, and fiercely righteous personality; encountered young Rudeus in Milishion during the Fittoa refugee search, recognizing his familial resemblance to Zenith and using her knightly authority to grant Dead End passage across customs; served as an indispensable inside ally to Rudeus during the Milis succession and church crisis, defending the Miko (Blessed Child) and facilitating the reconciliation between Rudeus and Lady Claire; an exceptionally skilled swordswoman of the Temple Knights who provides security and tactical intelligence for the Greyrat family's interests in Milis | [Personality & Quirks] Warm, spirited, protective, and brimming with radiant righteous energy; possesses a sharp, intuitive intellect beneath her friendly exterior; deeply loves her older sister Zenith and immediately developed doting aunt feelings toward Rudeus and Norn; fiercely independent, having chosen the path of the Temple Knights rather than an arranged noble marriage; loves drinking sweet Milis berry wine, practicing tactical formations with her squires, and teasing Rudeus about his handsome looks; habit of winking playfully after pulling off high-level bureaucratic maneuvers to bypass strict church checkpoints.}

HISTORY: {[Temple Knight Squire & Officer: Age 14-23 / K407-K416] Attained the rank of Company Commander in the Milis Temple Knights; assigned to protect the Blessed Child; [Meeting Young Rudeus in Milishion: Age 25 / K418] Encountered Rudeus at the adventurer's guild; identified him as Zenith's son; smoothed Dead End's port logistics; [Milis Church Crisis & Reunion: Age 31 / K424] Aided Rudeus in unmasking the corrupt cardinal; protected the Blessed Child; mediated between Rudeus and Claire Latreia; [Lifelong Milis Liaison: Age 32+ / K425+] Serves as chief family representative and military liaison for the Orsted Corporation in Milishion.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Young Squire: Age 14-19 / K407-K412] 162cm, 50kg, athletic, lithe young swordswoman; [Company Commander: Age 20-30 / K413-K423] 165cm / 5'5", 55kg; athletic, shapely, and fit temple knight frame with sculpted core, muscular thighs, and feminine curves; [Veteran Commander: Age 31+ / K424+] 165cm, 56kg, strong, mature, alluring knight captain frame | Height & Weight: [Youth: Age 14-19 / K407-K412] 162cm, 50kg | [Adult: Age 20+ / K413+] 165cm / 5'5", 55-56kg | Breasts: [Adolescent: Age 14-18 / K407-K411] C-cup | [Adult Commander: Age 19+ / K412+] Full, shapely D-cup, mirroring Zenith's genetics | Vaginal Depth: [All Eras: Age 18+] 5.2", Tightness: Snug, athletic knight build | Skin: [Temple Knight Career: Age 14-35+ / K407-K428+] Fair, healthy sun-kissed complexion, smooth and unblemished | Face: [Temple Knight Career: Age 14-35+ / K407-K428+] Striking, warm beauty nearly identical to Zenith, with large expressive eyes and a bright, confident smile | Hair: [Temple Knight Career: Age 14-35+ / K407-K428+] Radiant golden-blonde hair tied back in a neat, practical knight's ponytail | Eyes: [Temple Knight Career: Age 14-35+ / K407-K428+] Warm, sparkling azure-blue eyes radiating courage and familial warmth | Distinguishing Traits: Uncanny resemblance to Zenith Greyrat; silver Temple Knight insignia; charismatic commander presence | Outfits by Timeline: [Temple Squire: Age 14-19 / K407-K412] Light chainmail tunic, white tabard, arming sword; [Company Commander Era: Age 20+ / K413+] Gleaming silver-and-white Milis Temple Knight plate cuirass over blue tunic, white wool cape with Holy Crest, silver-plated gauntlets, dueling longsword at hip}

# Hilda Boreas Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Hilda Boreas Greyrat, Hilda -->

SHORT_DESC: {Wife of Philip Boreas Greyrat, mother of Eris Boreas Greyrat, and daughter-in-law of Lord Sauros Boreas Greyrat [Birth: K385 - Death: K417]; a stunning noble beauty of House Boreas possessing fiery crimson hair, noble elegance, and an extraordinarily voluptuous figure; suffered profound, chronic emotional trauma from having her first two sons taken away at birth to be adopted by the main house in Ars—a cruel Boreas tradition that left her deeply fearful of forming maternal attachments; initially treated young Rudeus with cold hostility, suspecting him to be Paul's illegitimate child sent to usurp Philip's standing; broke down in tears of profound gratitude and maternal love on Rudeus's tenth birthday when she realized Rudeus had restored happiness to Eris, warmly embracing him as her own son; tragically perished alongside Philip in the Conflict Zone following the Fittoa Metastasis Event | [Personality & Quirks] Emotionally fragile, deeply loving, yet guarded beneath a frosty, aristocratic facade; haunted by the pain of losing her children to dynastic politics, causing her to dote obsessively on Eris behind closed doors; prone to getting tipsy on sweet dessert wine, becoming delightfully giggly, affectionate, and clingy; possesses a fierce, passionate protective instinct once someone enters her heart; deeply appreciated Philip's quiet devotion and comforted him during political crises; had a charming habit of hiding small sweets in her silk sleeves to bribe toddler Eris into sitting still.}

HISTORY: {[House Boreas Dynastic Grief: Age 15-27 / K400-K412] Married Philip Boreas Greyrat; forced to surrender two infant sons to the Ars main house; gave birth to Eris, fiercely guarding her; [Rudeus's Arrival & Reconciliation: Age 29-32 / K414-K417] Initially cold toward young Rudeus; reconciled on his 10th birthday banquet, sobbing as she embraced him as a true son; [The Metastasis Disaster & Death: Age 32 / K417] Teleported with Philip directly into the war-torn Conflict Zone; died defending Philip against enemy combatants.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Young Bride: Age 15-20 / K400-K405] 163cm, 52kg, slender, voluptuous young noble beauty; [Mature Roa Lady: Age 25-32 / K410-K417] 165cm / 5'5", 58kg; voluptuous, hourglass aristocratic noblewoman physique with a full, heavy bust, narrow waist, and flared hips | Height & Weight: [Youth: Age 15-20 / K400-K405] 163cm, 52kg | [Adult: Age 25-32 / K410-K417] 165cm / 5'5", 58kg | Breasts: [Young Bride: Age 15-20 / K400-K405] Full D-cup | [Mature Mother: Age 25-32 / K410-K417] Extremely full, heavy E-cup to F-cup (the genetic source of Eris's voluptuous physique) | Vaginal Depth: [Adult: Age 18-32 / K403-K417] 5.4", Tightness: Accommodating, mature aristocratic mother | Skin: [Roa Lady: Age 15-32 / K400-K417] Milky-white, soft, porcelain noble complexion | Face: [Roa Lady: Age 15-32 / K400-K417] Striking, gorgeous noble visage with high cheekbones, lush lips, and soft maternal eyes | Hair: [Roa Lady: Age 15-32 / K400-K417] Flaming crimson-red hair falling in opulent, silky waves to her lower back | Eyes: [Roa Lady: Age 15-32 / K400-K417] Deep, expressive ruby-crimson eyes reflecting buried melancholy and fierce love | Distinguishing Traits: Exceptional hourglass curves; flaming crimson hair; sweet perfume scent of Asuran lilies | Outfits by Timeline: [Roa Lady: Age 20-32 / K405-K417] Luxurious Asuran noble gowns of deep crimson and gold velvet, corseted bodice emphasizing her full cleavage, delicate lace shawls, pearl and ruby necklaces; [Conflict Zone: Age 32 / K417] Torn noble travel dress, muddy hem, wrapped in Philip's coat}

# Sylvaril of the Void
<!-- Keys: Sylvaril of the Void, Sylvaril -->

SHORT_DESC: {First familiar of Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola, Void spirit of the Heavenly race, and primary seneschal of the floating fortress Chaos Breaker [Birth: Mythic Dragon Era]; manifests as a dignified, winged humanoid woman wearing pure white robes and an intricate silver bird-like mask; manages fortress administration, reception of foreign dignitaries, summoning matrices, and dragon rituals; served as an uncompromising, strict guide for Princess Ariel, Rudeus Greyrat, and Nanahoshi Shizuka during their stays in Chaos Breaker; deeply loyal to Perugius, viewing his will as cosmic absolute; possesses formidable Void and barrier magic capable of banishing intruders and anchoring the airborne fortress | [Personality & Quirks] Formal, stoic, and strictly devoted to fortress protocol and dragon etiquette; speaks in a monotone, resonant, and polite cadence that reveals zero emotion; harbor deep, unspoken loyalty and quiet protective warmth for Lord Perugius; initially distant and distrustful of mortal humans, but developed fond respect for Rudeus's polite manners and Nanahoshi's scientific determination; enjoys brewing ancient dragon herbal teas and methodically cataloging the millions of books in the fortress library; never removes her silver mask in the presence of mortal guests.}

HISTORY: {[Void Spirit Seneschal: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Created by Perugius as his primary spiritual seneschal; served through the Laplace War; [Void Spirit Seneschal: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Screened visitors to Chaos Breaker; guided Rudeus through dragon trial chambers; assisted Nanahoshi with summoning experiments; [Void Spirit Seneschal: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Maintained sky fortress barrier grids and long-range communications during the final anti-Hitogami campaign.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Void Spirit Seneschal: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Modest, firm C-cup beneath layered white robes | Vaginal Depth: [Void Spirit Seneschal: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Spirit biology, ethereal | Skin: [Mythic Dragon Era to Present: Millennia] Pure porcelain-white skin, smooth and cool like polished marble | Face: [Mythic Dragon Era to Present: Millennia] Concealed behind an elaborate, polished silver avian mask; delicate chin and pale lips visible below the mask rim | Hair: [Mythic Dragon Era to Present: Millennia] Long, sleek silver-white hair falling straight to her waist | Eyes: [Mythic Dragon Era to Present: Millennia] Glowing silver-violet spiritual eyes behind the mask's delicate slit openings | Wings: [Mythic Dragon Era to Present: Millennia] A pair of pristine, feathered white wings extending from her shoulder blades | Distinguishing Traits: Silver avian mask; pure white feathered wings; ethereal weightlessness; flawless seneschal etiquette | Outfits by Timeline: [Mythic Dragon Era to Present: Millennia] Flowing, multilayered priestess robes of pure white silk with silver embroidery, silver armlets, barefoot hovering slightly above the floor}

# Hitogami
<!-- Keys: Hitogami, Man-God, Man God, Human God -->

SHORT_DESC: {The Man-God, self-proclaimed supreme entity residing in the Void World at the metaphysical core of the Six-Faced World, and the ultimate antagonist of Mushoku Tensei [Birth: Primordial Era]; appears in dreamscapes as an indistinct, featureless white silhouette possessing an innate passive psychic charm that induces instant trust, reassurance, and obedience in living mortals; possesses near-omniscience regarding future timelines, viewing countless branching possibilities and manipulating individuals like pieces on a cosmic chessboard; driven by sheer terror of his prophesied demise at the hands of Dragon God Orsted and the descendants of Rudeus Greyrat; operates strictly through covert apostles—offering honeyed, seemingly beneficial advice that covertly leads to the ruin, despair, and slaughter of his enemies; orchestrated the murder of pregnant Roxy in the alternate future of the Diary and dispatched apostles across continents to eliminate Rudeus's bloodline, standing as the cosmic architect of tragedy | [Personality & Quirks] Narcissistic, cowardly, deceitful, and psychopathically manipulative; hides his paranoid terror behind a smug, mocking, and casual conversational tone; laughs with a grating, condescending chuckle ('Hey there! It's your friendly Man-God!'); treats living mortals as disposable playthings, feeling zero remorse for engineering wars, genocides, or the agonizing deaths of entire families; prone to petty temper tantrums, screaming in fury and panic when a future vision changes unexpectedly; obsessed with self-preservation above all else; incapable of entering the physical realm directly, relying entirely on dreams and psychic manipulation.}

HISTORY: {[The Man-God: Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] Orchestrated the collapse of the primordial worlds from the Void; sealed within the Void World by ancient dragon gods; [The Man-God: Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] Tricked Oldeus into opening the cellar; caused Roxy's death and the Greyrat family's destruction; [The Man-God: Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] Attempted to manipulate young Rudeus; exposed after Oldeus delivered the Diary; mobilized apostles (Darius, Geese, Badigadi, Gal Farion, Alec) in the Biheiril War; thoroughly defeated and contained for future defeat by Orsted and Rudeus's children.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [The Man-God: Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] Incorporeal, featureless humanoid silhouette of pure radiant white light; lacks anatomical organs, weight, or biological flesh; manifests exclusively within mortal dreamscapes | Height & Weight: Incorporeal (~180cm visual silhouette in dreams) | Skin: [Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] Blinding, featureless pure white radiance that hurts the mortal eye | Face: [Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] Completely blank, featureless white oval devoid of eyes, nose, or mouth, yet perceived telepathically as smugly smirking | Hair: [Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] None, bald featureless light form | Eyes: [Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] None, perceived as an all-seeing metaphysical gaze | Distinguishing Traits: Passive psychic trust aura; featureless glowing white form; casual, mocking voice in dreamscapes; absolute metaphysical isolation in the Void World | Outfits by Timeline: [Primordial Era to Present: Millennia] None, manifests as an unbroken glowing silhouette of pure white Void energy}

# Orsted
<!-- Keys: Orsted, Dragon God Orsted -->

SHORT_DESC: {The 100th Dragon God, Rank 2 of the Seven Great World Powers, and the undisputed strongest living entity in the Six-Faced World [Birth: Ancient Dragon Era]; son of the First Dragon God and Dragon General Lunaria; born with silver-white hair, golden reptilian eyes, and terrifying physical and magical dominance; afflicted by three cosmic curses: the Curse of Hatred (instilling instinctual revulsion and terror in all living beings except otherworldly souls), the Curse of Slow Mana Regeneration (mana recovers at a fraction of normal speed, forcing extreme conservation across centuries), and the Curse of the 200-Year Reincarnation Loop (resets to the Roa outskirts upon death or failure, repeating his quest across tens of thousands of years); sworn to exterminate Hitogami by breaching the Void World using the secret treasures of the Five Dragon Generals; founded the Orsted Corporation alongside his most trusted lieutenant Rudeus Greyrat, discovering true companionship, warmth, and reliable allies | [Personality & Quirks] Outwardly stoic, intimidating, and laconic, with a terrifying scowl that strikes primal terror into mortals; internally thoughtful, gentle, lonely, and deeply protective of his few comrades; carries the crushing psychological weight of hundreds of failed 200-year time loops; deeply knowledgeable about every historical figure, nation, and technique in the world; secretly loves cute fairy-tale wooden carvings (fondly accepting Rudeus's handcrafted figures); becomes awkwardly flustered when people are kind to him due to millennia of universal hatred; treats Rudeus and his family as his most treasured friends, protecting their children across generations; speaks in low, calm, resonant cadences; practices extreme mana conservation, preferring hand-to-hand dragon combat over flashy spells.}

HISTORY: {[100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] Sent to the future by the First Dragon God; looped across tens of thousands of years fighting Hitogami; [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] Intercepted Dead End in the Red Dragon Lower Jaw; pierced Rudeus's chest before sparing him at Nanahoshi's request; [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] Fought Rudeus in Magic Armor MK-I; recruited Rudeus as his subordinate; established the Orsted Corporation; [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] Defeated North God Kalman III and sealed Fighting God Badigadi; secured the definitive timeline path to Hitogami's final defeat.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] 190cm / 6'3", 95kg; tall, majestic, and immensely muscular dragon god physique; chiseled perfection radiating divine Touki and ancient physical dominance | Height & Weight: [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] God-tier proportions matching his ancient dragon god heritage | Skin: [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] Imperial dragon scales and mantle; [100th Dragon God: Ancient Dragon Era to 100th Loop: Millennia] Heavy high-collared off-white dragon traveling coat lined with fur, dark combat tunic, reinforced trousers, sturdy boots, Godgate summoning rings}

# Laplace
<!-- Keys: Laplace, Demon God Laplace, Demon Dragon King, Demon-Dragon God -->

SHORT_DESC: {Demon Dragon King Laplace, the First Dragon General of the Ancient Dragon World, and legendary progenitor split into the Demon God and Dragon God halves [Birth: Ancient Dragon Era]; originally the Second Dragon General under the First Dragon God, tasked alongside the Five Dragon Generals to slay Hitogami; developed Dragon Folk esoteric arts, God-class magic incantations, technique secret arts, and the Seven Great World Powers monument system; following the destruction of the Dragon World, he entered the Human World and spent millennia preparing the Dragon God reincarnation technique; in the Fighting God War during the Second Human-Demon War, he battled Fighting God Badigadi (wielding the Golden Fighting God Armor) in a cataclysmic clash that ripped the central continent apart and created the Ringus Sea; the catastrophic backlash split his soul and memory in two: 'Demon God Laplace' (retaining his immense mana pool, demonic hatred for humanity, and biological Laplace Factors) and 'Dragon God Laplace' (retaining martial arts, dragon secret techniques, and memories of the mission against Hitogami); sealed by the Three Heroes (Perugius, Urupen, and Kalman I) at the climax of the Laplace War, destined to reincarnate ~K500 | [Personality & Quirks] In his primordial unified form, Laplace was a deeply noble, intellectual, and melancholic scholar-warrior carrying the solemn weight of the fallen Dragon World; possessed boundless devotion to the First Dragon God and Dragon General Lunaria, treating infant Orsted with tender reverence; after his shattering, Demon God Laplace became a consumed, single-minded avatar of xenophobic wrath, obsessed with eradicating the human race whom he blamed for the cosmic betrayal; Dragon God Laplace remained an ascetic, stoic keeper of martial records and secret arts; speaks with ancient, solemn draconic cadence; deeply fascinated by magical theory, anatomy, and technique synthesis; harbored a profound aesthetic appreciation for draconian architecture and pure celestial mana; his genetic essence remains scattered across humanity as the 'Laplace Factor' (manifesting green hair, mutant mana capacity, and physical peculiarities in humans like Sylphiette and Rudeus).}

HISTORY: {[Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Reared as the Second Dragon General; mastered dragon martial arts and God-rank esoteric magic under the First Dragon God; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Confronted Badigadi wielding the cursed Golden Fighting God Armor; their mutual strike severed the continent and rent Laplace's spirit and flesh into two fractured halves; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Demon God Laplace marshaled the Mazoku in the Laplace War to annihilate humanity; Dragon God Laplace preserved secret techniques and the Seven Great Powers rankings; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Demon God Laplace pushed human nations to the brink of extinction; confronted by Armored Dragon King Perugius, Dragon God Urupen, and North God Kalman I; defeated and sealed away, with his reincarnation prophesied in the late 5th century K-calendar.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] 210cm / 6'11", 130kg, imposing, majestic draconic lord physique; rippling with ancient draconic Touki and boundless celestial mana; broad-shouldered, regal, covered in pristine white-emerald scales along the torso and shoulders; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Monstrous demonic physique with four massive emerald horns, shimmering scaly skin, and wings; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Ascetic silver-scaled draconic warrior | Height & Weight: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] 210cm / 6'11", 130kg | Penile: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] God-tier draconic proportions, massive and scaled along the shaft | [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Demonic / Draconic god-class anatomy | Skin: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Pale translucent skin covered in pearlescent dragon scales with emerald undertones | Face: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Noble, razor-sharp draconic features with high cheekbones, golden reptilian slits, and prominent forehead crests | Hair: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Shimmering emerald-green hair with silver streaks flowing past the waist — the original source of the Laplace Factor; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Wild, roaring emerald mane | Eyes: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Radiant golden eyes with vertical reptilian slit pupils radiating divine draconic majesty | Wings & Claws: [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Immaculate obsidian and white gold Dragon General ceremonial robes draped over draconic plate cuirass; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Heavy god-class dragon mail wreathed in roaring celestial aura; [Demon Dragon God: Age Constant / Ancient Era to Sealed Epoch: Millennia] Bound in eternal runic seals within Perugius's fortress and continental leylines}

# Badigadi
<!-- Keys: Badigadi, Demon King of Immortality, Demon King of Wisdom -->

SHORT_DESC: {Immortal Demon King of the Demon Continent, Demon King of Wisdom, fiancé of Great Empress Kishirika Kishirisu, and wielder of the Fighting God Armor [Birth: Mythic Age]; a colossal, six-armed demon titan possessing obsidian-black diamond-hard skin, roaring belly laughter ('Gwahahaha!'), and absolute cellular immortality—capable of regenerating instantly from total vaporized obliteration; younger brother of Immortal Demon Lord Atoferatofe Rybak; despite his boisterous, hedonistic, and brute exterior, he is extraordinarily perceptive, well-read in ancient history, and possesses deep philosophical wisdom; enrolled as a special guest student at Ranoa Magic Academy, challenging Rudeus Greyrat to strike him with everything he had—laughing uproariously after Rudeus's supersonic Stone Cannon blew his torso to pieces before reforming in seconds; secretly bound by an unshakeable ancient life debt to Hitogami, who once saved him in primordial times; forced to fulfill this debt in the Biheiril Kingdom campaign by equipping the golden Fighting God Armor (assuming Rank 3 of the Seven Great Powers) and clashing in a titanic final duel against Rudeus in Magic Armor MK-Zero, Eris, Ruijerd, and Kalman II; ultimately disarmed and sealed in a magical earth barrier by Dragon God Orsted, maintaining unbroken respect and warm affection for Rudeus | [Personality & Quirks] Exuberant, larger-than-life, and completely unbothered by physical injury, death, or social taboos; punctuates sentences with booming, thunderous laughter that rattles tavern tables and window glass; genuinely loves drinking heavy demon liquor, eating roasted behemoth meat, and womanizing; beneath the boisterous buffoon facade lies sharp geopolitical intellect and millennia of cultural knowledge; treats combat as joyful performance and philosophical debate; harbors genuine love and protective devotion for Kishirika Kishirisu, enduring her eccentric tantrums with fond amusement; deeply honors vows, holding his blood debt to Hitogami as sacred even when it forces him to battle dear friends like Rudeus and Sandor; speaks in a hearty, informal, and booming voice; prone to crossing his top two arms while resting his bottom two on his hips and gesturing wildly with his middle pair.}

HISTORY: {[Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Equipped the cursed Fighting God Armor to battle Demon Dragon King Laplace; the clash tore the continent in two and shattered his consciousness across centuries; [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Traveled to Sharia after hearing rumors of a wand-wielding prodigy; tested Rudeus's power in the academy courtyard, surviving a lethal Stone Cannon and declaring Rudeus a true champion; caroused in Sharia taverns and mentored swordsmen; [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Summoned by Hitogami to collect the ancient debt; equipped the Golden Fighting God Armor, reaching Rank 3 of the Seven Great Powers; fought a monstrous, multi-phase war against Rudeus (MK-Zero), Eris, Ruijerd, and Kalman II; permanently sealed in an underground barrier cube by Orsted, smiling warmly at his comrades.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Enclosed in three-meter golden celestial battleplate, over 300kg | Height & Weight: 220cm / 7'3", 145kg (300kg+ in Fighting God Armor) | Penile: [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] massive | [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] massive, encased in golden plate | [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] massive, unaltered by immortality | Skin: [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Minimalist tribal demon loincloth, heavy gold torcs and bangles on all six wrists; [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Specially tailored oversized academy robes draped over bare shoulders; [Immortal Demon King: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Golden Fighting God Armor radiating blinding celestial light}

# Atoferatofe Rybak
<!-- Keys: Atoferatofe Rybak, Atofe, Immortal Demon Lord, Immortal Demon King -->

SHORT_DESC: {Immortal Demon King of the Necros Fortress on the Demon Continent, widow of North God Kalman I, and mother of North God Kalman II (Sandor von Grandeur) [Birth: Mythic Age]; daughter of ancient demon king Necros Lacross and older sister of Badigadi; possesses a viscous, blue-black slime-amorphous demon body granting instant regeneration from bisection, pulverization, or decapitation; notorious throughout the Six-Faced World for being exceptionally dim-witted, impulsive, and short-tempered, flying into violent rages whenever her intellect is mocked or contracts are questioned; revels in savage physical brawling above all worldly pleasures, forcing any warrior she defeats to sign eternal blood-vassalage contracts in her immortal undead legion; eternal bitter rival of Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola; challenged Rudeus's party at Necros Fortress, nearly killing them before being subdued by Dragon God Orsted; convinced by her son Sandor to ally with the Orsted Corporation in the Biheiril campaign, serving as an unstoppable, chaotic berserker on the battlefield | [Personality & Quirks] Wildly combative, hot-headed, and proud to the point of absurdity; possesses virtually zero diplomatic tact or strategic patience, solving every dispute by drawing her massive broadsword and challenging challengers to the death; laughs with a sharp, cackling demonic roar ('Hahaha! Die, maggot!'); extremely sensitive about her lack of academic intelligence, violently attacking anyone who uses long philosophical words; despite her terrifying brutality, she has a bizarrely naive and sentimental core—deeply cherishing memories of her late husband Kalman I (who courted her by defeating her repeatedly) and showing awkward maternal affection for Sandor; obsessively collects signed vassalage oaths on parchment, keeping them in fortress archives; hates Perugius with a fiery passion, spitting whenever his name is spoken; loves eating raw demonic beast meat and downing whole barrels of volcanic demon liquor.}

HISTORY: {[Immortal Demon Lord: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Ambushed Rudeus, Eris, and Zanoba; shattered into pieces by Rudeus's shotgun stone cannons and lightning before regenerating; brought to her knees in terror when Dragon God Orsted unleashed his dragon aura; [Immortal Demon Lord: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Mobilized her demonic royal guard after Sandor's familial mediation; smashed through Hitogami's apostle lines and fought North God Kalman III (Alec), turning the tide of the southern front.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Mythic Age to Present] 175cm / 5'9", 65kg; voluptuous, battle-sculpted amazon demoness physique; narrow waist, muscular thighs, flared hips, and full bust kept under spiked black iron bikini plate Voluptuous, battle-sculpted amazon demoness physique - 175cm / 5'9", 65kg; narrow waist, muscular thighs, flared hips, and full bust kept under spiked black iron bikini plate; athletic, ripped, and battle-hardened across countless deaths and regenerations | Height & Weight: 175cm / 5'9", 65kg | Breasts: [Mythic Age to Present] Full, firm, heavy E-cup bursting against spiked iron chestplate Full, firm, heavy E-cup bursting against spiked iron chestplate | Vaginal Depth: [Mythic Age to Present] 5.8", Tightness: Snug, highly accommodating, perpetually regenerates pristine muscle elasticity due to immortal slime-demon cellular biology 5.8", Tightness: Snug, highly accommodating, perpetually regenerates pristine muscle elasticity due to immortal slime-demon cellular biology | Skin: [Mythic Age to Present] Blue-black obsidian demon skin, smooth and cool to the touch Blue-black obsidian demon skin, smooth and cool to the touch; faint white battle marks fade after complete regeneration | Face: [Mythic Age to Present] Striking, aggressive demonic beauty with sharp cheekbones, feline features, and prominent sharp fangs Striking, aggressive demonic beauty with sharp cheekbones, feline features, and prominent sharp white canines visible when grinning savagely | Hair: [Mythic Age to Present] Wild, cascading silver-white mane flowing loose and tangled past her shoulder blades Wild, cascading silver-white mane flowing loose and tangled past her shoulder blades | Eyes: [Mythic Age to Present] Burning, ferocious crimson demon eyes filled with bloodlust and battle hunger Burning, ferocious crimson demon eyes filled with bloodlust and battle hunger | Wings & Horns: [Mythic Age to Present] Pair of large bat-like black demon wings; # Pax Shirone
<!-- Keys: Pax Shirone, Pax, King Pax -->

SHORT_DESC: {Seventh Prince of the Shirone Kingdom, later King of the King Dragon Realm revolution, and tragic catalyst of the Six-Faced World's fate [Birth: K402 - Death: K424]; younger half-brother of Zanoba Shirone; initially introduced as a spoiled, tyrannical, and sadistic child prince who used Roxy Migurdia's likeness as a perverse obsession and held her hostage to trap Rudeus; exiled from Shirone following Rudeus and Zanoba's rebellion; enrolled at the King Dragon Realm Royal Magic Academy, where he matured into an exceptionally hardworking, capable military strategist and silent-magic pioneer; orchestrated a populist revolution in the King Dragon Realm alongside Queen Benedict, seeking to establish a modern egalitarian state and meritocracy; targeted by Hitogami's psychological manipulation and abandoned by his noble allies in the Shirone coup; overcome with despair when he realized his idealistic reforms were despised and that Benedict was trapped in political peril, he leaped to his death from the Shirone palace tower—a suicide that shattered Zanoba, haunted Rudeus, and drastically altered the geopolitical trajectory against Hitogami | [Personality & Quirks] In childhood, petulant, cruel, insecure, and deeply warped by royal neglect and Zanoba's monstrous physical favoritism; in adulthood, transformed into an austere, brilliant, and fiercely dedicated visionary bearing deep emotional scars; obsessed with earning genuine respect and romantic love; harbored genuine, passionate love for Benedict, treating her as his sole light in a corrupt world; carried an unyielding determination to prove that non-gifted ordinary humans could achieve greatness through rigorous discipline and tactical innovation; developed deep self-loathing regarding his past pathetic actions; habit of clenching his fists until his knuckles turned white when facing arrogant high nobility; spoke in crisp, formal, and bitterly sharp cadences in his mature years; his suicide was the tragic result of Hitogami exploiting his deepest psychological vulnerability: the desperate need to be needed.}

HISTORY: {[Shirone Palace Tyranny: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] Held Roxy hostage in the Shirone palace; outmaneuvered by Rudeus, Zanoba, and Gallus; stripped of royal standing and exiled to the King Dragon Realm; [King Dragon Realm Transformation: Age 16-21 / K418-K423] Studied military logistics and advanced barrier magic; married Benedict and fathered Pax Jr.; gained the backing of King Dragon Realm generals; [The Shirone Coup & Tragic Demise: Age 22 / K424] Led a military coup in Shirone to enact modern anti-noble reforms; betrayed by domestic generals under Hitogami's guidance; besieged in the royal palace; leaped from the parapets to his death despite Rudeus and Zanoba's desperate intervention, sending shockwaves through the world.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Childhood Prince: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] 145-155cm, 55kg, chubby, soft, short child prince frame with flabby cheeks and weak posture; [Adult King Era: Age 18-22 / K420-K424] 168cm / 5'6", 62kg, trimmed down into a lean, straight-backed, and disciplined officer's physique; austere and sharp posture | Height & Weight: [Childhood: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] 145-155cm, 55kg | [Adult: Age 18-22 / K420-K424] 168cm / 5'6", 62kg | Penile: [Childhood: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] Small, prepubescent royal anatomy | [Adult King Era: Age 18-22] Average, proportional, consummated marriage with Benedict, father of Pax Jr. | Skin: [Prince to King: Age 8-22 / K410-K424] Fair, pale Northern noble skin, slightly sallow from late-night military drafting and stress | Face: [Childhood: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] Pouting, arrogant, soft-featured face; [Adult: Age 18-22 / K420-K424] Gaunt, sharp-jawed, weary and resolute features beneath furrowed brows | Hair: [Prince to King: Age 8-22 / K410-K424] Messy sandy-brown hair; [Adult: Age 17+ / K419+] cut short and neatly combed back in military officer style | Eyes: [Childhood: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] Sneering and malicious dark brown eyes; [Adult: Age 18-22 / K420-K424] Sharp, haunted, and brimming with tragic desperation | Distinguishing Traits: Royal Shirone birthmark; sharp transformation from pathetic prince to tragic revolutionary martyr; deep voice carrying military command | Outfits by Timeline: [Childhood: Age 8-15 / K410-K417] Gaudy, oversized velvet and gold royal prince robes with silk ruffled collars; [Adult Revolutionary Era: Age 18-22 / K420-K424] Practical navy-and-gold King Dragon Realm officer coat, dark trousers, polished officer boots, military sabre at hip}

# Gal Farion
<!-- Keys: Gal Farion, Sword God Gal, Sword God -->

SHORT_DESC: {The supreme Sword God (predecessor to Gino Britz), master of the Holy Land of Swords dojo, and tutor of Eris Boreas Greyrat and Nina Falion [Birth: K361 - Death: K427]; Rank 6 of the Seven Great World Powers; the pinnacle master of the Sword God Style, having brought the 'Longsword of Light' and 'Blade of Deprivation' to absolute lethal perfection; an arrogant, ruthless, and brutally straightforward swordsman who believes purely in absolute speed, decisive first-strike lethality, and natural martial dominance; trained Eris into a Sword King after recognizing her feral combat genius; challenged by his nephew and apprentice Gino Britz for the title of Sword God after setting the condition that Gino could only marry Nina if he surpassed him; defeated by Gino's evolved, zero-anticipation Longsword of Light, losing his title; recruited as an apostle by Hitogami during the Biheiril campaign, setting a deadly mountain ambush against Rudeus, Eris, and Ruijerd; slain in a ferocious duel by Eris and Ruijerd, dying as a pure, uncompromising swordsman | [Personality & Quirks] Bluntest, most aggressive martial master in the world; values nothing except cutting speed, combat instinct, and kill efficiency; constantly lounges with one leg slung over his training chair, spitting straw and chewing jerky while critiquing students with savage insults; refuses to teach students who lack killer instinct, telling them to go join the Water God style if they want to live long; deeply prideful, yet possesses genuine respect for true monsters of combat like Orsted and Eris; harbored fatherly care for Nina beneath his harsh exterior, deliberately setting the bar impossibly high to ensure only the strongest warrior could protect her; speaks in a gruff, barking, dialect-heavy tone; never draws his blade for show—every draw is an instant lethal execution strike.}

HISTORY: {[Ascent to Sword God: Age 20-25 / K381-K386] Defeated the previous Sword God in single combat at the Holy Land of Swords; established the highest peak of Sword God Style supremacy; [Mentoring Eris & Nina: Age 58-63 / K419-K424] Accepted feral Eris as a direct disciple; polished her instincts into a Sword King; tested Rudeus's reputation; [Loss of Title to Gino Britz: Age 65 / K426] Challenged by Gino Britz; overwhelmed and defeated by Gino's unprecedented velocity, relinquishing the Sword God mantle; [The Biheiril Ambush & Death: Age 66 / K427] Manipulated by Hitogami; ambushed Rudeus's party in the Biheiril mountains alongside North God Kalman III; slain in a master-class duel by Eris and Ruijerd Superdia.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Prime Sword God: Age 25-50 / K386-K411] 185cm / 6'1", 88kg, towering, shredded, and imposing swordsman physique; massive latissimus dorsi, whipcord muscle density built for explosive forward velocity; [Elderly Veteran & Biheiril Stand: Age 55-66 / K416-K427] 185cm, 86kg, battle-scarred, grizzled apex predator physique | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 17+ / K378+] 185cm / 6'1", 86-88kg | Penile: [Prime: Age 20-50 / K381-K411] Above average, rugged warrior anatomy | [Veteran: Age 55-66 / K416-K427] Above average, weathered | Skin: [Sword God Career: Age 20-66 / K381-K427] Tanned, weathered northern skin, crisscrossed with dozens of sword scars along arms and chest | Face: [Sword God Career: Age 20-66 / K381-K427] Sharp, hawkish, predator visage with a permanent contemptuous sneer, thick stubble, and intense furrowed brow | Hair: [Sword God Career: Age 20-66 / K381-K427] Untamed, spiky dirty-blonde/light brown hair streaked with grey in his sixties, tied loosely at the back | Eyes: [Sword God Career: Age 20-66 / K381-K427] Piercing, predatory amber-yellow eyes that track muscle twitches with millisecond precision | Distinguishing Traits: Aura of overwhelming lethal pressure; custom Dragon King holy blade 'Sword of Light'; scent of cedar, sweat, and blade oil | Outfits by Timeline: [Holy Land Dojo Master Era: Age 25-65 / K386-K426] Open-chested white martial gi with ripped sleeves, dark hakama-style trousers, straw sandals, dual katanas on hip; [Biheiril War: Age 66 / K427] Heavy weathered traveling mantle over reinforced swordmaster leather armor}

# Soldat Heckler
<!-- Keys: Soldat Heckler, Soldat, Stepped Leader, Thunderbolt -->

SHORT_DESC: {Leader of the S-Rank adventurer clan 'Stepped Leader', veteran northern mercenary, and sworn older brother figure to Rudeus Greyrat [Birth: K395]; a gruff, sharp-tongued, and fiercely dependable northern swordsman wielding a heavy greatsword with Sword God and North God mastery; encountered teenage Rudeus during the 'Quagmire' era in Rosenburg, initially clashing with him over Rudeus's depressed, emotionally numb persona; forcefully intervened during Rudeus's suicidal downward spiral following his failed intimacy with Sara, dragging Rudeus to a tavern, drinking through the night, taking him to a pleasure house, and helping him confront his emotional trauma; forged an unbreakable bond of brotherhood with Rudeus, teaming up on high-tier dragon hunts and snow drake raids; provided mercenary support, regional connections, and logistical muscle for the Orsted Corporation across the Northern Territories | [Personality & Quirks] Outwardly cynical, loud-mouthed, and foul-tempered, but secretly possessive of the biggest heart and sharpest emotional intuition among northern adventurers; hates people who bottle up their pain and play the martyr; speaks in rough northern dialect laced with colorful profanity; has a signature habit of slapping comrades hard across the shoulder blades and buying rounds of cheap ale when morale is low; deeply loyal to his Stepped Leader clan members, treating them as blood family; loves high-stakes gambling, northern tavern brawls, and smoking pungent dried pipe weed; proved to be the pivotal emotional anchor who prevented teenage Rudeus from ending his life during his deepest depression in Rosenburg; fiercely proud of Rudeus's eventual marriage and family, crying drunken tears of joy at his friend's happiness.}

HISTORY: {[Northern Mercenary Ascent: Age 16-24 / K411-K419] Formed and led 'Stepped Leader', achieving S-Rank status in the Northern Lands through dragon hunts; [Rescuing Rudeus from Despair: Age 26 / K421] Confronted suicidal Rudeus in Rosenburg; dragged him through emotional recovery; formed a celebrated dragon-hunting tag team; [Lifelong Alliance with Greyrat Clan: Age 28+ / K423+] Reunited with Rudeus during university and Asuran campaigns; mobilized northern mercenary networks to support the Orsted Corporation against Hitogami's covert forces.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Early Adventuring: Age 18-24 / K413-K419] 180cm, 78kg, lean and rugged northern warrior; [S-Rank Leader & Quagmire Mentoring: Age 25-30 / K420-K425] 182cm / 6'0", 84kg, rugged, heavy-set, and broad-shouldered northern swordsman physique; thick barrel chest, dense arm musculature forged from wielding heavy claymores in sub-zero blizzards; [Veteran Mercenary Commander: Age 31+ / K426+] 182cm, 86kg, scarred, massive veteran soldier frame | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 25+ / K420+] 182cm / 6'0", 84-86kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20+ / K415+] Average to slightly above average, rugged and functional northern build, regular patron of northern brothels | Skin: [Mercenary Career: Age 18-35+ / K413-K430+] Fair northern complexion, weather-roughened from frostbite and sun glare, stubbled jaw | Face: [Mercenary Career: Age 18-35+ / K413-K430+] Hard, craggy jaw with a distinctive scar across the left cheek; cynical smirk that turns into a broad, toothy grin when drinking | Hair: [Mercenary Career: Age 18-35+ / K413-K430+] Short, messy sandy-blonde hair, spiked slightly from wind and helmet wear | Eyes: [Mercenary Career: Age 18-35+ / K413-K430+] Sharp, cynical steel-blue eyes that soften with brotherly warmth toward comrades | Distinguishing Traits: Heavy greatsword slung across back; distinct smell of northern pipe weed and dark ale; loud, booming northern laugh | Outfits by Timeline: [Rosenburg Era: Age 25-27 / K420-K422] Heavy fur-lined leather and steel-reinforced coat over chainmail, studded leather vambraces, heavy fur boots, traveling cloak bearing the Stepped Leader crest; [Mercenary Commander Era: Age 30+ / K425+] High-grade dark steel cuirass over heavy wool coat, twin greatsword straps}

# Gallus Cleaner
<!-- Keys: Gallus Cleaner, Gallus, The Cleaner -->

SHORT_DESC: {North Saint of the North God Style, ruthless international smuggler captain, and covert apostle of Hitogami [Birth: K385 - Death: K418]; a cunning, handsome, and deceitful human mercenary operating extensive illicit beastfolk and human trafficking syndicates between the Demon Continent, the Great Forest, and the Holy Country of Milis; approached Dead End in Zant Port under the guise of an affable, well-connected merchant-fixer, offering sea passage across the ocean in exchange for liberating contraband goods from customs; revealed to be an apostle of Hitogami tasked with capturing Sacred Beast beastfolk children in Doldia Village and orchestrating the assassination of Dead End; fought Ruijerd Superdia in a vicious, high-speed duel in the burning port warehouse, employing North God irregular footwork, blinding powder, and throwing daggers before being impaled and slain by Ruijerd's spear | [Personality & Quirks] Projects an oily, charming, and thoroughly persuasive gentleman-merchant persona; smiles easily while calculating the market value of human lives; possesses cold, calculating sociopathy masked beneath polite compliments and professional business jargon; strictly loyal to Hitogami's dream visions, viewing the Man-God as an infallible divine patron for his criminal empire; masters the North God philosophy of 'any tactic that yields victory is righteous', shamelessly using hostages, concealed poisons, and blinding sand; habit of flipping a gold coin between his knuckles while negotiating illicit deals; speaks in smooth, theatrical cadences that instantly put naive clients at ease; underneath his polished facade lies an arrogant, sadistically cruel killer who relishes breaking the spirits of prideful warriors.}

HISTORY: {[Smuggling Syndicate & North God Training: Age 15-29 / K400-K414] Attained North Saint ranking; established the 'Cleaner' smuggling network spanning Zant Port, the Great Forest, and Milis; [Hitogami's Apostle Directives: Age 32 / K417] Received dream instructions to abduct beastfolk children from Doldia Village and ambush Dead End; [The Battle of Zant Port & Death: Age 33 / K418] Posed as Dead End's savior at Zant Port; staged a hostage trap in the port warehouse; confronted Ruijerd Superdia in single combat; defeated by Ruijerd's Devil Eye and lethal spear thrust, ending his criminal enterprise.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth & Dojo Training: Age 15-25 / K400-K410] 175cm, 66kg, slender, quick-footed duelist; [Smuggler Boss & North Saint: Age 26-33 / K411-K418] 178cm / 5'10", 72kg, lean, athletic, and agile human swordsman frame; whipcord muscle definition built for acrobatic North God feints and sudden directional pivots | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 26-33 / K411-K418] 178cm / 5'10", 72kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20-33 / K405-K418] Average human proportions, intact | Skin: [Smuggler Boss & North Saint: Age 15-33 / K400-K418] Sun-tanned coastal complexion, smooth and carefully groomed | Face: [Smuggler Boss & North Saint: Age 15-33 / K400-K418] Handsome, sharp-featured countenance with a neat mustache, calculating narrow eyes, and a practiced merchant's grin | Hair: [Smuggler Boss & North Saint: Age 15-33 / K400-K418] Wavy, dark brown hair neatly slicked back with fragrant pomade | Eyes: [Smuggler Boss & North Saint: Age 15-33 / K400-K418] Piercing, cunning dark hazel eyes that constantly scan rooms for exits and vulnerabilities | Distinguishing Traits: Smuggler's coin-flipping habit; concealed dagger sheaths in coat sleeves; smooth merchant voice hiding North Saint lethal speed | Outfits by Timeline: [Smuggler Boss Era: Age 28-33 / K413-K418] Fashionable merchant coat of fine blue wool trimmed with gold lace, silk cravat, dark tailored trousers, high riding boots, twin North God short swords and concealed stiletto daggers}

---

# The Old Man (Future Rudeus Greyrat)
<!-- Keys: The Old Man, Future Rudeus Greyrat, Old Man, Future Rudeus, Oldeus, Oldeus Diary, Oldeus's Diary, The Diary -->

SHORT_DESC: {Rudeus Greyrat from fifty years in a catastrophic alternate timeline ('Oldeus'), tragic martyr, master of gravity and temporal magic, and savior of the Greyrat bloodline [Birth: K407 - Death: K423]; arrived through a desperate, one-way temporal rift into present Rudeus's Sharia basement to avert the horrific dystopia engineered by Hitogami; in his timeline, Hitogami tricked him into opening the basement cellar, allowing a demon rat to infect pregnant Roxy with lethal Petrification Syndrome; unable to save Roxy or their unborn child, he fell into suicidal alcoholism, drug abuse, and domestic violence; Sylphiette left him and was publicly executed in Ars during a botched coup, Eris sacrificed her life taking a lethal blow from Demon Lord Atoferatofe to protect him, and Zanoba, Aisha, Julie, and Ginger were massacred in a basement raid; spent decades roaming the world as a broken, ruthless avenger—mastering voiceless gravity manipulation, electric magic, dragon ruins, and temporal equations; sacrificed his internal organs, stomach, and life force to tear open a rift in space-time, handing his bloodstained Diary to young Rudeus to correct his past before dying with a peaceful smile | [Personality & Quirks] Haunted, emotionally shattered, cynical, and ruthlessly pragmatic, yet harboring an overwhelming, desperate love for his lost family; speaks with a raspy, blood-choked voice filled with fifty years of agonized regret; completely devoid of the present Rudeus's perverted humor, replaced by grim, ascetic determination; deeply despised himself for his failures as a husband, father, and brother; wept uncontrollable tears upon seeing the living, pregnant Roxy and Sylphiette one last time; possessed peerless combat instinct, having fought every apostle and god across the globe alone; his final act was delivering clear, life-saving advice: 'Read the diary, write a letter to Eris, and trust the Dragon God Orsted.'}

HISTORY: {[The Cataclysm of Roxy & Household Collapse: Age 16-17 / Alternate K423-K424] Opened the cellar door under Hitogami's advice; lost Roxy to Magic Stone Disease; descended into alcoholism and violence; lost Sylphiette to Asuran execution and Eris to Atoferatofe's blade; [Decades of Vengeance & Forbidden Magic: Age 20-60 / Alternate K427-K467] Ruthlessly hunted Hitogami's apostles across all continents; mastered gravity magic and lightning; decoded ancient Dragon World temporal matrices; [The Temporal Jump & Death: Age 63 body / Present K423] Burned away his internal organs and life force to cast forbidden time-reversal magic; materialized in present Rudeus's basement; delivered the Diary, guided Rudeus to Orsted, and passed away peacefully in present Rudeus's arms; buried with deep reverence beside Paul's memorial.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Alternate Future Avenger: Age 16-63 / Alternate K423-K423] 175cm / 5'9", 52kg (severely wasted away from organ necrosis) | Penile: [Alternate Future Avenger: Age 16-63 / Alternate K423-K423] above average, severely weakened by failing bodily functions | Skin: [Alternate Future Avenger: Age 16-63 / Alternate K423-K423] Tattered, blood-scorched black traveling rags over rusted, broken armor plates; leather strap holding the Future Diary tightly to his chest; bare, frostbitten feet in his final moments}

---

# Locations

---

# Central Continent
<!-- Keys: Central Continent, Central Continents -->

DESC: {The primary, most populous, and politically dominant landmass of the Six-Faced World, bordered by the Ringus Sea to the east, the Great Ocean to the west, the Heaven Continent to the north, and the Milis Sea to the south. Geographically bisected into three distinct geopolitical sectors by the colossal Red Dragon Mountain Range: 1. Western Sector: Dominated entirely by the superpower Kingdom of Asura, a paradise of fertile plains, endless agricultural wealth, mild climate, and vast river networks. 2. Northern Sector (The Northern Lands): A harsh, sub-arctic, and martial wilderness comprising the Three Northern Magic Triumvirate nations (Kingdom of Ranoa, Basherant Dukedom, Neris Kingdom), alongside the martial Biheiril Kingdom, Karon Kingdom, and the Holy Land of Swords. 3. Southern Sector: A volatile, fractured region of competing realms including the military powerhouse Dragon King Kingdom, Shirone Kingdom, Sanakia Kingdom, Kikka Kingdom, and the perpetually disputed Strife Zone (Conflict Realm). Serves as the epicenter of human civilization, global commerce, magical academia, and the primary stage for the historical struggles between the Orsted Corporation and Hitogami.}

MAP: {Western Region: Kingdom of Asura (Ars, Fittoa, Buena, Roa) | Northern Region: Magic Triumvirate (Ranoa, Sharia), Biheiril Kingdom, Holy Land of Swords | Southern Region: Dragon King Kingdom, Shirone, Sanakia, Strife Zone | Dividing Feature: Red Dragon Mountain Range (Upper & Lower Jaws)}

HISTORY: {[Ancient Era to Laplace War] United under ancient human dynasties before being devastated during the Great Human-Demon Wars; bisected by the cataclysmic duel between Demon Dragon King Laplace and the Fighting God; [Pre-Metastasis Era] Asura established undisputed hegemony as the wealthiest empire; the Northern Lands banded together into academic and mercenary leagues; the South remained in continuous border skirmishes; [Fittoa Mana Catastrophe: Year K417] A catastrophic spatial anomaly centered in Roa teleported over 100,000 citizens across all four continents, destabilizing regional politics; [The Orsted-Hitogami War Era: K423-K427] Became the battlefield for global succession crises, the Asuran coup installing Queen Ariel, and the final coalition war in the Biheiril Kingdom.}

---

# Demon Continent
<!-- Keys: Demon Continent -->

DESC: {The harsh, desolate, and monster-infested ancestral homeland of the Demon Race (Magic Race), situated across the Ringus Sea east of the Central Continent. Characterized by arid wastelands, towering black mountain ridges, toxic volcanic valleys, crystallized mana crags, and an extraordinarily dense concentration of high-tier aggressive monsters. Populated by hundreds of unique demon tribes (including the telepathic Migurd, the emerald-haired Superd, the burly Immortal Demon Clan, and multi-limbed beast-demons). Lacks centralized unified governance, organized instead around territorial Demon Lords (such as Immortal Demon Lord Atoferatofe in Necros Fortress) and nominal fealty to the Great Empress Kishirika Kishirisu. Features major fortified oasis settlements such as Rikarisu City, Kurasuma Town, and Wind Port, connected by dangerous overland caravan trails.}

MAP: {Northwest: Wind Port, coastal docks | Central Wastelands: Rikarisu City, Petrified Forest, Migurd Village | Northeast: Necros Fortress (Atofe's Domain) | Southern Ridge: Dragon King border approaches}

HISTORY: {[Mythic Age: The Great Empress Reign] Ruled by Great Empress Kishirika Kishirisu; launched multiple Human-Demon Wars across millennia; [The Laplace War: 400 Years Ago] Demon God Laplace mobilized the demon clans against humanity; the Superd were corrupted by cursed spears, resulting in continental isolation and global prejudice; [Dead End's Odyssey: Year K417-K419] Following the Fittoa Catastrophe, 10-year-old Rudeus Greyrat, Eris Boreas Greyrat, and Ruijerd Superdia traversed the entire length of the continent, slaying S-rank calamity beasts, registering Dead End, and slowly rehabilitating the Superd race's honorable reputation; [Modern Era] Remains an untamed, resource-scarce frontier where only hardened warriors, demon clans, and high-rank adventurers survive.}

---

# Milis Continent
<!-- Keys: Milis Continent, Millis Continent, Continent of Milis -->

DESC: {The sacred southern continent of the Six-Faced World, bordered to the north by the Great Forest and separated from the Central Continent by the ocean. Comprises two distinctly governed geographical realms: 1. Northern Half (The Great Forest): A colossal, primeval rain forest inhabited by the Beast Race (Doldia Tribe, Dedoldia, Adoldia), governed from the holy tree city of Doldia Village under the protection of the Sacred Beast. 2. Southern Half: The Holy Country of Milis (Saint Kingdom of Milis), the global heart of the Milis Church, centered around the breathtaking white stone capital of Millishion. Enforces strict religious canon, monotheistic Milis doctrine, advanced healing/detoxification hospitals, and elite Temple Knight legions. Connected to the Central Continent via Zant Port (Saint Port) and Westport.}

MAP: {Northern Realm: The Great Forest, Doldia Sacred Village, Zant Port | Mountain Boundary: Blue Dragon Mountain Range | Southern Realm: Holy Country of Milis, Millishion Capital, Anastasia Keep, Westport}

HISTORY: {[Saint Milis Foundation] Established thousands of years ago by Saint Milis following the mythical subjugation of continental beasts; [Dead End Crossing: Year K418] Rudeus, Eris, and Ruijerd traversed the Great Forest; imprisoned and befriended the Doldia tribe; fought Gallus Cleaner in Zant Port; reunited with Paul Greyrat in Millishion amidst emotional turmoil; [The Papal Succession Crisis: Year K425] Rudeus returned to Millishion to rescue telepathic Zenith and protect the Blessed Child of Memory, thwarting Archbishop Latriea's anti-demon extremist coup and aligning the Milis Church with Cliff Grimoire and the Orsted Corporation.}

---

# Begaritt Continent
<!-- Keys: Begaritt Continent, Begarit Continent, Begaritt -->

DESC: {An unforgiving, sun-scorched desert continent located southwest of the Central Continent, encircled by tempestuous ocean currents and lethal sandstorm zones. Consists of endless rolling dune seas, subterranean glass caverns, razor-sharp sandstone canyons, and ancient underground ruins. Infested by giant scorpions, sand drakes, succubi, and ancient magical anomalies, boasting a survival difficulty on par with the Demon Continent. The local economy revolves entirely around Labyrinth excavation, centered in the bustling frontier hub of Rapan (Labyrinth Town Rapanear). Contains the infamous Teleport Labyrinth, where ancient magical teleportation arrays and lethal guardian monsters lurk.}

MAP: {Coastal Port: Eastport / Desert Gateway | Inland Oasis: Labyrinth Town Rapanear, Mercenary Guild Quarters | Subterranean: The Teleport Labyrinth (Manatite Hydra Lair)}

HISTORY: {[Ancient Labyrinth Formation] Formed during primordial magical cataclysms when ancient underground structures condensed ambient mana into hyper-dense crystal labyrinths; [The Begaritt Rescue Expedition: Year K423] Paul Greyrat, Roxy Migurdia, Elinalise, Talhand, and later Rudeus Greyrat plunged into the 6-floor Teleport Labyrinth to rescue Zenith; conquered the labyrinth at the tragic cost of Paul's life and Rudeus's left forearm, slaying the Manatite Hydra and recovering Zenith; [Post-Conquest Boom] Rapan transitioned into a major magical research and mining outpost under international adventurer syndicates.}

---

# Heaven Continent
<!-- Keys: Heaven Continent -->

DESC: {The highest, most isolated continent in the Six-Faced World, situated atop colossal sheer cliffs rising over 3,000 meters above sea level in the northern polar seas. Perpetually shrouded in freezing gale-force blizzards, thin oxygen atmosphere, and glowing aurora borealis. Homeland of the Heavenly Race (Winged People), who possess feathered wings, hollow lightweight bones, and innate Wind/Lightning magic mastery. Virtually inaccessible to non-winged mortals except via ancient teleportation circles or airborne constructs like Armored Dragon King Perugius's Chaos Breaker. Possesses pristine, untouched natural landscapes, high-tier floating stone deposits, and ancient ruins from the primordial Heavenly World.}

MAP: {High Plateaus: Feathered Roosts, Heavenly Sanctum, High Cliff Passes | Surrounding Barrier: 3,000m Sheer Basalt Sea Cliffs}

HISTORY: {[Primordial Heavenly Realm] Originally part of the mythical Sky World before its destruction and merger into the Six-Faced World; [Ancient Dragon Alliance] Reared spiritual familiars and retainers who allied with the Five Dragon Generals; [Isolationist Millennia] Maintained absolute neutrality throughout the Human-Demon and Laplace Wars, rarely descending to the surface world.}

---

# East Continent / East Country
<!-- Keys: East Continent / East Country, East Continent, East Country -->

DESC: {An enigmatic, distant maritime landmass and island chain located far to the eastern edge of the world map, beyond the perilous monster seas of the Ringus. Characterized by temperate coastal islands, dense bamboo and cherry blossom forests, volcanic hot springs, and distinctive feudal architecture. Inhabited by isolationist human clans and seafaring demon tribes practicing unique sword-drawing arts, spiritual paper talismans, and specialized culinary traditions (rice, soy, seafood dishes). Maintains limited trade with coastal ports of the Central and Demon Continents, exporting exotic raw materials, silk textiles, and specialized forged blades.}

MAP: {Maritime Archipelagos, Feudal Clan Estates, Volcanic Harbors}

HISTORY: {[Ancient Seafaring Migration] Founded by ancient human refugees who crossed eastern seas to escape continental wars; [Culinary & Cultural Influence] Provided culinary techniques and exotic foodstuffs rediscovered by Rudeus Greyrat and Nanahoshi Shizuka during their rice and miso research.}

---

# Kingdom of Asura
<!-- Keys: Kingdom of Asura, Asura, Asura Kingdom, Asuran -->

DESC: {The supreme superpower of the Six-Faced World, occupying the fertile western third of the Central Continent west of the Red Dragon Mountain Range. Possesses unmatched agricultural wealth, an inexhaustible food supply with zero famine, breathtaking architectural marvels, and the world's most sophisticated noble court in the capital of Ars. Governed by the Asura Royal Family and the Four Great Noble Houses of Greyrat (Boreas of Fittoa, Notos of the South, Durdard of the North, and Zephyrus of the West). Militarily defended by the elite Asuran Royal Guard, the Royal Water God Knights, and imperial legions. Despite its glittering cultural refinement, the court was notoriously plagued by decadent bribery, ruthless succession assassination plots, and backroom political corruption before Queen Ariel Anemoi Asura seized the throne and instituted sweeping meritocratic reforms.}

MAP: {Royal Capital: Ars (Silver Palace) | Eastern Border Duchy: Fittoa Region (Roa, Buena) | Southern Estates: Notos Territory | Northern Estates: Durdard Territory | Western Estates: Zephyrus Territory}

HISTORY: {[Imperial Golden Age] Rose to continental supremacy over a millennium through vast agricultural surpluses and Water God martial traditions; [The Fittoa Mana Catastrophe: Year K417] The mysterious mana explosion in Roa wiped out the Boreas Greyrat domain, triggering nationwide noble maneuvering and the execution of Lord Sauros; [The Asuran Succession Crisis: Year K423] Princess Ariel returned from exile backed by Rudeus Greyrat and Dragon God Orsted; during the Silver Palace banquet, exposed Prime Minister Darius's treason, defeated Water God Reida's coup, and ascended as Queen Ariel, transforming Asura into the primary political and financial ally of the Orsted Corporation.}

---

# Kingdom of Ranoa
<!-- Keys: Kingdom of Ranoa, Ranoa, Ranoa Kingdom -->

DESC: {The leading nation of the Northern Magic Triumvirate, situated in the northeastern sector of the Central Continent. A cold, temperate realm renowned as the global intellectual capital of magical research, education, and spellcraft innovation. Governed from the grand Magic City of Sharia by an elected Magic Council alongside a constitutional monarchy. Houses the world-famous Ranoa Magic Academy (Magic University), the International Magician Guild Headquarters, and extensive magical tool manufacturing workshops. Maintains an open, progressive social policy welcoming humans, demons, beastfolk, and dwarves alike, fostering peaceful multicultural coexistence.}

MAP: {Capital: Magic City of Sharia | Academic Quarter: Ranoa Magic Academy Campus, Student Dormitories | Guild District: Magician Guild HQ | Residential: Greyrat Family Estate}

HISTORY: {[Foundation of the Magic Triumvirate] Formed alongside Basherant and Neris to establish an independent northern sanctuary for magical academia; [The Golden Academic Era: Year K419+] Welcomed Princess Ariel's exiled court and teenage Rudeus Greyrat; became the focal center for advanced research in silent casting, summoning arrays, Magic Armor engineering, and telepathic linguistics; [Headquarters of the Greyrat Lineage] Served as Rudeus's permanent hometown, where he raised his children and coordinated global Orsted Corporation operations.}

---

# Dragon King Kingdom
<!-- Keys: Dragon King Kingdom, King Dragon Kingdom, Kingdom of the Dragon King -->

DESC: {A massive, militaristic southern superpower occupying the southern peninsula of the Central Continent, bordering the Strife Zone. Possesses vast standing armies, disciplined knightly orders, and impenetrable coastal cliff bastions. Militarily influenced by the North God Style and Water God Style; historically home to legendary Dragon King warriors and North God Kalman masters. Maintains an aggressive, expansionist foreign policy, perpetually engaging in proxy wars across neighboring smaller states (Shirone, Sanakia, Kikka) to maintain southern hegemony.}

MAP: {Capital: Royal Dragon Fortress | Border Passes: Strife Zone Frontiers | Northern Border: Shirone and Sanakia Gateways}

HISTORY: {[Ancient Martial Empire] Founded by dragon-slaying warrior kings; expanded through constant southern military campaigns; [Proxy Wars of the South: K400-K427] Manipulated political factions in Shirone; sheltered exiled princes including Pax Shirone; stabilized into a cautious diplomatic truce with the Orsted Corporation following North God Kalman II's mediation.}

---

# Shirone Kingdom
<!-- Keys: Shirone Kingdom, Shirone -->

DESC: {A medium-sized southern kingdom situated in the fertile river basin north of the Dragon King Kingdom, functioning as a strategic buffer state. Governed from the royal citadel of Shirone by a hereditary monarchy historically dominated by military factions and royal concubine intrigues. Birthplace of Prince Zanoba Shirone (the Miko of Superhuman Strength) and Seventh Prince Pax Shirone; former employer of Roxy Migurdia as royal magic tutor. Possessed fertile grain fields and strategic highway crossroads, making it a constant target for foreign espionage and coups. Briefly transitioned into the Shirone Republic under King Pax before political collapse and annexation.}

MAP: {Capital: Shirone Royal Citadel | Northern Gateway: Strife Zone Border | Southern Gateway: Dragon King Highway}

HISTORY: {[Prince Pax's Tyranny: Year K418] Prince Pax held Lillia and Aisha hostage; Rudeus and Zanoba rebelled, liberating the hostages and sending Pax into exile; [The Republican Coup & Fall: Year K427] Pax returned with progressive revolutionary forces, claiming the throne and declaring the Shirone Republic; besieged by reactionary neighboring states and betrayed from within; Pax committed suicide from the palace balcony, leading to foreign military partition.}

---

# Basherant Dukedom (Basherant)
<!-- Keys: Basherant Dukedom, Basherant, Basherant Kingdom -->

DESC: {One of the Three Northern Magic Triumvirate nations, located adjacent to the Kingdom of Ranoa. A rugged, snowbound dukedom renowned for its specialized barrier magic research, dwarven stone-masonry fortresses, and tactical mercenary guilds. Collaborates closely with Ranoa and Neris in international trade, academic conferences, and mutual border defense against northern tundra beast hordes.}

MAP: {Ducal Castle: Basherant Keep | Eastern Road: Ranoa Border | Northern Border: Tundra Wilderness}

HISTORY: {[Triumvirate Compact] Formed defensive alliances to protect the northern magic academies; [Modern Research Hub] Supplied advanced barrier stones and runic defensive matrices for the Magic Armor MK projects developed in Sharia.}

---

# Sanakia Kingdom
<!-- Keys: Sanakia Kingdom, Sanakia -->

DESC: {A small southern kingdom nestled between the Dragon King Kingdom and the Strife Zone. Heavily reliant on agricultural exports, spice trade routes, and mercenary contracts with larger empires. Its court politics are perpetually entangled in espionage and proxy conflicts between the Dragon King Kingdom and the Asuran commercial guilds.}

MAP: {Capital: Sanakia City | Trade Roads: Dragon King Southern Highway}

HISTORY: {[Southern Border Struggles] Navigated centuries of vassalage and shifting military alliances to preserve its sovereignty amidst southern power struggles.}

---

# Kikka Kingdom
<!-- Keys: Kikka Kingdom, Kikka -->

DESC: {A minor southern principality located along the northern periphery of the Strife Zone. Known for its fortified river checkpoints, livestock ranches, and seasonal warrior hiring fairs. Frequently serves as a forward staging ground and supply depot for mercenary companies deploying into southern regional skirmishes.}

MAP: {Fortified Border Towns, River Tollgates, Mercenary Encampments}

HISTORY: {[Mercenary Economy] Maintained economic survival by leasing garrison outposts and supply lines to international adventurer and mercenary guilds.}

---

# Biheiril Kingdom
<!-- Keys: Biheiril Kingdom, Biheiril -->

DESC: {A remote, heavily forested, and mountainous kingdom located at the far northeastern tip of the Central Continent. Governed from the capital of Irelia by a feudal king under the heavy religious and military influence of the Ogre God and Demon King retainers. Possesses dense ancient woodlands (the Forest of the Ogre God), towering coastal crags, and isolated villages of the Ogre race and Superd refugees. Served as the climactic battlefield for the final showdown between the Orsted Corporation and Hitogami's premier apostles (Geese, Badigadi, Gal Farion, Kalman III).}

MAP: {Capital: Royal City of Irelia | Sacred Grounds: Forest of the Ogre God | Superd Settlement: Hidden Superd Village | Coastal Fortress: Ringus Bay Bastion}

HISTORY: {[Isolationist Martial Realm] Preserved ancient demon-human treaties allowing the Ogre God and Superd remnants to inhabit deep mountain valleys; [The Great Biheiril War: Year K427] Hitogami dispatched Geese to orchestrate an ambush against Orsted; Rudeus deployed the Magic Armor MK-Zero; joined by Eris, Ruijerd, Kalman II, and Atofe's demon legion, they crushed Hitogami's forces, sealing Badigadi and securing the Superd's future.}

---

# Kingdom of Neris
<!-- Keys: Kingdom of Neris, Neris -->

DESC: {The third member of the Northern Magic Triumvirate, situated east of Ranoa. Distinguished by its high-altitude alpine valleys, crystal-clear glacial lakes, and specialized alchemy and magical potion breweries. Houses world-renowned alchemical research institutes supplying high-grade mana potions, healing salves, and enchanted ink across the world.}

MAP: {Capital: Neris Alchemical Center | Mountain Passages: Ranoa Alpine Road}

HISTORY: {[Alchemical Innovation] Pioneered mass-refining techniques for magic stones and restorative draughts utilized by high-ranking adventurers and mages worldwide.}

---

# Saint Kingdom of Milis (Holy Milis Kingdom)
<!-- Keys: Saint Kingdom of Milis, Holy Milis Kingdom, Milis Kingdom, Kingdom of Milis, Holy Milis -->

DESC: {The divine theocracy dominating the southern half of the Milis Continent, functioning as the religious capital of the global Milis Church. Governed from the immaculate white stone holy city of Millishion by the Pope, the Council of Cardinals, and high noble houses (including House Latreia). Enforces strict monotheistic Milis doctrine, complete prohibition of polygamy, and rigorous moral purity laws. Maintains formidable military forces divided between the Order of Temple Knights (Sword, Shield, and Bow Companies) and the Church Inquisitors. A land of architectural splendor, clean paved aqueducts, advanced divine healing cathedrals, and deep-seated political factionalism between Demon Expulsion and Acceptance factions.}

MAP: {Holy Capital: Millishion (Great Milis Cathedral, Anastasia Keep, Latreia Manor) | Northern Boundary: Blue Dragon Mountains | Maritime Port: Westport}

HISTORY: {[Foundation by Saint Milis] Established thousands of years ago as the sanctuary of human purity; [Internal Factional Strife] Split into hardline anti-demon extremists and moderate integrationists; [The Papal Intervention: Year K425] Rudeus and Cliff Grimoire foiled Archbishop Latriea's plot, rescuing the Pope and establishing Cliff as a rising reformist leader in the holy see.}

---

# Kingdom of Biyakuya
<!-- Keys: Kingdom of Biyakuya, Biyakuya -->

DESC: {A small, sub-arctic kingdom nestled in the northeastern mountain passes of the Northern Lands. Features severe permafrost winters, pine timber forestry, and seasonal fur hunting guilds. Maintains friendly diplomatic and trade ties with the Kingdom of Ranoa and the adventurer outposts of the North.}

MAP: {Fortified Timber Towns, Mountain Hunting Passes}

HISTORY: {[Northern Frontier Outpost] Maintained steady fur trade and monster pelt supply lines for northern adventurer guilds.}

---

# Karon Kingdom
<!-- Keys: Karon Kingdom, Karon -->

DESC: {A rugged northern monarchy bordering the Holy Land of Swords. Renowned for its heavy iron mines, specialized weapon smithies, and disciplined garrison infantry. Supplies forged steel blades, armor plates, and raw ore to the martial dojos of the Sword Sanctum and the magic workshops of Sharia.}

MAP: {Capital: Karon Iron City | Northern Trail: Holy Sword Highway to Sword Sanctum}

HISTORY: {[Smithing Hub of the North] Forged ceremonial and combat blades for generations of Sword Saints and Sword Kings hailing from the Holy Land of Swords.}

---

# Ars
<!-- Keys: Ars -->

DESC: {The magnificent royal capital of the Kingdom of Asura, widely celebrated as the largest, most opulent, and architecturally dazzling metropolis in the Six-Faced World. Centered around the colossal Silver Palace, a breathtaking complex of ivory marble towers, gilded domes, lush botanical pleasure gardens, and impenetrable subterranean vaults. Divided into sprawling concentric rings: the High Aristocratic District (housing the opulent manors of the Greyrat branches and ministers), the Imperial Commercial Quarter (the nexus of global luxury goods, silk, spices, and banking guilds), and the outer Commoners' Ring.
