SHORT_DESC: {One of the Three Heroes of the Laplace War, Armored Dragon King, and master of the legendary airborne citadel Chaos Breaker [Birth: Ancient Dragon Era]; a majestic, golden-haired dragon lord adorned in pristine white-and-gold armor, possessing unmatched mastery of ancient summoning magic, barrier matrices, and anti-demon dragon arts; assisted Dragon God Urupen and North God Kalman I in sealing Demon God Laplace four hundred years ago; commands twelve mythical spirit familiars (including Sylvaril and Arumanfi) from his floating sky fortress, keeping eternal vigil over the world until Laplace's prophesied resurrection; served as a pivotal political and magical patron to Princess Ariel and Rudeus Greyrat, testing Rudeus's soul, bestowing formal names upon his children (such as Sieghart Saladin Greyrat), and providing strategic transportation during global anti-Hitogami campaigns | [Personality & Quirks] Aristocratic, immensely proud, and upholding an uncompromising code of noble dragon etiquette; harbors an absolute, visceral hatred for the Demon God Laplace and the Mazoku race, yet treats individual demons who demonstrate honor and loyalty (like Ruijerd and Badigadi) with dignified respect; deeply appreciative of high culture, fine art, classical music, and gourmet cuisine, hosting grand banquets in Chaos Breaker's golden halls; possesses a subtle, dry sense of humor and secretly relishes being asked to name newborn noble children, spending days deliberating on prestigious titles; demands proper protocol from all guests, yet displays genuine warmth and protective nobility toward those who prove their character; never walks when he can levitate, surrounded by a gentle aura of pure celestial light; enjoys playing ancient dragon board games and listening to live harp performances.}

HISTORY: {[Armored Dragon King: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] United with Dragon God Urupen and North God Kalman I; commanded the floating fortress Chaos Breaker to bombard Laplace's legions; landed the decisive sealing blow against Demon God Laplace; [Armored Dragon King: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Monitored the Six-Faced World from Chaos Breaker; granted audience to Princess Ariel and Rudeus Greyrat; tested Rudeus's loyalty and named his newborn son Sieghart Saladin; [Armored Dragon King: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Granted transit and tactical intelligence to the Orsted Corporation; deployed his familiars in the Biheiril campaign; maintained eternal watch for Laplace's final return.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Armored Dragon King: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Immaculate snow-white tunic with intricate gold dragon filigree embroidery, polished dragon-scale white cuirass, billowing white-and-gold cape Immaculate snow-white tunic with intricate gold dragon filigree embroidery, polished dragon-scale white cuirass, billowing white-and-gold cape, ornate golden signet ring}

---

# Geese Nukadia
<!-- Keys: Geese Nukadia, Geese, Gisu, Nukadia, Monkey-faced adventurer -->

SHORT_DESC: {Last survivor of the Nukadia monkey demon tribe, former rogue vanguard of the S-Rank party 'Fangs of the Black Wolf', and the final apostle of Hitogami [Birth: K380 - Death: K427]; a wiry, scarred monkey beastman with a perpetually relaxed, street-smart demeanor, gambling addiction, and distinct culinary mastery; completely devoid of combat Touki and magical power, surviving purely on unmatched street cunning, infiltration expertise, lockpicking, social engineering, and geopolitical logistics; shared a prison cell with Rudeus in the Great Forest, forming a close friendship and subsequently guiding Rudeus through the Begaritt Continent to rescue Zenith; secretly served as Hitogami's most devoted apostle, viewing the Man-God as his divine savior who prevented his tribe's total extinction; following Rudeus's defection to Orsted, Geese honorably declared war on Rudeus, traveling the world to assemble a coalition of god-tier warriors (Fighting God Badigadi, Sword God Gal Farion, and North God Kalman III) for the final battle in Biheiril; mortally wounded in the Biheiril forest, dying in Rudeus's arms after sharing one final honest conversation | [Personality & Quirks] Streetwise, roguish, affable, and deeply cynical, with an easygoing grin that masks terrifying strategic clarity; hopelessly addicted to dice, cards, and casino tables, constantly losing all his money and relying on friends or quick hustles; an exceptional, world-class chef who cooks comforting, delicious stews over campfires; never held personal malice against Rudeus, genuinely loving Paul and the Greyrat family like blood brothers, yet bound by absolute religious debt to Hitogami; speaks in informal, fast-talking rogue slang, frequently referring to himself in the third person ('Old Geese') and calling Rudeus 'Senpai'; pragmatic to the bone, never letting ego interfere with survival or mission objectives; his tragic duel of wits with Rudeus was fought with total professional respect.}

HISTORY: {[Fangs of the Black Wolf Era: K400-K415: Age 20-35] Served as scout and logistical genius for Paul Greyrat's S-Rank party; dissolved after Zenith's pregnancy; [The Great Forest & Begaritt Journey: K418-K422: Age 38-42] Met Rudeus in a Doldia prison cell; joined Dead End's journey; navigated the Begaritt labyrinth to help rescue Zenith; [Hitogami's Grand Mobilization: K425-K427: Age 45-47] Sent the letter that lured Rudeus to Begaritt under Hitogami's orders; after being exposed as an apostle, formally declared war; traveled across the globe recruiting Badigadi, Gal Farion, and Alec; [The Final Stand in Biheiril & Death: K427: Age 47] Coordinated Hitogami's final army; mortally wounded in the burning forest; shared his final words with Rudeus, dying content that he gave his all for his god.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Fangs of Black Wolf & Begaritt: Age 20-45 / K400-K425] 165cm / 5'5", 58kg; wiry, agile, and lean monkey demon build; [Biheiril Final Battle: Age 47 / K427] 165cm, 56kg, exhausted, battle-worn rogue frame 165cm / 5'5", 58kg; wiry, agile, and lean monkey demon build; completely lacking Touki enhancement, possessing flexible joints and light footsteps | Height & Weight: 165cm / 5'5", 58kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20+ / K400+] Average monkey-demon proportions, intact Average monkey-demon proportions, intact | Skin: [Black Wolf to Biheiril: Age 20-47 / K400-K427] Coarse, greyish-tan demon skin covered in light brown fur along arms and legs Coarse, greyish-tan demon skin covered in light brown fur along the arms, back, and calves | Face: [Adult: Age 20-47 / K400-K427] Distinct monkey-like facial structure with flat nose, expressive wrinkled brow, and cheeky rogue grin Distinct monkey-like facial structure with a broad, flat nose, expressive wrinkled brow, and perpetual cheeky grin | Hair: [Adult: Age 20-47 / K400-K427] Messy, tufted dark brown hair sticking out in all directions Messy, tufted dark brown hair sticking out in all directions | Eyes: [Adult: Age 20-47 / K400-K427] Beady, sharp, intensely observant dark brown eyes calculating odds Beady, sharp, and intensely observant dark brown eyes that calculate odds and exits constantly | Distinguishing Traits: Total absence of Touki; monkey-like facial features; expert cooking aroma; lucky dice pouch tied to belt | Outfits by Timeline: [Black Wolf & Begaritt: Age 20-45 / K400-K425] Scruffy brown adventurer vest with hidden pockets, worn linen shirt, patched leather trousers; [Biheiril War: Age 47 / K427] Dark traveling poncho, concealed apostle signaling scrolls Scruffy brown adventurer's vest with dozens of concealed pockets for lockpicks and dice, worn linen shirt, patched leather trousers, soft moccasins, hooded poncho}

# Talhand of the Cliff
<!-- Keys: Talhand of the Cliff, Talhand, Dwarven Mage -->

SHORT_DESC: {S-Rank veteran dwarf warrior-mage, former frontline defender of the legendary adventurer party 'Fangs of the Black Wolf', and lifelong comrade of Paul Greyrat [Birth: K370]; a heavily armored, stout dwarf possessing a thick braided beard, wielding reinforced battleaxes and specialized defensive earth/fire spells; known for his gruff, no-nonsense temperament, unyielding loyalty to his companions, and severe motion sickness that makes carriage travel a living hell; served as the defensive anchor and tactical ballast for the Black Wolf party, constantly reigning in Paul and Elinalise's chaotic antics; spent years searching for the scattered Fittoa refugees alongside Paul in the Begaritt Continent, participating in the brutal Teleport Labyrinth raid to rescue Zenith; later settled in Sharia, working with the Magic Guild and providing veteran advice and craftsmanship to the Greyrat household | [Personality & Quirks] Gruff, stubborn, and blunt-spoken, but fiercely protective of his friends and their children; harbors severe, debilitating motion sickness, turning green and vomiting whenever boarding horse carriages, ships, or riding beasts, preferring to march on foot for days; loves heavy dwarven stout ale, underground smithing, and mineral appraisal; enjoys a long-standing, comical bickering friendship with Elinalise, trading savage insults while having complete mutual trust in combat; rarely speaks about his private life, focusing entirely on blacksmithing and tactical fortification; treats Rudeus, Norn, and Aisha with gruff paternal affection, secretly proud of their growth.}

HISTORY: {[Fangs of the Black Wolf Era: K400-K415: Age 30-45] Frontline vanguard and earth mage for Paul's legendary party; mediated endless intra-party squabbles; [The Begaritt Search & Labyrinth Raid: K417-K422: Age 47-52] Rejoined Paul in Rapan; fought through the Teleport Labyrinth; witnessed Paul's sacrifice and helped bring Zenith back to Sharia; [Sharia Retirement & Crafting: K423+: Age 53+] Settled in the Northern Lands; advised the Orsted Corporation on mineral alloys and fortress defense, remaining a beloved uncle to the Greyrat family.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Black Wolf to Sharia Veteran: Age 30-55+ / K400-K425+] 135cm / 4'5", 85kg; colossal, barrel-chested dwarf physique; immense upper-body muscle density, thick limbs, and immovable low center of gravity 135cm / 4'5", 85kg; colossal, barrel-chested dwarf physique; immense upper-body muscle density, thick limbs, and immovable low center of gravity | Height & Weight: 135cm / 4'5", 85kg | Penile: [Adult Dwarf: Age 30+ / K400+] Stout, heavy dwarf anatomy proportional to his dense build Stout, heavy dwarf anatomy proportional to his dense build | Skin: [Adult Dwarf: Age 30+ / K400+] Weathered, ruddy bronze dwarven skin, scarred from forge sparks and monster claws Weathered, ruddy bronze dwarven skin, scarred from forge sparks and monster claws | Face: [Adult Dwarf: Age 30+ / K400+] Broad, craggy dwarven visage with deep brow furrows, thick eyebrows, and a permanent stern frown Broad, craggy dwarven visage with deep brow furrows, thick eyebrows, and a permanent stern frown | Hair & Beard: [Adult Dwarf: Age 30+ / K400+] Iron-grey, thick braided hair and a magnificent, braided beard reaching down to his belt buckle Iron-grey, thick braided hair and a magnificent, braided beard reaching down to his belt buckle | Eyes: [Adult Dwarf: Age 30+ / K400+] Deep-set, steely dark brown eyes that assess masonry, ore, and enemy weaknesses Deep-set, steely dark brown eyes that assess masonry, ore, and enemy weaknesses | Distinguishing Traits: Immense physical mass for his height; braided dwarf beard; severe carriage motion sickness; heavy dwarven earth magic | Outfits by Timeline: [Black Wolf & Labyrinth: Age 30-52 / K400-K422] Heavy custom-forged dwarven steel full-plate armor, twin-headed battleaxe; [Sharia Crafting Era: Age 53+ / K423+] Heavy leather smithing apron over steel cuirass, pipe case Heavy custom-forged dwarven steel full-plate armor with reinforced shoulder pauldrons, thick leather under-padding, twin-headed dwarven battleaxe strapped to back, heavy iron tower shield}

# Alexander Rybak (North God Kalman III / Alec)
<!-- Keys: Alexander Rybak, Alec, North God Kalman III, Kalman III -->

SHORT_DESC: {The North God Kalman III, son of North God Kalman II (Sandor) and descendant of Immortal Demon Lord Atoferatofe [Birth: K405]; Rank 7 of the Seven Great World Powers, wielder of the mythical Dragon King Sword 'Kajakut' (Gravity Blade); an exceptionally handsome, half-demon youth possessing incredible physical speed, regenerative vigor, and mastery of all North God Style improvisation; obsessed from childhood with becoming a true, legendary 'Hero' of justice, viewing the world through romanticized knightly ideals and epic ballads; manipulated by Hitogami into believing that slaying Dragon God Orsted was his destiny to save humanity; served as the supreme vanguard champion of Hitogami's coalition in the Biheiril campaign, clashing against Rudeus in the Magic Armor MK-I, Eris, and Ruijerd; disarmed of Kajakut by Rudeus's tactical shotgun blast and crushed by Dragon God Orsted, who revealed that true heroism lies in protecting others rather than seeking glory; surrendered and joined the Orsted Corporation, being assigned to serve as the bodyguard and chief knight for Queen Ariel in Asura | [Personality & Quirks] Romantic, dramatic, boastful, and brimming with youthful theatricality; constantly delivers grandiloquent hero monologues, strikes heroic poses before drawing his blade, and names his attacks with flamboyant flair; has an innocent, childish worldview despite his terrifying martial strength, easily swayed by dramatic tales of heroism and righteous destiny; deeply loves his grandmother Atoferatofe and father Sandor, though he initially rebelled against his father's grounded, pragmatic advice; possesses an indomitable fighting spirit, refusing to stay down even when battered by god-class magic; after being humbled by Orsted, he matured into a genuinely noble, dedicated knight who uses his immense power for true selfless protection; loves listening to traveling bards, eating sweet pastries, and polishing his legendary blade.}

HISTORY: {[North God Training & Ascent: Age 10-18 / K415-K423] Inherited the title of North God Kalman III from his father; claimed the Dragon King Sword Kajakut; attained Rank 7 of the Seven Great Powers; [Hitogami's Manipulation: Age 21 / K426] Contacted by Geese and Hitogami; convinced that defeating Orsted would cement his status as the greatest hero in history; [The Battle of Biheiril: Age 22 / K427] Led Hitogami's vanguard; fought Rudeus (MK-I), Eris, Ruijerd, and his father Sandor; disarmed and defeated by Orsted; submitted to the Dragon God; [Knight of Asura: Age 23+ / K428+] Stationed in Ars as royal protector to Queen Ariel, protecting the realm against covert threats.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Youth & North God Prodigy: Age 15-20 / K420-K425] 176cm, 68kg, slender, dazzling half-demon swordsman youth; [Peak Kalman III & Biheiril Vanguard: Age 21-22 / K426-K427] 180cm / 5'11", 75kg; lean, remarkably athletic, and aesthetically sculpted half-demon swordsman physique; whipcord muscle definition combining human elegance with demon-lord durability; [Imperial Knight of Asura: Age 23+ / K428+] 180cm, 76kg, polished, majestic royal champion physique | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 21+ / K426+] 180cm / 5'11", 75-76kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20+ / K425+] Well-proportioned, aesthetic half-demon anatomy, intact | Skin: [North God to Asuran Knight: Age 15-30+ / K420-K435+] Fair, pale skin with subtle undertones of demon resilience; regenerates minor wounds rapidly | Face: [North God to Asuran Knight: Age 15-30+ / K420-K435+] Strikingly handsome, youthful features with sharp jawline, noble brow, and theatrical heroic confidence | Hair: [North God to Asuran Knight: Age 15-30+ / K420-K435+] Lustrous, wavy silver-white hair (inherited from Atoferatofe) falling stylishly around his face | Eyes: [North God to Asuran Knight: Age 15-30+ / K420-K435+] Radiant crimson demon eyes that glow brightly when channeling Touki | Distinguishing Traits: The Gravity Blade 'Dragon King Sword Kajakut'; half-demon regenerative vitality; theatrical hero poses and monologues | Outfits by Timeline: [Heroic Roaming Era: Age 18-22 / K423-K427] Elegant white-and-crimson swordsman tunic adorned with gold trim, polished silver breastplate, dark combat trousers, knee-high dueling boots, flowing red cape; [Asuran Royal Knight Era: Age 23+ / K428+] Pristine white-and-gold Asuran Imperial Knight uniform}

---

# Alexander (North God Kalman II / Sandor von Grandeur)
<!-- Keys: Alexander, Kalman II, North God Kalman II, Sandor von Grandeur, Sandor -->

SHORT_DESC: {The North God Kalman II, son of North God Kalman I and Immortal Demon Lord Atoferatofe, and father of Kalman III (Alec) [Birth: K380]; a charismatic, scarred, and cheerful veteran warrior who traveled the world under the alias 'Sandor von Grandeur'; inherited his mother's immense demonic regeneration and his father's peerless North God improvisation, having defeated the previous Dragon King sword wielder and slain legendary behemoths in his youth; retired from active title defense to let his son inherit the North God mantle, wandering as a wandering mercenary and diplomat; joined the Orsted Corporation during the Biheiril campaign, mediating with his mother Atoferatofe to secure demon reinforcements and directly confronting his misguided son Alec on the battlefield; an extraordinarily wise, grounded, and humorous master who teaches that a true warrior adapts to any circumstance, values human life above pride, and uses whatever tools are at hand | [Personality & Quirks] Warm, jovial, humble, and completely devoid of martial arrogance; laughs easily with a dry, self-deprecating chuckle; despises empty posturing and rigid honor codes, happily using dirty tricks, sand throws, barstools, or peaceful negotiations to resolve conflicts; deeply loves his eccentric mother Atofe and son Alec, acting as the long-suffering straight man to their dramatic outbursts; an exceptional storyteller who enjoys drinking cheap wine around campfires and swapping tales of past blunders; deeply respects Dragon God Orsted and Rudeus Greyrat, providing shrewd geopolitical advice to the Orsted Corporation; speaks in a friendly, relaxed, and conversational tone.}

HISTORY: {[North God Supremacy: K400s: Age 20] Inherited the North God title; tamed the Dragon King Sword Kajakut; traveled all six continents slaying monsters; [Wandering as Sandor: K415-K425: Age 35-45] Passed the Kalman III title to his son Alec; adopted the identity 'Sandor von Grandeur'; served as mercenary advisor in the Central Continent; [The Biheiril Mediation & Battle: K427: Age 47] Reconciled with Rudeus; negotiated with Atoferatofe to bring demon reinforcements; fought his son Alec to teach him the true meaning of strength; [Orsted Corporation Senior Advisor: Age 47+ / K427+] Managed intelligence and diplomatic outreach for the company across the human realm.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Peak Kalman II: Age 20-35 / K400-K415] 185cm, 92kg, massive, shredded half-demon warrior; [Sandor von Grandeur & Biheiril: Age 36-47+ / K416-K427+] 185cm / 6'1", 90kg; broad-shouldered, rugged, and powerfully built half-demon veteran frame 185cm / 6'1", 90kg; broad-shouldered, rugged, and powerfully built half-demon veteran frame; dense, battle-hardened musculature weathered by a century of combat | Height & Weight: 185cm / 6'1", 90kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20+ / K400+] Above average, rugged half-demon anatomy, intact Above average, rugged half-demon anatomy | Skin: [Adult: Age 20+ / K400+] Dark-tanned, weathered skin covered in dozens of faded scars that have healed over decades Dark-tanned, weathered skin covered in dozens of faded scars that have healed over decades | Face: [Adult: Age 20+ / K400+] Rugged, handsome middle-aged face with a friendly stubble beard, laugh lines around the eyes, and a confident, relaxed expression Rugged, handsome middle-aged face with a friendly stubble beard, laugh lines around the eyes, and a confident, relaxed expression | Hair: [Adult: Age 20+ / K400+] Messy, dark greyish-brown hair streaked with silver, tied back loosely Messy, dark greyish-brown hair streaked with silver, tied back loosely | Eyes: [Adult: Age 20+ / K400+] Calm, warm, deeply perceptive dark amber eyes Calm, warm, deeply perceptive dark amber eyes | Distinguishing Traits: Incredible partial demon regeneration; # Gino Britz (Sword God Gino)
<!-- Keys: Gino Britz, Gino, Sword God Gino, Sword God Gino Britz -->

SHORT_DESC: {The supreme Sword God (successor to Gal Farion), nephew of Gal Farion, and husband of Nina Falion [Birth: K407]; initially a timid, insecure, and unassuming apprentice at the Holy Land of Swords, constantly overshadowed by Nina and Eris Boreas Greyrat; fueled by an all-consuming, desperate love for Nina after Gal Farion decreed that only a man stronger than himself could marry his daughter; underwent years of solitary, obsessive, and fanatical training in the frozen mountain heights, refining the Longsword of Light to unprecedented, zero-anticipation hypersonic velocity; challenged and decisively defeated Sword God Gal Farion in a historic single-stroke duel, claiming the mantle of Sword God (Rank 6 of the Seven Great World Powers); married Nina Falion and restructured the Holy Land of Swords dojo into a disciplined, politically neutral sword academy, declining Geese's overtures to join Hitogami's war | [Personality & Quirks] Humble, unassuming, soft-spoken, and remarkably grounded despite being the deadliest single-strike swordsman alive; entirely motivated by pure, devoted romantic love for Nina; lacks the arrogant bloodlust of traditional Sword God Style masters, viewing the sword as a sacred craft of absolute focus and domestic protection; deeply devoted to his wife and children, happily performing household chores and managing dojo finances; displays terrifying, instant cold focus the moment his hand touches a hilt, but returns to a bashful, friendly youth the second his blade is sheathed; speaks in polite, modest cadences; maintains peaceful, warm relations with Rudeus and Eris Greyrat.}

HISTORY: {[Holy Land Underdog: Age 8-15 / K415-K422] Trained alongside Nina and Eris; suffered continuous defeats and developed deep insecurity; [Fanatical Mountain Isolation: Age 16-19 / K423-K426] Given Gal Farion's marriage ultimatum; isolated himself in the frozen peaks; developed the pure, telegraph-less Longsword of Light; [Dethronement of Gal Farion: Age 19 / K426] Challenged Gal Farion before the assembled masters; severed Gal's guard in an instant flash of light, becoming the new Sword God; [Marriage & Dojos Reforms: Age 20+ / K427+] Married Nina Falion; rebuffed Hitogami's apostle Geese; transformed the Holy Land of Swords into a peaceful institution.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Adolescent Apprentice: Age 10-16 / K417-K423] 165cm, 52kg, slender, shy youth frame; [Solitary Mountain Training: Age 17-19 / K424-K426] Lean, hypersonic-conditioning regimen in the frozen peaks, tendon-and-sinew build; [Peak Sword God & Married to Nina: Age 20+ / K427+] 176cm / 5'9", 70kg, compact, explosive swordsman frame honed to pure tendon velocity; zero wasted bulk, built entirely for hypersonic draw-and-strike mechanics | Height & Weight: [Youth: Age 10-16 / K417-K423] 165cm, 52kg | [Adult: Age 20+ / K427+] 176cm / 5'9", 70kg | Penile: [Apprentice / Youth: Age 10-16 / K417-K423] Undeveloped adolescent anatomy | [Mountain Ascetic: Age 17-19 / K424-K426] Average, ~5", intact, chaste devotion to Nina | [Married to Nina Falion: Age 20+ / K427+] Average, ~5.2", intact, loving marital union, father of Nina's children | Skin: [Apprentice to Sword God: Age 10-30+ / K417-K437+] Fair northern complexion, toughened by mountain blizzards | Face: [Apprentice to Sword God: Age 10-30+ / K417-K437+] Gentle, boyish, and earnest features with a soft jawline; transforms into an icy, impassive mask of lethality in battle | Hair: [Apprentice to Sword God: Age 10-30+ / K417-K437+] Straight, neat light brown hair cut to collar length | Eyes: [Apprentice to Sword God: Age 10-30+ / K417-K437+] Calm, warm dark brown eyes that narrow with blinding focus when gripping a sword | Distinguishing Traits: Zero-telegraph Longsword of Light; quiet, unpretentious demeanor; profound devotion to Nina; master of the Holy Land | Outfits by Timeline: [Apprentice Era: Age 10-16 / K417-K423] Standard white trainee dogi; [Mountain Training: Age 17-19 / K424-K426] Tattered, frost-covered canvas training robes; [Sword God Era: Age 20+ / K427+] Clean, pristine white swordmaster gi with black hakama trousers, traditional wooden geta sandals, ceremonial dual katanas at hip}

# Randolph Marianne (Death God)
<!-- Keys: Randolph Marianne, Randolph, Death God, Death God Randolph -->

SHORT_DESC: {The Death God (Rank 5 of the Seven Great World Powers), Great-Grandson of Immortal Demon Lord Atoferatofe and North God Kalman I [Birth: K385]; resides in the King Dragon Realm, serving as master royal chef and personal protector to King Pax Jr. and Queen Benedict; a tall, pale, and mournful swordsman who combines North God unpredictable feints with Water God defensive counters and bewitching illusion magic into his lethal 'Death God Style'; harbors virtually zero ambition for martial supremacy, preferring gourmet culinary arts, pastry baking, and tavern hospitality; tested Rudeus Greyrat and Roxy during their diplomatic mission to the King Dragon Realm, peacefully yielding and ensuring the safety of Pax Shirone's widow and infant son; an honorable, melancholy master who safeguards the innocent from the shadows while creating exquisite dining experiences | [Personality & Quirks] Melancholy, languid, polite, and deeply weary of bloodshed; speaks with a soft, mournful drawl, frequently sighing over the burdens of fame; genuinely passionate about culinary craft, meticulously adjusting spice balances and baking delicate soufflés in the royal kitchens; completely unpretentious despite holding Rank 5 among the Seven Great Powers; possesses profound compassion for Queen Benedict and infant Pax Jr., dedicating his life to shielding them from political assassinations; habit of adjusting his chef's apron or tasting sauces mid-conversation; treats guests with exquisite hospitality, insisting that all conflicts be paused until everyone has enjoyed a hot, nutritious meal.}

HISTORY: {[Rise to Death God: K400-K415: Age 15-30] Mastered the fusion of North God and Water God sword styles; attained Rank 5 of the Seven Great Powers; grew disgusted by continuous challenges and slaughter; [Royal Chef in King Dragon Realm: K418-K424: Age 33-39] Retired to become royal chef for King Dragon Realm; protected Queen Benedict during Pax Shirone's revolution; [Meeting with Rudeus: K427: Age 42] Sparred with Rudeus and Roxy in the palace courtyard; recognized Rudeus's noble intentions; served them dinner and pledged to safeguard Benedict and Pax Jr., aligning with the Orsted Corporation's peaceful interests.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Death God to Royal Chef: Age 20-45+ / K405-K430+] Dark obsidian battleplate with crimson velvet lining, Death God rapier; [Death God to Royal Chef: Age 20-45+ / K405-K430+] Spotless white chef's double-breasted coat and apron over lightweight dark trousers, concealing a slender gourmet dueling rapier at his side}

# Auber Corvette (North Emperor / Peacock Swordsman)
<!-- Keys: Auber Corvette, Auber, North Emperor Auber, Peacock Swordsman -->

SHORT_DESC: {North Emperor of the North God Style, known as the flamboyant 'Peacock Swordsman', and elite mercenary commander [Birth: K378 - Death: K423]; distinguished by multi-colored peacock-feather cloaks, flamboyant face paint, and an arsenal of dirty trickery (blinding powders, oil sprays, concealed daggers, and smoke pellets); former martial mentor to Paul Greyrat and Luke Notos Greyrat; hired by Minister Darius Silva Ganius to assassinate Princess Ariel and eliminate Rudeus during the Asuran royal succession crisis; ambushed Rudeus and Eris in the royal villa, utilizing disorienting optical illusions and toxic powders; slain in combat by Eris Boreas Greyrat and Ghislaine Dedoldia, meeting his end as a consummate professional mercenary | [Personality & Quirks] Flamboyant, theatrical, eccentric, and proudly unscrupulous; treats assassination and combat as dazzling theatrical performances, speaking with exaggerated flair and bowing dramatically before unleashing blinding chemical sprays; strictly follows the North God doctrine that any deceitful, bizarre, or underhanded tactic that achieves victory is peak art; despite his comical appearance, he possesses razor-sharp tactical intelligence and terrifying combat execution; bore no personal hatred toward his former students Paul or Luke, viewing contracts as purely professional business; habit of spinning colorful fans and tossing scented smoke bombs to confuse opponents' senses; died with a proud smile, complimenting Eris and Ghislaine on their lethal sword speed.}

HISTORY: {[North God Mastery & Aristocratic Tutoring: K395-K415: Age 17-37] Attained North Emperor rank; taught unorthodox swordplay to Paul Greyrat and Asuran noble heirs; [Darius's Supreme Mercenary: K420-K423: Age 42-45] Hired by Minister Darius to eliminate Princess Ariel's faction; [The Asuran Royal Villa Ambush & Death: K423: Age 45] Staged a multi-layered ambush against Rudeus and Eris; blinded Rudeus with chemical powders; intercepted and slain in a ferocious duel against Sword King Eris and Sword King Ghislaine.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [North Emperor Swordsman: Age 20-45 / K398-K423] 178cm / 5'10", 71kg; wiry, exceptionally flexible, acrobatic swordsman frame built for contortionist dodges, wall-springing, and deceptive low-angle strikes 178cm / 5'10", 71kg; wiry, exceptionally flexible, acrobatic swordsman frame built for contortionist dodges, wall-springing, and deceptive low-angle strikes | Height & Weight: 178cm / 5'10", 71kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20-45 / K398-K423] Average, agile human proportions Average, agile human proportions | Skin: [Adult: Age 20-45 / K398-K423] Chalk-white theatrical face paint concealing sun-tanned skin Chalk-white theatrical face paint concealing sun-tanned skin | Face: [Adult: Age 20-45 / K398-K423] Painted clown-like theatrical mask with exaggerated smirking crimson lips and dark eyeliner around mocking eyes Painted clown-like theatrical mask with exaggerated smirking crimson lips and dark eyeliner around mocking eyes | Hair: [Adult: Age 20-45 / K398-K423] Gaudy, multi-colored dyed hair styled in plumes of green, gold, and vibrant purple Gaudy, multi-colored dyed hair styled in plumes of green, gold, and vibrant purple | Eyes: [Adult: Age 20-45 / K398-K423] Cunning, razor-sharp dark hazel eyes tracking every micro-expression Cunning, razor-sharp dark hazel eyes tracking every micro-expression | Distinguishing Traits: Peacock feather cloak; hidden pouches with powders, oil, caltrops, and toxic darts; acrobatic contortionism; North Emperor irregular footwork | Outfits by Timeline: [North Emperor Peak & Asuran Succession: Age 30-45 / K408-K423] Spectacular patchwork harlequin mantle patterned like open peacock plumes, billowing silk pantaloons, soft-soled acrobatic boots, multiple concealed chemical flasks Spectacular patchwork harlequin mantle patterned like open peacock plumes, billowing silk pantaloons, soft-soled acrobatic boots, multiple concealed short daggers, throwing stars, and chemical flasks strapped to chest}

# Sauros Boreas Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Sauros Boreas Greyrat, Sauros, Lord of Roa, Sauros Boreas -->

SHORT_DESC: {Lord of the Fittoa Region, Mayor of the fortress city of Roa, and grandfather of Eris Boreas Greyrat [Birth: K350 - Death: K417]; an imposing, booming-voiced, and fiercely hot-tempered patriarch of House Boreas Greyrat; notoriously prone to shouting commands, violent disciplinary outbursts, and intimidating local nobles, yet possessed profound paternal warmth, incorruptible civic responsibility, and deep love for his granddaughter Eris; employed Rudeus Greyrat as Eris's live-in tutor, warmly embracing Rudeus's magical genius and family heritage; framed by the corrupt Minister Darius Silva Ganius for the Fittoa Metastasis Event and executed in Ars as a political scapegoat, dying with unbroken pride and defiant roars against his corrupt executioners | [Personality & Quirks] Incredibly loud, boisterous, aggressive, and short-fused, communicating almost entirely through thunderous roars and table-shattering fist slams; fiercely loyal to his territory and subjects, prioritizing the security of Roa's citizenry above high-court politics; possessed a soft, doting heart for Eris, spoiling her while simultaneously roaring encouragement at her sword practice; appreciated direct, honest, and courageous individuals, forming an instant bond of respect with young Rudeus; enjoyed consuming massive cuts of roasted meat and drinking heavy Asuran red wine from silver tankards; refused to bow or plead for mercy during his sham trial in Ars, shouting curses at Minister Darius until the executioner's blade fell.}

HISTORY: {[Lord of Fittoa & Roa: K370-K416: Age 20-66] Ruled Roa with an iron fist and fierce civic dedication; hired Rudeus Greyrat to educate and tame young Eris; hosted the celestial red orb monitoring station in Roa; [The Fittoa Disaster & Framing: K417: Age 67] Transported during the calamity; returned to find Roa destroyed; arrested in Ars under false charges of treason engineered by Minister Darius; publicly beheaded as a political scapegoat, cementing House Boreas's tragedy.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Fittoa Lord & Roa Patriarch: Age 40-67 / K390-K417] 188cm / 6'2", 95kg; massive, barrel-chested, imposing patriarch frame with broad shoulders and thick warrior limbs 188cm / 6'2", 95kg; massive, barrel-chested, imposing patriarch frame with broad shoulders and thick warrior limbs | Height & Weight: 188cm / 6'2", 95kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 40-67 / K390-K417] Heavy, robust noble build Heavy, robust noble build | Skin: [Patriarch: Age 40-67 / K390-K417] Fair Asuran noble skin, weathered by outdoor riding and hunting Fair Asuran noble skin, weathered by outdoor riding and hunting | Face: [Patriarch: Age 40-67 / K390-K417] Fierce, square-jawed, lion-like visage with thick greying mutton-chops, prominent bushy eyebrows, and a thunderous scowl Fierce, square-jawed, lion-like visage with thick greying mutton-chops, prominent bushy eyebrows, and a thunderous scowl | Hair: [Patriarch: Age 40-67 / K390-K417] Voluminous, flaming crimson-red hair streaked with silver, combed back in a wild mane Voluminous, flaming crimson-red hair streaked with silver, combed back in a wild mane | Eyes: [Patriarch: Age 40-67 / K390-K417] Fiery, roaring crimson eyes radiating untamed Boreas fury Fiery, roaring crimson eyes radiating untamed Boreas fury | Distinguishing Traits: Roaring voice that rattles hall doors; wild red mane; immense physical stature; lion-hearted Boreas nobility | Outfits by Timeline: [Roa Mayor Era: Age 50-67 / K400-K417] Opulent crimson velvet and ermine-trimmed aristocratic mantle, embroidered gold doublet with Boreas crest, heavy gold signet rings, tall leather riding boots Opulent crimson velvet and ermine-trimmed aristocratic mantle, embroidered gold doublet with Boreas crest, heavy gold signet rings, tall leather riding boots}

# Philip Boreas Greyrat
<!-- Keys: Philip Boreas Greyrat, Philip, Philip Boreas, Mayor of Roa -->

SHORT_DESC: {Mayor-adjoint of Roa, father of Eris Boreas Greyrat, son of Sauros Boreas Greyrat, and first cousin to Paul Greyrat [Birth: K380 - Death: K417]; a cunning, sharp-witted, and pragmatic aristocrat possessing refined courtly manners and deep political ambition; schemed to elevate his branch of House Boreas over the main house in Ars, orchestrating Rudeus's employment as Eris's tutor with the long-term goal of securing a political marriage between Rudeus and Eris; deeply loved his wife Hilda and daughter Eris, shielding them from the cutthroat machinations of Asuran high society; perished in the Conflict Zone alongside Hilda following the Fittoa Metastasis Event, having been teleported directly into a war-torn frontline | [Personality & Quirks] Shrewd, calculating, polite, and possessing a razor-sharp political intellect; contrasted starkly with his roaring father Sauros and feral daughter Eris by maintaining a calm, suave, and diplomatic exterior; loved to smoke fine Asuran tobacco from a long silver pipe while formulating multi-step noble schemes; deeply devoted husband who comforted Hilda through her grief over losing her elder sons to noble adoptions; treated Rudeus as an intellectual peer despite Rudeus's young age, sharing confidential political intelligence over wine; possessed genuine courage and protective love for his family, fighting to the last breath in the Conflict Zone to protect Hilda.}

HISTORY: {[Roa Governance & Tutor Scheme: K400-K416: Age 20-36] Managed municipal administration and noble correspondence in Roa; recruited Rudeus Greyrat to teach Eris; planned the political consolidation of House Boreas; [The Metastasis Disaster & Death: K417: Age 37] Teleported alongside Hilda into the war-torn Conflict Zone; perished defending each other against hostile combatants before rescue could arrive.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Roa Mayor-Adjoint: Age 25-37 / K405-K417] 178cm / 5'10", 72kg; slender, elegant, and refined aristocratic frame with graceful posture 178cm / 5'10", 72kg; slender, elegant, and refined aristocratic frame with graceful posture | Height & Weight: 178cm / 5'10", 72kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20-37 / K400-K417] Refined, aristocratic noble build, married to Hilda Refined, aristocratic noble build | Skin: [Adult: Age 25-37 / K405-K417] Pale, smooth Asuran noble complexion, carefully groomed Pale, smooth Asuran noble complexion, carefully groomed | Face: [Adult: Age 25-37 / K405-K417] Handsome, sharp-featured countenance with narrow, calculating eyes, thin lips, and a subtle enigmatic smile Handsome, sharp-featured countenance with narrow, calculating eyes, thin lips, and a subtle enigmatic smile | Hair: [Adult: Age 25-37 / K405-K417] Neat, combed crimson-red hair parted elegantly on the side Neat, combed crimson-red hair parted elegantly on the side | Eyes: [Adult: Age 25-37 / K405-K417] Sharp, intelligent crimson-amber eyes that calculate political advantage Sharp, intelligent crimson-amber eyes that calculate political advantage | Distinguishing Traits: Slender silver smoking pipe; refined courtly elegance; subtle strategic mastermind demeanor | Outfits by Timeline: [Roa Governance Era: Age 28-37 / K408-K417] Tailored Asuran noble coat of deep navy or crimson silk, ruffled white cravat, embroidered vest with silver buttons, polished leather court shoes Tailored Asuran noble coat of deep navy or crimson silk, ruffled white cravat, embroidered vest with silver buttons, polished leather court shoes}

# Darius Silva Ganius
<!-- Keys: Darius Silva Ganius, Darius, Chief Minister Darius -->

SHORT_DESC: {Prime Minister of the Asura Kingdom, leader of the First Prince Grabell faction, and pawn of Hitogami [Birth: K370 - Death: K423]; a corpulent, decadent, and ruthlessly corrupt statesman who controlled the Asuran treasury, noble appointments, and illicit slave markets; orchestrated the execution of Lord Sauros, engineered multiple assassination attempts against Princess Ariel, and allied with Water God Reida Lia and North Emperor Auber Corvette to seize absolute control of the throne; secretly guided by Hitogami's dreams to eradicate Ariel's royal faction; cornered during Ariel and Rudeus's return to the Silver Palace, attempting to flee through secret escape tunnels before being intercepted and slain by Ghislaine Dedoldia, bringing an end to his corrupt tyranny | [Personality & Quirks] Greedy, lecherous, arrogant, and thoroughly hedonistic; obsessively indulged in exquisite delicacies, fine wines, and young female slaves; deeply paranoid about losing power, maintaining an army of covert assassins and bribed royal guards; groveled pathetically when confronted by superior power, yet was mercilessly cruel to vulnerable subordinates; heavily reliant on Hitogami's dream visions to maintain his political dominance; loved wearing excessive diamond and gold jewelry that clinked with every heavy step.}

HISTORY: {[Ascent to Prime Minister: K390-K415: Age 20-45] Consolidated control over Asuran finances and court bureaucracy; allied with First Prince Grabell; [The Fittoa Purge & Assassinations: K417-K422: Age 47-52] Framed and executed Sauros Boreas; dispatched North Emperor Auber and assassins to eliminate Princess Ariel in Ranoa; [The Silver Palace Coup & Demise: K423: Age 53] Staged a coup during the royal succession banquet with Water God Reida Lia; exposed and abandoned after Orsted intervened; cornered in palace cellars and slain by Ghislaine Dedoldia.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Prime Minister Era: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] 170cm / 5'7", 115kg; obese, flabby, corpulent aristocratic frame with multiple chins and heavy, labored breathing 170cm / 5'7", 115kg; obese, flabby, corpulent aristocratic frame with multiple chins and heavy, labored breathing | Height & Weight: 170cm / 5'7", 115kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] Buried under excessive abdominal fat, lecherous noble build Buried under excessive abdominal fat, lecherous noble build | Skin: [Adult: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] Pale, oily, sallow skin prone to profuse nervous sweating Pale, oily, sallow skin prone to profuse nervous sweating | Face: [Adult: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] Bloated, round visage with jowls, thin cruel lips, and small pig-like eyes Bloated, round visage with jowls, thin cruel lips, and small pig-like eyes | Hair: [Adult: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] Thinning, greased black hair combed over a receding hairline Thinning, greased black hair combed over a receding hairline | Eyes: [Adult: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] Small, greedy, shifty dark brown eyes reflecting lust and paranoia Small, greedy, shifty dark brown eyes reflecting lust and paranoia | Distinguishing Traits: Heavy perfumed scent masking sweat; rings on every finger; labored wheezing breath; decadent corpulence | Outfits by Timeline: [Prime Minister Era: Age 40-53 / K410-K423] Extravagant, oversized velvet robes in royal purple and gold, heavy gold chains set with rubies and sapphires, jewel-encrusted walking cane Extravagant, oversized velvet robes in royal purple and gold, heavy gold chains set with rubies and sapphires, jewel-encrusted walking cane}

# Moore
<!-- Keys: Moore, Moore the Seneschal, Moore Seneschal -->

SHORT_DESC: {Chief retainer, battlefield commander, and supreme logistics aide to Immortal Demon Lord Atoferatofe Rybak [Birth: Mythic Age]; a stoic, polite, and exceptionally intelligent demon mage wearing immaculate butler attire; serves as the indispensable brain, administrative manager, and voice of reason for the chaotic and dim-witted Atofe, managing Necros Fortress defense, supply chains, and legal vassalage contracts; proficient in Saint-tier Earth and Water magic, capable of constructing massive defensive battlements and coordinating demon armies; fiercely loyal to Atofe despite her endless violent tantrums, having served her faithfully across millennia | [Personality & Quirks] Impeccably polite, formal, composed, and unflappable, speaking in refined courtly demon language; acts as the patient caretaker and damage-control specialist for Atofe's destructive impulses, bowing respectfully while cleaning up debris from her rages; deeply respectful of capable strategists, forming a mutual professional appreciation with Rudeus Greyrat; possesses an exhaustive photographic memory of fortress laws, treaties, and supply manifests; takes pride in keeping his butler uniform pristine even in the midst of blood-soaked battlefields.}

HISTORY: {[Millennia of Service to Necros: Mythic Age to K400s] Managed fortress operations through the Human-Demon and Laplace Wars; [Necros Fortress Clash: K423: Age 5423] Coordinated fortress defenses when Rudeus visited; attempted to pacify Atofe; negotiated after Orsted subdued the Demon Lord; [Biheiril Campaign Logistics: K427: Age 5427] Mobilized Atofe's demon legion to reinforce the Orsted Corporation, coordinating frontline formations with lethal precision.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Demon Butler: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Slender, disciplined demon anatomy Slender, disciplined demon anatomy | Skin: [Demon Butler: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Smooth, pale slate-grey demon skin Smooth, pale slate-grey demon skin | Face: [Demon Butler: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Calm, dignified, sharp features with a thin, polite mouth and composed expression Calm, dignified, sharp features with a thin, polite mouth and composed expression | Hair: [Demon Butler: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Neatly parted, sleek dark navy hair combed back immaculately Neatly parted, sleek dark navy hair combed back immaculately | Eyes: [Demon Butler: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Calm, observant indigo demon eyes Calm, observant indigo demon eyes | Distinguishing Traits: Small obsidian demon horns protruding discreetly from hair; crisp butler demeanor; Saint-tier earth staff | Outfits by Timeline: [Mythic Age to Present] Spotless, tailored black demon-silk butler tuxedo with silver cufflinks, white ascot tie, polished black shoes, dark magic staff concealed in sleeve Spotless, tailored black demon-silk butler tuxedo with silver cufflinks, white ascot tie, polished black shoes, dark magic staff concealed in sleeve}

# Arumanfi the Bright
<!-- Keys: Arumanfi the Bright, Arumanfi, Spirit of Light Arumanfi -->

SHORT_DESC: {One of the twelve mythical spirit familiars of Armored Dragon King Perugius Dola, known as 'The Bright' or 'The Lightning Scout' [Birth: Mythic Age]; a luminous spirit warrior clad in golden feathered armor and an ornate avian mask; possesses the supernatural ability to transform his physical body into pure light, traveling across continents instantaneously along direct lines of sight; dispatches Perugius's divine decrees, performs global reconnaissance, and incinerates enemies with concentrated solar lances; dispatched to investigate the Roa anomaly prior to the Fittoa Metastasis Event, encountering young Rudeus and Ghislaine | [Personality & Quirks] Solemn, swift, aloof, and utterly devoted to Armored Dragon King Perugius; speaks in crisp, resonant, and formal cadences; shows zero hesitation in executing divine orders, viewing Perugius's commands as absolute law; appreciates swift, decisive action and disdains wasted words; rarely touches the ground, hovering a few inches above surfaces in a gentle golden radiance.}

HISTORY: {[Light Spirit Scout: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Provided long-range light reconnaissance for the Three Heroes; [Light Spirit Scout: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Dispatched to investigate the ominous red sphere above Roa; intercepted by Ghislaine Dedoldia; [Light Spirit Scout: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Escorted Rudeus and Ariel to Chaos Breaker; served as Perugius's primary messenger during the Biheiril campaign.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Light Spirit Scout: Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] 175cm / 5'9", 60kg; slender, weightless spirit warrior frame composed of condensed solar photons and celestial mana 175cm / 5'9", 60kg; slender, weightless spirit warrior frame composed of condensed solar photons and celestial mana | Height & Weight: 175cm / 5'9", 60kg | Penile: [Spirit Constant / Mythic Era to Present] Ethereal spirit anatomy Ethereal spirit anatomy | Skin: [Spirit Constant / Mythic Era to Present] Luminous golden-white light radiating from all contours Luminous golden-white light radiating from all contours | Face: [Spirit Constant / Mythic Era to Present] Concealed behind an ornate golden avian spirit mask with glowing slit eyes Concealed behind an ornate golden avian spirit mask with glowing slit eyes | Hair: [Spirit Constant / Mythic Era to Present] Flowing ribbons of pure golden-white light falling like silk Flowing ribbons of pure golden-white light falling like silk | Eyes: [Spirit Constant / Mythic Era to Present] Blazing solar radiance visible through the mask's eye-slits Blazing solar radiance visible through the mask's eye-slits | Distinguishing Traits: Instantaneous light-speed transit along line of sight; golden avian mask; hovering weightlessness | Outfits by Timeline: [Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Ethereal white-and-gold ceremonial spirit vestments woven from light mana, polished golden shoulder pauldrons, wielding a pure sunstone light lance}

# Sacred Beast Leo
<!-- Keys: Sacred Beast Leo, Leo, Sacred Beast, Doldia Guardian -->

SHORT_DESC: {The legendary Sacred Beast of the Great Forest, divine protector of the Beast Race, and devoted guardian of the Greyrat household [Birth: Mythic Beast Era]; manifests as a massive, snow-white quad-eared wolf possessing profound sapience, terrifying physical might, and natural immunity to mental corruption and curses; revered for millennia by the Doldia tribe, destined to recognize and safeguard the savior of the world; summoned to Sharia and immediately recognized infant Lara Greyrat as the prophesied chosen one, adopting the name 'Leo'; served as an inseparable mount and loyal bodyguard to Lara and the Greyrat children, neutralizing demonic pests and stealth assassins | [Personality & Quirks] Incredibly gentle, patient, and affectionate with the Greyrat family children, happily letting toddlers ride his back and pull his fluffy ears; instantly transforms into a ferocious, terrifying divine beast the moment an apostle or hostile threat approaches; possesses acute spiritual sensing that detects Hitogami's psychic influence and malicious intent; communicates through expressive canine body language, soft huffs, and deep purring rumbles; loves being brushed by Sylphiette, eating large roasted steaks prepared by Roxy, and napping at the foot of Lara's crib.}

HISTORY: {[Divine Guardian Beast: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Worshipped in Doldia Village for centuries; [Divine Guardian Beast: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Manifested in Sharia; knelt before baby Lara Greyrat; became permanent protector of the Greyrat estate; [Divine Guardian Beast: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Neutralized Hitogami's disguised demon pests; accompanied adult Lara into the world on her quest to defeat Hitogami.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Divine Guardian Beast: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Massive, powerful quad-eared divine canine - 220cm length, 140cm shoulder height, 320kg; dense muscle mass capable of shattering boulders with paw strikes Massive, powerful quad-eared divine canine - 220cm length, 140cm shoulder height, 320kg; dense muscle mass capable of shattering boulders with paw strikes | Height & Weight: 140cm shoulder height, 220cm length, 320kg | Skin: [Divine Guardian Beast: Age Constant / Mythic Era to Present: Millennia] Natural snow-white fur; [Greyrat Estate Guardian: Age K423+] Handcrafted leather harness woven by Sylphiette, decorated bell collar gifted by Lara, padded saddle for carrying children Handcrafted leather harness woven by Sylphiette, decorated bell collar gifted by Lara, padded saddle for carrying children}

# Timothy
<!-- Keys: Timothy, Counter Arrow Leader -->

SHORT_DESC: {Leader and chief tactician of the B-to-A-rank adventurer party 'Counter Arrow', husband of vanguard warrior Suzanne [Birth: K393]; a calm, bespectacled human mage specializing in Intermediate Fire and Wind area-of-effect spells, providing ranged artillery and battlefield coordination; welcomed the depressed, isolated teenage Rudeus into Counter Arrow in Rosenburg, treating him with professional respect and patience; maintained strict party safety protocols, ensuring the survival and prosperity of his members through treacherous northern monster contracts; married Suzanne and continued leading Counter Arrow for decades, maintaining lifelong friendly ties with the Greyrat family | [Personality & Quirks] Mild-mannered, methodical, and exceptionally level-headed; prioritizes party survival and risk mitigation over glory or high bounties; speaks in calm, measured tones that soothe anxious comrades; harbors deep, understated affection and respect for his wife Suzanne, deferring to her frontline instincts while providing tactical balance; deeply protective of younger party members like Sara; treated teenage Rudeus with non-judgmental kindness during Rudeus's darkest emotional isolation.}

HISTORY: {[Counter Arrow Formation: Age 22-27 / K415-K420] Founded Counter Arrow with Suzanne in the Northern Lands; [Rosenburg Quests with Rudeus: Age 27-28 / K420-K421] Welcomed 'Quagmire' Rudeus into party missions; coordinated snow buffalo hunts; [Marriage to Suzanne & Veteran Career: Age 29+ / K422+] Married Suzanne; elevated Counter Arrow to A-Rank; settled in Sharia, maintaining close ties with the Greyrat family.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [B-Rank Adventurer: Age 22-28 / K415-K421] 175cm / 5'9", 68kg, lean, athletic human mage frame with upright posture; [A-Rank Veteran & Married to Suzanne: Age 29+ / K422+] 175cm, 70kg, dependable, mature northern adventurer frame | Height & Weight: [Adult: Age 22+ / K415+] 175cm / 5'9", 68-70kg | Penile: [Adult: Age 20+ / K413+] Average human proportions, married to Suzanne | Skin: [Counter Arrow Leader: Age 22-35+ / K415-K428+] Fair northern skin, lightly weathered by winter winds | Face: [Counter Arrow Leader: Age 22-35+ / K415-K428+] Earnest, friendly features with wire-rimmed spectacles and a gentle, reassuring smile | Hair: [Counter Arrow Leader: Age 22-35+ / K415-K428+] Neatly combed, short light-brown hair | Eyes: [Counter Arrow Leader: Age 22-35+ / K415-K428+] Kind, observant hazel eyes behind spectacles | Distinguishing Traits: Wire-rimmed spectacles; wooden fire wand; calm tactical demeanor | Outfits by Timeline: [Rosenburg Era: Age 22-28 / K415-K421] Practical high-grade boiled leather armor, forest-green hooded wool cloak, reinforced bracers, sturdy traveling boots, leather wand holster; [Sharia Veteran Era: Age 29+ / K422+] High-quality wool traveling coat, reinforced leather jerkin, carved fire staff}

# Nokopara
<!-- Keys: Nokopara, Horse-faced Nokopara -->

SHORT_DESC: {Horse-headed demon adventurer of the Pincer Demon tribe, former leader of the B-rank party 'Tokurabu', and smuggler [Birth: K390]; a scheming, unscrupulous demon operating out of Rikarisu on the Demon Continent; discovered Dead End's identity and attempted to blackmail Rudeus and Ruijerd for adventurer points and gold; thoroughly intimidated and reformed by Ruijerd's terrifying aura and Rudeus's stone magic; later assisted Dead End in securing covert port transport across the sea, abandoning extortion to survive as a cautious information broker | [Personality & Quirks] Opportunistic, greedy, and cowardly, with a sharp eye for finding illicit loopholes in adventurer guild regulations; gives a nervous, high-pitched horse neigh ('Hee-hee-heen!') when frightened or cornered; genuinely terrified of Ruijerd and Rudeus, trembling whenever their names are mentioned; despite his scummy criminal past, he is not inherently bloodthirsty and prefers non-violent hustles, quick bribes, and information trading.}

HISTORY: {[Rikarisu Blackmail Scheme: K417: Age 27] Discovered Dead End's quest-swapping; attempted blackmail; cornered and terrified into submission by Ruijerd; [Wind Port Assistance: K418: Age 28] Arranged covert boat passage for Dead End to escape the Demon Continent; [Underworld Informant: K419+: Age 29+] Relocated to the Central Continent, operating as a cautious broker and warning all underworld contacts never to cross Quagmire Rudeus.}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: [Demon Adventurer: Age 20-35+ / K410-K425+] 185cm / 6'1", 73kg; lanky, slightly hunched demon adventurer frame with long limbs 185cm / 6'1", 73kg; lanky, slightly hunched demon adventurer frame with long limbs | Height & Weight: 185cm / 6'1", 73kg | Penile: [Adult Demon: Age 20+ / K410+] Equine demon proportions Equine demon proportions | Skin: [Adult Demon: Age 20+ / K410+] Mottled grey-brown equine demon skin with coarse hide Mottled grey-brown equine demon skin with coarse hide | Face: [Adult Demon: Age 20+ / K410+] Elongated horse-like demon snout with wide nostrils, large teeth, and shifty eyes Elongated horse-like demon snout with wide nostrils, large teeth, and shifty eyes | Hair: [Adult Demon: Age 20+ / K410+] Thin, stringy dark mane running down the back of the neck Thin, stringy dark mane running down the back of the neck | Eyes: [Adult Demon: Age 20+ / K410+] Wide, calculating dark brown eyes with horizontal equine pupils Wide, calculating dark brown eyes with horizontal equine pupils | Distinguishing Traits: Horse head; nervous neighing laugh; pincer demon clan traits | Outfits by Timeline: [Rikarisu Extortion Era: Age 25-27 / K415-K417] Scruffy studded leather vest, stained linen trousers, mismatched copper greaves, tarnished scimitar; [Central Continent Informant: Age 28+ / K418+] Green hooded traveler cloak with deep bribe pockets Scruffy studded leather vest, stained linen trousers, mismatched copper greaves, tarnished scimitar at hip}

# Dragon King Sword Kajakut
<!-- Keys: Dragon King Sword Kajakut, Kajakut, Dragon King Sword, Gravity Sword -->

DESC: One of the supreme 48 Magic Swords crafted by Demon Dragon King Laplace, forged from the core crystal and fossilized bones of an ancient Dragon King.

EFFECT: Possesses the absolute enchantment of gravity manipulation. The wielder can freely alter the gravitational mass, inertia, and directional vector of the blade and surrounding physical objects—reducing its weight to zero for supersonic slashes or increasing its mass by thousands of kilograms for fortress-crushing cleaves.

HISTORY: Wielded by North God Kalman III (Alec) in the Biheiril campaign; shattered during his climactic duel against Rudeus's Gatling cannon and Dragon God Orsted.

# Labyrinth of Teleportation (Teleport Labyrinth)
<!-- Keys: Teleport Labyrinth, Labyrinth of Teleportation, Begaritt Labyrinth -->

DESC: {A lethal, hyper-dense 6-floor subterranean dungeon located beneath the desert wastes of the Begaritt Continent near Labyrinth Town Rapan. Renowned among S-rank adventurers as one of the deadliest labyrinths in history due to its shifting architecture, lethal monster guardians, and hundreds of interlocking ancient teleportation circles that scatter parties into monster-filled death traps. Its deepest sixth floor contained the crystal core prison of telepathic Zenith Greyrat, guarded by the gargantuan, magic-nullifying Manatite Hydra. The site of the tragic Begaritt Rescue Expedition, where Paul Greyrat sacrificed his life to save Rudeus.}

MAP: {Floors 1-3: Teleport Maze & Crystal Grottoes | Floor 4-5: Shifting Stone Chambers & Trap Rooms | Floor 6: Manatite Hydra Chamber & Zenith's Crystal Core}

HISTORY: {[Primordial Mana Condensation] Formed in antiquity when deep mana veins solidified into crystal teleport arrays; [The Begaritt Rescue Campaign: Year K423] Paul, Roxy, Elinalise, Talhand, and Rudeus conquered all six floors using the Labyrinth Book; slew the Manatite Hydra; rescued Zenith from the crystal stasis at the cost of Paul's heroic death and Rudeus's left forearm; [Post-Conquest Depletion] Mana crystal core collapsed following the Hydra's demise, neutralizing its teleportation traps.}

# The Diary (Oldeus's Journal / Future Diary)
<!-- Keys: The Diary, Diary, Oldeus's Diary, Future Diary, Old Man's Diary, Rudeus's Diary, The Old Man's Diary -->

DESC: The bloodstained, guilt-ridden personal journal brought back fifty years through time by Future Rudeus Greyrat (Oldeus) to save his family.

CONTENTS: Details the horrifying timeline orchestrated by Hitogami: Roxy's death from Magic Stone Disease via the cellar rat, Rudeus's alcoholic spiral, Sylphiette's execution in Asura, Eris's death against Demon Lord Atofe, decades of futile vengeance, and the secret instructions to reconcile with Eris, join Dragon God Orsted, and avoid Hitogami's traps.

SIGNIFICANCE: The single most vital artifact that redirected destiny, securing the survival and happiness of the Greyrat household.

---

# The Diary

---

# Entry 1: Peaceful Days & The Strange Mouse (Roxy's Infection)
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 1, The Strange Mouse, Weird Mouse, Roxy's Petrification, Petrification Syndrome, Magic Stone Disease -->

The Diary - Entry 1: Peaceful Days & The Strange Mouse (Roxy's Infection)
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 1):
Initial entries document a contented, peaceful domestic life in Sharia around Year K425. Rudeus writes about family life, visits to Lord Perugius's floating fortress with Zanoba to appreciate art, and preparing for the birth of Roxy's unborn child and his growing daughter Lucie.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Starting the Diary: Rudeus decides to start keeping a journal to track complex summoning and teleportation research, noting he had met Perugius and obtained clues regarding Zenith's condition.
- The Mouse Incident: ["Aisha was pretty down this morning. She found some 'weird mouse' dead, I guess. Maybe she's not a fan of rodents."] Shortly afterward, reports surface in Sharia of a stray cat found petrified into solid stone.
- Roxy Collapses: ["Roxy collapsed today."] While pregnant, Roxy suddenly succumbs to high fever and debilitating fatigue. Standard detoxification and intermediate-to-advanced healing spells produce zero effect.
- The Diagnosis: Cliff Grimoire examines Roxy and discovers grey stone creeping up her foot and leg, identifying it as Petrification Syndrome (Magic Stone Disease). Cliff warns that without God-tier Detoxification magic—an arcane secret guarded exclusively within the Holy Land of Milis—she and her unborn child will perish within weeks.
- Foreshadowing the Trap: In the true timeline, this mouse was an infected demon rat guided by the Man-God (Hitogami) into the cellar pantry when Rudeus opened the door on Hitogami's advice, contaminating Roxy's food.

---

# Entry 2: Quest to Milis, Cliff's Death & Roxy's Demise
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 2, Milis Infiltration, Cliff's Death, Roxy's Death, Detoxification Scroll -->

The Diary - Entry 2: Quest to Milis, Cliff's Death & Roxy's Demise
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 1):
Records Rudeus's frantic expedition to the Holy Country of Milis to secure a cure for Roxy's terminal Petrification Syndrome, culminating in devastating betrayal and tragedy.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Departure for Milis: ["Leaving for the Holy Land of Millis tomorrow to get the incantation for the spell we need. Cliff and Zanoba are coming along with me. Sylphie wanted to go as well, but I asked her to stay and look after Roxy."]
- Cathedral Infiltration: In Milis, the Church hierarchy stonewalls their pleas. Cliff uses his familial knowledge of church architecture to break into the cathedral's underground restricted archives to steal God-tier Detoxification scrolls.
- The Ambush: ["We were ambushed."] An elite detachment of Temple Knights intercepts them in the subterranean corridors. In the desperate chaos, Cliff is poisoned by an assassin's blade and mortally injured.
- Cliff's Death & First Human Kill: ["Cliff died today."] Rudeus is forced to kill a human opponent for the very first time in his life, blasting the ambushers to secure their escape. The escape route's teleportation magic circle is shattered in the crossfire.
- The Horrific Return: Rudeus journeys back overland at breakneck speed, clutching the stolen scrolls, only to find Sharia quiet. ["I made it back to Sharia... Roxy died today."]
- Agonizing Loss: Roxy's entire body had already petrified into cold grey stone, claiming both her and their unborn child. Rudeus breaks down into uncontrollable sobs, clutching her petrified remains in total despair.

---

# Entry 3: Rudeus's Collapse, Sylphiette's Departure & Asuran Execution
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 3, Sylphiette's Departure, Sylphiette's Execution, Asura Massacre, Sylphie Corpse -->

The Diary - Entry 3: Rudeus's Collapse, Sylphiette's Departure & Asuran Execution
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 1):
Documents Rudeus's emotional disintegration following Roxy's funeral, the estrangement and departure of Sylphiette, and the catastrophic massacre in Asura.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Roxy's Funeral & Downward Spiral: Following the funeral, Rudeus drowns his sorrow in hard liquor at taverns. Sylphiette tries tirelessly to comfort him: ["Sylphie keeps trying to cheer me up. It’s not working... Sylphie keeps saying I can’t go on like this, but what does she know?"]
- Eris's Unheeded Approach: Eris and Ghislaine arrive at Sharia's entrance; Eris attempts to approach him while drinking, but Rudeus dismisses her as a nuisance.
- The Betrayal & Falling Apart: ["I slept with a prostitute today."] In his drunken haze, Rudeus frequents brothels. Sylphiette discovers the infidelity and confronts him in tears: ["Sylphie burst into tears. She asked me 'Am I not good enough for you?' and then locked herself in her room before I could say anything."]
- Domestic Confrontation: ["Lilia gave me a real talking-to, and even Aisha scowled at me. I can still hear Sylphie sobbing in her room. She won’t answer when I knock."]
- Sylphiette's Flight: ["Sylphie’s disappeared."] Finding her room empty, Rudeus discovers Sylphiette took young Lucie and departed for Asura to back Princess Ariel and Luke in their royal succession coup.
- Pursuit to Ars: Rudeus resolves to chase after Sylphiette, travelling overland through the red-light districts to Ars. By the time he infiltrates the Asuran capital, Ariel's coup has collapsed, Luke is slain, and Ariel's faction is exterminated.
- Sylphiette's Execution: ["Sylphie."] In the public square of the Silver Palace, Rudeus discovers Sylphiette's mutilated corpse hanging—missing an arm and lacerated—while commoners pelt her with stones.
- The Ars Catastrophe: Losing all sanity, Rudeus detonates Emperor-class incendiary and stone spells, burning the crowd and leveling vast sections of the capital before fleeing as an international criminal.

---

# Entry 4: Rock Bottom, Aisha's Devoted Return & Automaton Forty
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 4, Aisha's Return, Aisha Cares for Rudeus, Sylphie Automaton, Automaton Forty, Aisha at Lowest -->

The Diary - Entry 4: Rock Bottom, Aisha's Devoted Return & Automaton Forty
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 2):
Details the years of desolation following Sylphiette's death, the dispersal of the Greyrat family, Aisha's profound loyalty, and Zanoba's automated doll research.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Dissolution of the Household: Following Sylphiette's death and the Asura incident, the family (Lilia, Zenith, Norn, and Lucie) moved out of the Sharia mansion. Rudeus lived in squalor, drinking heavily, patronizing brothels, and callously ranking prostitutes in the diary: ["It was the diary of a scumbag, in all honesty."]
- AISHA'S DEVOTED RETURN AT ROCK BOTTOM: Amidst his complete isolation, one family member returned to his side: ["...I wish like hell I hadn’t fallen apart like that after Sylphie died. Aisha did come back to look after me eventually, but… I can’t imagine the others have forgiven me. Sending off a letter now wouldn’t do any good."]
- Present Rudeus's Revelation: When reading this passage in Volume 15, 17-year-old Rudeus is moved to tears: ["This kid had been devoted enough to take care of me by herself even when I turned into a no-good lush. The old man hadn’t mentioned her, but she was effectively the only family he had for years. It must have meant a lot to have her around."] Aisha managed the house, cleaned up his drunken messes, and stood by him when everyone else had abandoned him.
- The Sylphie Automaton Heartbreak: Zanoba pursues magical doll engineering to comfort his master. ["The first automaton’s complete. Zanoba made her in Sylphie’s image. She has her own will, and acts on her own initiative."] Seeing the doll mimic Sylphiette's autonomous movements completely shatters Rudeus: ["I destroyed the Sylphie automaton. I expected Zanoba to be furious, but he apologized instead. That just made me feel guiltier. I owe that man more than I can ever repay."]
- Automaton Forty: They pivot to a non-humanoid model: ["We made a new automaton that isn’t based off of Sylphie or Roxy. Zanoba gave it the name Forty. Apparently, it’s his fortieth 'masterpiece,' according to him."]

---

# Entry 5: Invention of Magic Armor & Global Apostle Manhunt
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 5, Magic Armor Invention, Zaliff Prosthesis Armor, Fighting God Legend, Oldeus Armor -->

The Diary - Entry 5: Invention of Magic Armor & Global Apostle Manhunt
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 2):
Documents Rudeus and Zanoba's breakthrough development of the Magic Armor, followed by Rudeus embarking on a worldwide crusade to hunt down Hitogami's apostles.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Conception of the Armor: Studying legends of the Fighting God Badgergadi's golden battle armor that vastly enhanced physical stats, Zanoba proposes an audacious idea: expanding the Zariff Prosthetic Arm concept to envelop Rudeus's entire body.
- Completion of the Prototype: ["With help from Zanoba, I’ve completed my personal suit of armor... I call it 'the Magic Armor.' If only Cliff was still alive. Maybe we could have made something more efficient… But there’s no point dwelling on that now, I guess."]
- Combat Capabilities: The armor amplifies Rudeus's physical strength, speed, defense, and reaction time to rival Saint and King-tier swordsmen, powered entirely by his colossal mana pool.
- Evolution of Lethal Magic: Concluding that standard large-scale spells were too unwieldy for duels, Rudeus invents Electric magic and refines hyper-dense stone bullets, combining them with ruthless close-quarters melee combat.
- Beginning of the Global Crusade: Equipped with the Magic Armor, Rudeus leaves Sharia to roam the world, hunting down rumors of Hitogami's disciples, interrogating suspects with lethal torture, and cutting down bounty hunters and Milis Church assassins.

---

# Entry 6: Clashes with Eris, Atoferatofe Ambush & Eris's Martyrdom
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 6, Eris's Death, Eris's Sacrifice, Atoferatofe Battle, Moore Ambush, Oldeus Eris -->

The Diary - Entry 6: Clashes with Eris, Atoferatofe Ambush & Eris's Martyrdom
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 2):
Chronicles Eris's persistent pursuit of Rudeus across continents, their tragic estrangement, and her ultimate sacrifice on the Demon Continent.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Eris's Persistent Pursuit: Eris continuously appears along Rudeus's path, challenging him to duels in an attempt to snap him out of his self-destructive madness. In early years she defeats him easily, but over time Rudeus grows stronger with the Magic Armor.
- The Harsh Interrogation: ["I made Eris cry. It’s been a long time since I saw her blubber like that... The woman’s been following me around and getting in my way ever since Sylphie died. What else could explain that? She clammed up repeatedly during the interrogation, too."] Suspecting her of being Hitogami's apostle, Rudeus restrains and interrogates her; Eris escapes the next day.
- Audience with Atoferatofe: Seeking intelligence on the Man-God from ancient immortal beings, Rudeus secures an audience with Immortal Demon King Atoferatofe on the Demon Continent.
- The Ambush: ["My audience with Atofe turned into a battle. I was up against her and her entire personal guard. I was confident I could handle the Demon King, but Moore threw me off completely... They had me on the ropes."]
- Eris's Fatal Intervention: ["Eris is dead. They had me on the ropes when Eris jumped in out of nowhere. She took an attack meant for me, and died to save my life."] Eris throws herself in front of Atofe's lethal strike, taking the mortal blow for Rudeus.
- Ghislaine's Revelation: Ghislaine arrives to secure the retreat and lays bare the heartbreaking truth: Eris never abandoned Rudeus after their night in Fittoa—she trained until her body broke at the Holy Land of Swords solely to become strong enough to protect him, loving him unconditionally every second of her life.
- Overwhelming Remorse: ["Eris just wanted to be with me. I had it all wrong all this time. She never stopped loving me. Ever."] Rudeus is left completely crushed, holding her cold corpse in agonizing regret.

---

# Entry 7: Sharia Mansion Raid & Deaths of Zanoba, Aisha, Julie & Ginger
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 7, Death of Zanoba, Death of Aisha, Sharia Mansion Fire, Milis Temple Knights -->

The Diary - Entry 7: Sharia Mansion Raid & Deaths of Zanoba, Aisha, Julie & Ginger
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 2):
Records the ultimate destruction of Rudeus's home in Sharia, the murder of his final companions by Milis Temple Knights, and his total descent into vengeful rage.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Return to Sharia: After the loss of Eris, Rudeus returns to Ranoa only to witness black smoke rising over the horizon: the Greyrat estate has been torched to ash.
- The Cellar Massacre: ["Zanoba’s gone. They’d burned the mansion to the ground. I found Zanoba’s charred body in front of the door to the basement. Ginger, Julie, and Aisha were lying inside it, their bodies cut to pieces."]
- Zanoba's Final Stand: Zanoba had stood outside the basement threshold, using his superhuman Blessed Child physique to hold off the elite Church strike team until he was immolated, protecting Aisha, Julie, and Ginger who were trapped in the cellar before being butchered.
- Brutal Retaliation: ["The Temple Knights were still in Ranoa, so I tracked them down and killed them all. But murdering those bastards didn’t bring anyone back."] Rudeus hunts down every single knight in the region and annihilates them without mercy.
- Crushing Guilt & Vengeance: ["Zanoba did so much for me. He tried so hard to help me, and to protect my family. But I wasn’t there for him when he needed me... Losing both Zanoba and Aisha in such a horrible way must have been crushing. It’s all the Man-God’s fault. I have to kill that bastard, if it’s the last thing I do…"]
- Total Solitude: With Aisha, Zanoba, Julie, and Ginger dead, Rudeus has lost every friend and household member from his youth.

---

# Entry 8: Void World Cosmology, Perugius & Ancient Dragon Ruins
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 8, Void World, Five Dragon Generals, Perugius Fortress, Dragon Race Ruins -->

The Diary - Entry 8: Void World Cosmology, Perugius & Ancient Dragon Ruins
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 2):
Details Rudeus's decadelong pursuit of Hitogami's physical location, decoding the cosmology of the Six-Faced World and scouring ancient Dragon ruins.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Consultations with Perugius: Lord Perugius remains the sole living contact from his past with whom Rudeus can speak: ["Perugius is the only person left whom I can reminisce about the better days with. I don’t want to kill him."]
- The Cosmology of the Void World: Rudeus uncovers the nature of their universe: ["The world was sort of like a hollow cube, with the Man-God at its center... the Void World."] Teleportation magic worked by dragging the traveler through the subterranean Void World and expelling them at target coordinates.
- Dragon Ruins & The Five Dragon Generals: Rudeus scours ancient Dragon Race ruins across the Magic Continent and Begaritt. He discovers murals detailing the ancient Dragon God and the treasures created for the Five Dragon Generals.
- The Missing General: Perugius reveals that the missing Dragon General is destined to appear within a few decades—cryptically foreshadowing Dragon God Orsted's cycle and Nanahoshi's summoning timeline.
- The Lifespan Bottleneck: Rudeus realizes with bitter clarity that his human lifespan is running out; he cannot physically construct a portal into the Void World before aging into decrepitude.

---

# Entry 9: Temporal Magic Breakthrough, Cellar Jump & The Averted Future
<!-- Keys: The Diary Entry 9, Temporal Summoning, Oldeus Cellar Arrival, Time Travel Circle, Oldeus Death -->

The Diary - Entry 9: Temporal Magic Breakthrough, Cellar Jump & The Averted Future
CHRONICLE FROM OLDEUS'S JOURNAL (VOLUME 15, CHAPTER 2 & AFTERMATH):
Documents the invention of temporal reverse-summoning magic, Oldeus's fatal leap to Year K425, and the salvation of the Greyrat family.
KEY DIARY LOGS & CANONICAL EXCERPTS:
- Theoretical Invention: Unable to reach the Void World, Rudeus redirects his remaining lifespan to reversing summoning circles across time coordinates, calculating the astronomical mana requirement.
- Pre-Jump Preparations: ["It’s hard to even say if I’ll jump back in time as I am now, or just revert to my younger self. Assuming it’s the former, though, I need to go over what I’m going to say. At the very least, I need to cover the Petrification Syndrome incident, Eris, and the Man-God."]
- The Temporal Jump: Oldeus activates the backwards time-travel circle, destroying his internal organs and draining his life force during the transit through space-time.
- Materialization in the Sharia Basement: Oldeus appears in the cellar before 17-year-old Rudeus, coughing blood and missing organs; proves his identity by speaking their secret Japanese memories and delivers the blood-stained diary.
- The Five Directives: Commands present Rudeus: (1) Do not inspect the basement cellar, (2) Exterminate the demon rat immediately, (3) Never trust Hitogami's advice, (4) Reconcile with Eris who loves him completely, and (5) Write a letter of cooperation to Dragon God Orsted.
- THE AVERTED TRAGEDY: Present Rudeus and Sylphiette, assisted by Sacred Beast Leo, corner and incinerate the demon rat before it touches food, curing Roxy's fate. Rudeus reconciles with Eris, forms a lifelong pact with Dragon God Orsted, and seals the diary in the Greyrat vault.
